0033  ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
0034  RESULTS = ROOT / "results"
0035  DOMESTIC_FEATURES = [
0036      "base_USO",
0037      "base_GLD",
0038      "base_KODEX200",
0039      "p_inflation_high",
0040      "proxy_mom1",
0041      "proxy_mom6",
0042      "proxy_vol6",
0043      "daily_mom21",
0044      "daily_mom252",
0045      "daily_vol21",
0046      "daily_downvol21",
0047      "daily_mean_corr63",
0048  ]
0049  STRESS_FEATURES = DOMESTIC_FEATURES + [
0050      "VIX_last",
0051      "VIX_last_d1",
0052      "VIX_last_z60",
0053      "BAA_SPREAD_last_d1",
0054      "NFCI_last_d1",
0055      "STLFSI_last_d1",
0056  ]
0057  TRIGGER_PERCENTILE = 0.80
0058  
0059  
0060  def make_model() -> Pipeline:
0061      return Pipeline(
0062          [
0063              ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median")),
0064              ("scale", StandardScaler()),
0065              (
0066                  "model",
0067                  LogisticRegression(
0068                      C=0.1,
0069                      class_weight="balanced",
0070                      max_iter=2000,
0071                      solver="liblinear",
0072                  ),
0073              ),
0074          ]
0075      )
0076  
0077  
0078  def walk_forward_probability(
0079      data: pd.DataFrame,
0080      target: str,
0081      features: list[str],
0082      min_train: int = 36,
0083  ) -> pd.Series:
0084      probability = pd.Series(np.nan, index=data.index, dtype=float)
0085      template = make_model()
0086      for number in range(len(data)):
0087          # Exclude the immediately prior row as a conservative one-month label embargo.
0088          train_end = number - 1
0089          if train_end < min_train:
0090              continue
0091          train = data.iloc[:train_end]
0092          y = train[target].astype(int)
0093          if y.sum() < 4 or (len(y) - y.sum()) < 12:
0094              continue
0095          model = clone(template)
0096          model.fit(train[features], y)
0097          probability.iloc[number] = float(model.predict_proba(data.iloc[[number]][features])[:, 1][0])
0098      return probability
0099  
0100  
0101  def make_factor(probability: pd.Series, target: pd.Series, name: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
0102      factor = pd.DataFrame({"p_tail_raw": probability, "tail_event": target.astype(float)})
0103      factor["risk_percentile"] = causal_percentile(factor["p_tail_raw"])
0104      severity = ((factor["risk_percentile"] - TRIGGER_PERCENTILE) / (1 - TRIGGER_PERCENTILE)).clip(0, 1)
0105      factor["risk_severity"] = severity
0106      factor["p_up"] = 0.5 - 0.15 * severity
0107      factor["score"] = -severity
0108      factor["factor_name"] = name
0109      return factor
0110  
0111  
0112  def predictive_metrics(factor: pd.DataFrame, start: pd.Period | None = None) -> dict[str, float]:
0113      view = factor.loc[start:] if start is not None else factor
0114      view = view.dropna(subset=["p_tail_raw", "tail_event"])
0115      y = view["tail_event"].astype(int)
0116      p = view["p_tail_raw"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0117      cutoff = float(p.quantile(0.80))
0118      selected = p >= cutoff
0119      return {
0120          "observations": int(len(view)),
0121          "events": int(y.sum()),
0122          "event_rate": float(y.mean()),
0123          "roc_auc": float(roc_auc_score(y, p)) if y.nunique() > 1 else np.nan,
0124          "average_precision": float(average_precision_score(y, p)) if y.nunique() > 1 else np.nan,
0125          "brier_score": float(brier_score_loss(y, p)),
0126          "recall_at_top_20pct": float(y[selected].sum() / max(y.sum(), 1)),
0127          "precision_at_top_20pct": float(y[selected].mean()),
0128      }
0129  
0130  
0131  def metric_record(period: str, strategy: str, backtest: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, float | str]:
0132      metrics = performance_summary(backtest["return"])
0133      return {
0134          "Period": period,
0135          "Strategy": strategy,
0136          **metrics.to_dict(),
0137          "AvgTurnover": float(backtest["turnover"].mean()),
0138      }
0139

0141  def main() -> None:
0142      feature_data = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0143      feature_data.index = pd.PeriodIndex(feature_data.index, freq="M")
0144      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
0145      asset_returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns()
0146      signals = compute_regime_signals(macro, asset_returns)
0147      defensive = run_backtest(asset_returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
0148  
0149      neutral = pd.DataFrame({"p_up": 0.5}, index=feature_data.index)
0150      baseline = run_factor_blend(
0151          asset_returns,
0152          signals,
0153          defensive,
0154          neutral,
0155          FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.0),
0156      )
0157      feature_data = feature_data.loc[feature_data.index.intersection(baseline.index)].copy()
0158      baseline_return = baseline.loc[feature_data.index, "return"]
0159      lagged_vol = baseline_return.rolling(24, min_periods=12).std(ddof=1).shift(1)
0160      feature_data["final_loss3"] = (baseline_return < -0.03).astype(int)
0161      feature_data["final_loss4"] = (baseline_return < -0.04).astype(int)
0162      feature_data["final_loss_1sigma"] = (baseline_return < -lagged_vol).astype(int)
0163  
0164      specifications = {
0165          "loss3_stress": ("final_loss3", STRESS_FEATURES),
0166          "loss4_domestic": ("final_loss4", DOMESTIC_FEATURES),
0167          "loss4_stress": ("final_loss4", STRESS_FEATURES),
0168          "loss_1sigma_stress": ("final_loss_1sigma", STRESS_FEATURES),
0169      }
0170      factors: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
0171      for name, (target, selected_features) in specifications.items():
0172          probability = walk_forward_probability(feature_data, target, selected_features)
0173          factor = make_factor(probability, feature_data[target], target)
0174          factors[name] = factor
0175          factor.to_csv(RESULTS / f"final_blend_crash_meta_{name}_factor.csv")
0176  
0177      baseline_calibration = performance_summary(baseline.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0178      shifts = [0.0, 0.025, 0.05, 0.075, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20]
0179      calibration_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0180      backtests: dict[tuple[str, float], pd.DataFrame] = {}
0181      winners: dict[str, tuple[float, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0182      for name, factor in factors.items():
0183          for shift in shifts:
0184              backtest = run_factor_blend(
0185                  asset_returns,
0186                  signals,
0187                  defensive,
0188                  factor,
0189                  FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=shift),
0190              )
0191              backtests[(name, shift)] = backtest
0192              metrics = performance_summary(backtest.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0193              calibration_rows.append(
0194                  {
0195                      "Strategy": name,
0196                      "max_shift": shift,
0197                      **metrics.to_dict(),
0198                      "AvgTurnover": float(backtest.loc[:CAL_END, "turnover"].mean()),
0199                  }
0200              )
0201          table = pd.DataFrame([row for row in calibration_rows if row["Strategy"] == name])
0202          eligible = table[
0203              (table["MDD"] >= -0.15)
0204              & (table["CAGR"] >= 0.95 * float(baseline_calibration["CAGR"]))
0205          ]
0206          pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0207          winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0208          shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0209          winners[name] = (shift, backtests[(name, shift)])
0210  
0211      comparison_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0212      periods = (
0213          ("calibration_2007_2017", baseline.index.min(), CAL_END),
0214          ("locked_2018_2026", TEST_START, None),
0215          ("full_2007_2026", baseline.index.min(), None),
0216      )
0217      for label, start, end in periods:
0218          strategies = {"FinalBlend": baseline, **{name: value[1] for name, value in winners.items()}}
0219          for name, backtest in strategies.items():
0220              view = backtest.loc[start:end] if end is not None else backtest.loc[start:]
0221              comparison_rows.append(metric_record(label, name, view))
0222      comparison = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
0223  
0224      baseline_locked = baseline.loc[TEST_START:]
0225      baseline_metrics = performance_summary(baseline_locked["return"])
0226      validation: dict[str, object] = {}
0227      for name, (shift, backtest) in winners.items():
0228          locked = backtest.loc[TEST_START:]
0229          metrics = performance_summary(locked["return"])
0230          validation[name] = {
0231              "selected_shift": shift,
0232              "prediction_calibration": predictive_metrics(factors[name].loc[:CAL_END]),
0233              "prediction_locked": predictive_metrics(factors[name], TEST_START),
0234              "locked_deltas": {
0235                  key: float(metrics[key] - baseline_metrics[key])
0236                  for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0237              },
0238              "bootstrap": paired_block_bootstrap(baseline_locked["return"], locked["return"]),
0239          }
0240          backtest.to_csv(RESULTS / f"final_blend_crash_meta_{name}_backtest.csv")
0241  
0242      report = {
0243          "method": {
0244              "target": "next-month FinalBlend loss meta-label",
0245              "features": "domestic portfolio state plus lagged VIX/credit/financial-stress auxiliaries",
0246              "model": "expanding walk-forward balanced logistic regression with one-month label embargo",
0247              "position_mapping": "causal top-20% risk percentile, de-risk only",
0248              "calibration_end": str(CAL_END),
0249              "locked_start": str(TEST_START),
0250          },
0251          "validation": validation,
0252      }
0253      pd.DataFrame(calibration_rows).to_csv(RESULTS / "final_blend_crash_meta_calibration.csv", index=False)
0254      comparison.to_csv(RESULTS / "final_blend_crash_meta_comparison.csv", index=False)
0255      (RESULTS / "final_blend_crash_meta_validation.json").write_text(
0256          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0257      )
0258      print("\n=== WINNERS ===")
0259      print(pd.DataFrame({name: {"max_shift": value[0]} for name, value in winners.items()}).T)
0260      print("\n=== COMPARISON ===")
0261      print(
0262          comparison[["Period", "Strategy", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]]
0263          .to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0264      )
0265      print("\n=== VALIDATION ===")
0266      print(json.dumps(validation, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
0267  
0268  
0269  if __name__ == "__main__":
0270      main()

0045  OAP_SOURCE = "https://openassetpricing.com/SignalDoc-Browser.html"
0046  OAP_SIGNAL_MAP = {
0047      "momentum_trend": ["Mom12m", "Mom6m", "IntMom", "High52", "IndMom", "MomVol"],
0048      "reversal_crowding": ["STreversal", "MRreversal", "LRreversal"],
0049      "low_risk_tail": ["RealizedVol", "IdioVol3F", "MaxRet", "ReturnSkew", "Beta"],
0050      "liquidity_activity": ["Illiquidity", "DolVol", "VolumeTrend", "VolSD"],

0053  
0054  def finite_summary(values: pd.Series, statistic: str, minimum: int = 4) -> float:
0055      clean = values.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan).dropna()
0056      if len(clean) < minimum:
0057          return np.nan
0058      if statistic == "median":
0059          return float(clean.median())
0060      if statistic == "mean":
0061          return float(clean.mean())
0062      if statistic == "std":
0063          return float(clean.std(ddof=1))
0064      raise ValueError(statistic)
0065  
0066  
0067  def lagged_return(history: pd.DataFrame, recent_lag: int, old_lag: int) -> pd.Series:
0068      if len(history) <= old_lag:
0069          return pd.Series(np.nan, index=history.columns)
0070      recent = history.iloc[-1 - recent_lag]
0071      old = history.iloc[-1 - old_lag]
0072      return recent.div(old).sub(1.0).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0073  
0074  
0075  def linear_trend_scaled(series: pd.Series, window: int, minimum: int) -> float:
0076      clean = series.dropna().tail(window)
0077      if len(clean) < minimum:
0078          return np.nan
0079      values = clean.to_numpy(dtype=float)
0080      mean = float(np.mean(values))
0081      if not np.isfinite(mean) or abs(mean) < 1e-12:
0082          return np.nan
0083      time = np.arange(len(values), dtype=float)
0084      slope = float(np.polyfit(time, values, 1)[0])
0085      return slope * len(values) / mean
0086  
0087  
0088  def monthly_volume_statistics(history: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series]:
0089      monthly = history.resample("ME").sum(min_count=1)
0090      trends = pd.Series(
0091          {column: linear_trend_scaled(monthly[column], 60, 30) for column in monthly.columns},
0092          dtype=float,
0093      )
0094      variation: dict[str, float] = {}
0095      for column in monthly.columns:
0096          clean = monthly[column].dropna().tail(36)
0097          if len(clean) < 24 or abs(float(clean.mean())) < 1e-12:
0098              variation[column] = np.nan
0099          else:
0100              variation[column] = float(clean.std(ddof=1) / clean.mean())
0101      return trends, pd.Series(variation, dtype=float)
0102  
0103  
0104  def capm_cross_section(window_returns: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.Series, pd.Series]:
0105      betas: dict[str, float] = {}
0106      residual_volatility: dict[str, float] = {}
0107      for sector in SECTORS:
0108          view = window_returns[[KOSPI200, sector]].dropna()
0109          if len(view) < 40:
0110              betas[sector] = np.nan
0111              residual_volatility[sector] = np.nan
0112              continue
0113          market = view[KOSPI200].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0114          asset = view[sector].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0115          variance = float(np.var(market, ddof=1))
0116          if variance < 1e-12:
0117              betas[sector] = np.nan
0118              residual_volatility[sector] = np.nan
0119              continue
0120          beta = float(np.cov(asset, market, ddof=1)[0, 1] / variance)
0121          alpha = float(np.mean(asset) - beta * np.mean(market))
0122          residual = asset - alpha - beta * market
0123          betas[sector] = beta
0124          residual_volatility[sector] = float(np.std(residual, ddof=1) * np.sqrt(252))
0125      return pd.Series(betas, dtype=float), pd.Series(residual_volatility, dtype=float)
0126  
0127  
0128  def build_oap_features(target_months: pd.PeriodIndex) -> pd.DataFrame:
0129      close, volume = load_index_history()
0130      columns = [KOSPI200] + SECTORS
0131      close = close.reindex(columns=columns)
0132      volume = volume.reindex(columns=columns).replace(0, np.nan)
0133      returns = close.pct_change(fill_method=None).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0134      dollar_volume = close * volume
0135      illiquidity = returns.abs().div(dollar_volume.replace(0, np.nan))
0136      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period]] = []
0137  
0138      for month in target_months:
0139          cutoff = month.to_timestamp(how="start") - pd.Timedelta(days=2)
0140          history_close = close.loc[:cutoff]
0141          history_return = returns.loc[:cutoff]
0142          history_volume = volume.loc[:cutoff]
0143          history_dollar_volume = dollar_volume.loc[:cutoff]
0144          history_illiquidity = illiquidity.loc[:cutoff]
0145          if len(history_close) < 756:
0146              continue
0147  
0148          sector_close = history_close[SECTORS]
0149          sector_return = history_return[SECTORS]
0150          mom12 = lagged_return(sector_close, 21, 252)
0151          mom6 = lagged_return(sector_close, 21, 126)
0152          intermediate_momentum = lagged_return(sector_close, 126, 252)
0153          short_reversal = lagged_return(sector_close, 0, 21)
0154          medium_reversal = lagged_return(sector_close, 252, 378)
0155          long_reversal = lagged_return(sector_close, 252, 756)
0156          high52 = sector_close.iloc[-1].div(sector_close.tail(252).max()).replace(
0157              [np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan
0158          )
0159  
0160          recent21 = sector_return.tail(21)
0161          realized_volatility = recent21.std(ddof=1) * np.sqrt(252)
0162          maximum_return = recent21.max()
0163          return_skewness = recent21.skew()
0164          betas, residual_volatility = capm_cross_section(history_return.tail(252))
0165  
0166          recent_dollar_volume = history_dollar_volume[SECTORS].tail(21).mean()
0167          valid_weight = recent_dollar_volume.where(mom6.notna()).dropna()
0168          if len(valid_weight) >= 4 and float(valid_weight.sum()) > 0:
0169              valid_weight = valid_weight / valid_weight.sum()
0170              volume_weighted_momentum = float((mom6.loc[valid_weight.index] * valid_weight).sum())
0171              mom_volume_correlation = float(
0172                  mom6.loc[valid_weight.index].corr(np.log1p(recent_dollar_volume.loc[valid_weight.index]))
0173              )
0174          else:
0175              volume_weighted_momentum = np.nan
0176              mom_volume_correlation = np.nan
0177  
0178          illiquidity63 = history_illiquidity[SECTORS].tail(63).mean()
0179          illiquidity21 = history_illiquidity[SECTORS].tail(21).mean()
0180          illiquidity252 = history_illiquidity[SECTORS].tail(252).mean()
0181          illiquidity_ratio = illiquidity21.div(illiquidity252).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0182          log_illiquidity63 = np.log10(illiquidity63.clip(lower=1e-18))
0183  
0184          log_dollar_volume63 = np.log1p(history_dollar_volume[SECTORS].tail(63))
0185          dollar_volume_z63 = (
0186              log_dollar_volume63.iloc[-1] - log_dollar_volume63.mean()
0187          ).div(log_dollar_volume63.std(ddof=1).replace(0, np.nan))
0188          volume_trend, volume_variation = monthly_volume_statistics(history_volume[SECTORS])
0189  
0190          market_illiquidity = history_illiquidity[KOSPI200].dropna()
0191          market_illiq_ratio = (
0192              float(market_illiquidity.tail(21).mean() / market_illiquidity.tail(252).mean())
0193              if len(market_illiquidity) >= 252
0194              else np.nan
0195          )
0196          market_monthly_volume = history_volume[KOSPI200].resample("ME").sum(min_count=1)
0197          market_volume_trend = linear_trend_scaled(market_monthly_volume, 60, 30)
0198          market_volume36 = market_monthly_volume.dropna().tail(36)
0199          market_volume_variation = (
0200              float(market_volume36.std(ddof=1) / market_volume36.mean())
0201              if len(market_volume36) >= 24 and float(market_volume36.mean()) > 0
0202              else np.nan
0203          )
0204          market_close = history_close[[KOSPI200]]
0205  
0206          row: dict[str, float | pd.Period] = {
0207              "month": month,
0208              # Momentum, 52-week-high, and industry-momentum analogues.
0209              "oap_sector_mom12_median": finite_summary(mom12, "median"),
0210              "oap_sector_mom6_median": finite_summary(mom6, "median"),
0211              "oap_sector_intmom_median": finite_summary(intermediate_momentum, "median"),
0212              "oap_sector_high52_median": finite_summary(high52, "median"),
0213              "oap_sector_mom6_breadth": float((mom6.dropna() > 0).mean())
0214              if len(mom6.dropna()) >= 4
0215              else np.nan,
0216              "oap_sector_mom12_dispersion": finite_summary(mom12, "std"),
0217              "oap_sector_mom6_dolvol_weighted": volume_weighted_momentum,
0218              "oap_sector_mom6_dolvol_corr": mom_volume_correlation,
0219              "oap_kospi_mom12": float(lagged_return(market_close, 21, 252).iloc[0]),
0220              "oap_kospi_high52": float(
0221                  market_close.iloc[-1, 0] / market_close.tail(252).max().iloc[0]
0222              ),
0223              # Reversal and crowding analogues.
0224              "oap_sector_streversal_median": finite_summary(short_reversal, "median"),
0225              "oap_sector_streversal_dispersion": finite_summary(short_reversal, "std"),
0226              "oap_sector_mrreversal_median": finite_summary(medium_reversal, "median"),
0227              "oap_sector_lrreversal_median": finite_summary(long_reversal, "median"),
0228              "oap_kospi_streversal": float(lagged_return(market_close, 0, 21).iloc[0]),
0229              # Low-risk, volatility, maximum-return, skewness, and beta analogues.
0230              "oap_sector_realizedvol21_median": finite_summary(realized_volatility, "median"),
0231              "oap_sector_idiovol252_median": finite_summary(residual_volatility, "median"),
0232              "oap_sector_maxret21_median": finite_summary(maximum_return, "median"),
0233              "oap_sector_returnskew21_median": finite_summary(return_skewness, "median"),
0234              "oap_sector_beta252_mean": finite_summary(betas, "mean"),
0235              "oap_sector_beta252_dispersion": finite_summary(betas, "std"),
0236              # Amihud, dollar-volume, volume-trend, and volume-variance analogues.
0237              "oap_sector_log_illiquidity63_median": finite_summary(
0238                  log_illiquidity63, "median"
0239              ),
0240              "oap_sector_illiquidity_ratio21_252_median": finite_summary(
0241                  illiquidity_ratio, "median"
0242              ),
0243              "oap_kospi_illiquidity_ratio21_252": market_illiq_ratio,
0244              "oap_sector_dolvol_z63_median": finite_summary(dollar_volume_z63, "median"),
0245              "oap_sector_volume_trend60_median": finite_summary(volume_trend, "median"),
0246              "oap_sector_volume_cv36_median": finite_summary(volume_variation, "median"),
0247              "oap_kospi_volume_trend60": market_volume_trend,
0248              "oap_kospi_volume_cv36": market_volume_variation,
0249          }
0250          rows.append(row)
0251  
0252      output = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")
0253      output.index = pd.PeriodIndex(output.index, freq="M")
0254      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)

0256  
0257  def build_oap_composites(features: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
0258      z = features.apply(causal_zscore)
0259      output = pd.DataFrame(index=features.index)
0260      output["oap_momentum_trend_stress"] = (
0261          -z["oap_sector_mom12_median"]
0262          - z["oap_sector_mom6_median"]
0263          - z["oap_sector_intmom_median"]
0264          - z["oap_sector_high52_median"]
0265          - z["oap_sector_mom6_breadth"]
0266          - z["oap_sector_mom6_dolvol_weighted"]
0267      ) / 6
0268      output["oap_reversal_crowding_stress"] = (
0269          -z["oap_sector_streversal_median"]
0270          - z["oap_kospi_streversal"]
0271          + z["oap_sector_streversal_dispersion"]
0272          + z["oap_sector_mom12_dispersion"]
0273      ) / 4
0274      output["oap_low_risk_tail_stress"] = (
0275          z["oap_sector_realizedvol21_median"]
0276          + z["oap_sector_idiovol252_median"]
0277          + z["oap_sector_maxret21_median"]
0278          - z["oap_sector_returnskew21_median"]
0279          + z["oap_sector_beta252_mean"]
0280          + z["oap_sector_beta252_dispersion"]
0281      ) / 6
0282      liquidity_parts = pd.concat(
0283          [
0284              z["oap_sector_log_illiquidity63_median"],
0285              z["oap_sector_illiquidity_ratio21_252_median"],
0286              z["oap_kospi_illiquidity_ratio21_252"],
0287              -z["oap_sector_dolvol_z63_median"],
0288              -z["oap_sector_volume_trend60_median"],
0289              z["oap_sector_volume_cv36_median"],
0290          ],
0291          axis=1,
0292      )
0293      output["oap_liquidity_activity_stress"] = liquidity_parts.mean(axis=1).where(
0294          liquidity_parts.notna().sum(axis=1) >= 4
0295      )
0296      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0297  
0298  
0299  def forward_path_loss(returns: pd.Series, horizon: int) -> pd.Series:
0300      output = pd.Series(np.nan, index=returns.index, dtype=float)
0301      for position in range(len(returns)):
0302          future = returns.iloc[position : position + horizon]
0303          if len(future) < horizon:
0304              continue
0305          path = (1 + future).cumprod() - 1
0306          output.iloc[position] = float(path.min())
0307      return output
0308  
0309  
0310  def walk_forward_probability_embargo(
0311      data: pd.DataFrame,
0312      target: str,
0313      features: list[str],
0314      embargo_months: int,
0315      min_train: int = 36,
0316  ) -> pd.Series:
0317      probability = pd.Series(np.nan, index=data.index, dtype=float)
0318      template = make_model()
0319      for number in range(len(data)):
0320          # The label consumes `embargo_months` future returns. Excluding the same
0321          # number of trailing rows prevents overlapping future returns entering
0322          # the training set for the prediction month.
0323          train_end = number - embargo_months
0324          if train_end < min_train:
0325              continue
0326          train = data.iloc[:train_end].dropna(subset=[target])
0327          y = train[target].astype(int)
0328          if y.sum() < 4 or (len(y) - y.sum()) < 12:
0329              continue
0330          model = clone(template)
0331          model.fit(train[features], y)
0332          probability.iloc[number] = float(
0333              model.predict_proba(data.iloc[[number]][features])[:, 1][0]
0334          )
0335      return probability
0336

0338  def metric_record(period: str, strategy: str, backtest: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, object]:
0339      metrics = performance_summary(backtest["return"])
0340      return {
0341          "Period": period,
0342          "Strategy": strategy,
0343          "Months": len(backtest),
0344          **metrics.to_dict(),
0345          "AvgTurnover": float(backtest["turnover"].mean()),
0346      }

0349  def load_factor(name: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
0350      frame = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / f"market_structure_{name}_factor.csv")
0351      frame["month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(frame["month"], freq="M")
0352      return frame.set_index("month")

0354  
0355  def blend_risk_factors(first: pd.DataFrame, second: pd.DataFrame, second_weight: float) -> pd.DataFrame:
0356      index = first.index.union(second.index).sort_values()
0357      first_severity = ((0.5 - first.reindex(index)["p_up"]) / 0.15).clip(0, 1)
0358      second_severity = ((0.5 - second.reindex(index)["p_up"]) / 0.15).clip(0, 1)
0359      severity = (1 - second_weight) * first_severity + second_weight * second_severity
0360      output = pd.DataFrame(index=index)
0361      output["risk_severity"] = severity
0362      output["p_up"] = 0.5 - 0.15 * severity
0363      output["score"] = -severity
0364      output["factor_name"] = f"blend_{second_weight:.2f}"
0365      return output
0366  
0367  
0368  def make_direct_trend_factor(composites: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
0369      # High momentum percentile is an upside signal. The percentile is causal and
0370      # therefore remains comparable across the changing KRX sector-index universe.
0371      strength = -composites["oap_momentum_trend_stress"]
0372      percentile = causal_percentile(strength)
0373      score = (2 * percentile - 1).clip(-1, 1)
0374      output = pd.DataFrame(index=composites.index)
0375      output["trend_percentile"] = percentile
0376      output["score"] = score
0377      output["p_up"] = 0.5 + 0.15 * score
0378      output["factor_name"] = "oap_direct_momentum"
0379      return output
0380  
0381  
0382  def compose_risk_override_trend(
0383      existing: pd.DataFrame,
0384      trend: pd.DataFrame,
0385      trend_weight: float,
0386      mode: str,
0387  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0388      index = existing.index.union(trend.index).sort_values()
0389      risk = ((0.5 - existing.reindex(index)["p_up"]) / 0.15).clip(0, 1).fillna(0)
0390      trend_score = ((trend.reindex(index)["p_up"] - 0.5) / 0.15).clip(-1, 1).fillna(0)
0391      if mode == "risk_on_only":
0392          upside = trend_score.clip(lower=0)
0393      elif mode == "symmetric":
0394          upside = trend_score
0395      else:
0396          raise ValueError(mode)
0397      # Existing crash-risk defense has priority. OAP trend only uses the residual
0398      # risk budget left when that defense is inactive.
0399      score = (-risk + (1 - risk) * trend_weight * upside).clip(-1, 1)
0400      output = pd.DataFrame(index=index)
0401      output["risk_severity"] = risk
0402      output["trend_score"] = trend_score
0403      output["score"] = score
0404      output["p_up"] = 0.5 + 0.15 * score
0405      output["factor_name"] = f"risk_override_{mode}_{trend_weight:.2f}"
0406      return output
0407  
0408  
0409  def choose_shift(
0410      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0411      asset_returns: pd.DataFrame,
0412      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0413      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0414      baseline_calibration: pd.Series,
0415  ) -> tuple[float, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0416      rows: list[dict[str, float]] = []
0417      paths: dict[float, pd.DataFrame] = {}
0418      for shift in (0.0, 0.025, 0.05, 0.075, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20):
0419          path = run_factor_blend(
0420              asset_returns, signals, defensive, factor, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=shift)
0421          )
0422          paths[shift] = path
0423          metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0424          rows.append({"max_shift": shift, **metrics.to_dict()})
0425      table = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0426      eligible = table[
0427          (table["MDD"] >= -0.15)
0428          & (table["CAGR"] >= 0.95 * float(baseline_calibration["CAGR"]))
0429      ]
0430      pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0431      winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0432      shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0433      return shift, paths[shift], table
0434  
0435  
0436  def choose_signal_blend(
0437      existing: pd.DataFrame,
0438      oap: pd.DataFrame,
0439      asset_returns: pd.DataFrame,
0440      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0441      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0442      baseline_calibration: pd.Series,
0443      existing_shift: float,
0444  ) -> tuple[float, float, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0445      rows: list[dict[str, float]] = []
0446      candidates: dict[tuple[float, float], tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0447      # Weight zero preserves the existing strategy and allows an honest no-change
0448      # decision when the OAP probability signal has no incremental value.
0449      for oap_weight in (0.0, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75):
0450          factor = blend_risk_factors(existing, oap, oap_weight)
0451          shift_grid = (existing_shift,) if oap_weight == 0 else (0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20)
0452          for shift in shift_grid:
0453              path = run_factor_vol_target(
0454                  asset_returns,
0455                  signals,
0456                  defensive,
0457                  factor,
0458                  max_shift=shift,
0459                  target_vol=0.15,
0460              )
0461              candidates[(oap_weight, shift)] = (factor, path)
0462              metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0463              rows.append(
0464                  {
0465                      "oap_weight": oap_weight,
0466                      "max_shift": shift,
0467                      **metrics.to_dict(),
0468                  }
0469              )
0470      table = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0471      eligible = table[
0472          (table["MDD"] >= -0.15)
0473          & (table["CAGR"] >= 0.95 * float(baseline_calibration["CAGR"]))
0474      ]
0475      pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0476      winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0477      weight = float(winner["oap_weight"])
0478      shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0479      factor, path = candidates[(weight, shift)]
0480      return weight, shift, factor, path, table
0481  
0482  
0483  def choose_risk_override_trend(
0484      existing: pd.DataFrame,
0485      existing_shift: float,
0486      trend: pd.DataFrame,
0487      asset_returns: pd.DataFrame,
0488      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0489      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0490      reference_calibration: pd.Series,
0491  ) -> tuple[str, float, float, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0492      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0493      candidates: dict[tuple[str, float, float], tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0494      baseline_factor = compose_risk_override_trend(existing, trend, 0.0, "risk_on_only")
0495      baseline_path = run_factor_vol_target(
0496          asset_returns,
0497          signals,
0498          defensive,
0499          baseline_factor,
0500          max_shift=existing_shift,
0501          target_vol=0.15,
0502      )
0503      candidates[("existing", 0.0, existing_shift)] = (baseline_factor, baseline_path)
0504      baseline_metrics = performance_summary(baseline_path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0505      rows.append(
0506          {
0507              "mode": "existing",
0508              "trend_weight": 0.0,
0509              "max_shift": existing_shift,
0510              **baseline_metrics.to_dict(),
0511          }
0512      )
0513      for mode in ("risk_on_only", "symmetric"):
0514          for trend_weight in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00):
0515              factor = compose_risk_override_trend(existing, trend, trend_weight, mode)
0516              for shift in (0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20):
0517                  path = run_factor_vol_target(
0518                      asset_returns,
0519                      signals,
0520                      defensive,
0521                      factor,
0522                      max_shift=shift,
0523                      target_vol=0.15,
0524                  )
0525                  candidates[(mode, trend_weight, shift)] = (factor, path)
0526                  metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0527                  rows.append(
0528                      {
0529                          "mode": mode,
0530                          "trend_weight": trend_weight,
0531                          "max_shift": shift,
0532                          **metrics.to_dict(),
0533                      }
0534                  )
0535      table = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0536      # Preserve both return and drawdown quality of the existing strategy on the
0537      # calibration sample; then rank the surviving choices by Calmar/Sharpe/CAGR.
0538      eligible = table[
0539          (table["CAGR"] >= 0.99 * float(reference_calibration["CAGR"]))
0540          & (table["MDD"] >= float(reference_calibration["MDD"]) - 0.005)
0541      ]
0542      pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0543      winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0544      mode = str(winner["mode"])
0545      weight = float(winner["trend_weight"])
0546      shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0547      factor, path = candidates[(mode, weight, shift)]
0548      return mode, weight, shift, factor, path, table
0549  
0550  
0551  def choose_risk_committee(
0552      factors: dict[str, pd.DataFrame],
0553      asset_returns: pd.DataFrame,
0554      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0555      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0556      reference_calibration: pd.Series,
0557  ) -> tuple[str, float, float, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0558      index = pd.PeriodIndex([], freq="M")
0559      for factor in factors.values():
0560          index = index.union(factor.index)
0561      severities = pd.concat(
0562          {
0563              name: ((0.5 - factor.reindex(index)["p_up"]) / 0.15).clip(0, 1).fillna(0)
0564              for name, factor in factors.items()
0565          },
0566          axis=1,
0567      )
0568      aggregations = {
0569          "minimum_consensus": severities.min(axis=1),
0570          "median_committee": severities.median(axis=1),
0571          "mean_committee": severities.mean(axis=1),
0572          "maximum_union": severities.max(axis=1),
0573      }
0574      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0575      candidates: dict[tuple[str, float, float], tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0576      for method, severity in aggregations.items():
0577          factor = pd.DataFrame(index=index)
0578          factor["risk_severity"] = severity
0579          factor["p_up"] = 0.5 - 0.15 * severity
0580          factor["score"] = -severity
0581          factor["factor_name"] = method
0582          for shift in (0.10, 0.15, 0.20):
0583              for target_vol in (0.145, 0.150, 0.155):
0584                  path = run_factor_vol_target(
0585                      asset_returns,
0586                      signals,
0587                      defensive,
0588                      factor,
0589                      max_shift=shift,
0590                      target_vol=target_vol,
0591                  )
0592                  candidates[(method, shift, target_vol)] = (factor, path)
0593                  metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0594                  rows.append(
0595                      {
0596                          "method": method,
0597                          "max_shift": shift,
0598                          "target_vol": target_vol,
0599                          **metrics.to_dict(),
0600                      }
0601                  )
0602      table = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0603      eligible = table[
0604          (table["CAGR"] >= 0.99 * float(reference_calibration["CAGR"]))
0605          & (table["MDD"] >= float(reference_calibration["MDD"]) - 0.005)
0606      ]
0607      pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0608      winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0609      method = str(winner["method"])
0610      shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0611      target_vol = float(winner["target_vol"])
0612      factor, path = candidates[(method, shift, target_vol)]
0613      return method, shift, target_vol, factor, path, table
0614  
0615  
0616  def choose_medium_horizon_oap(
0617      data: pd.DataFrame,
0618      feature_columns: list[str],
0619      configurations: list[tuple[str, int]],
0620      asset_returns: pd.DataFrame,
0621      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0622      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0623      reference_calibration: pd.Series,
0624  ) -> tuple[str, int, float, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0625      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0626      candidates: dict[tuple[str, float], tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0627      for target, horizon in configurations:
0628          probability = walk_forward_probability_embargo(
0629              data,
0630              target,
0631              feature_columns,
0632              embargo_months=horizon,
0633          )
0634          factor = make_factor(probability, data[target], target)
0635          for shift in (0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20):
0636              path = run_factor_vol_target(
0637                  asset_returns,
0638                  signals,
0639                  defensive,
0640                  factor,
0641                  max_shift=shift,
0642                  target_vol=0.15,
0643              )
0644              candidates[(target, shift)] = (factor, path)
0645              metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0646              rows.append(
0647                  {
0648                      "target": target,
0649                      "horizon": horizon,
0650                      "max_shift": shift,
0651                      "events": int(data.loc[:CAL_END, target].sum()),
0652                      **metrics.to_dict(),
0653                  }
0654              )
0655      table = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0656      # The multi-objective goal is explicit: only candidates that improve all
0657      # three calibration objectives over the existing strategy are eligible.
0658      eligible = table[
0659          (table["CAGR"] >= float(reference_calibration["CAGR"]))
0660          & (table["Sharpe"] >= float(reference_calibration["Sharpe"]))
0661          & (table["MDD"] >= float(reference_calibration["MDD"]) - 1e-8)
0662      ]
0663      pool = eligible if not eligible.empty else table
0664      winner = pool.sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False).iloc[0]
0665      target = str(winner["target"])
0666      horizon = int(winner["horizon"])
0667      shift = float(winner["max_shift"])
0668      factor, path = candidates[(target, shift)]
0669      return target, horizon, shift, factor, path, table
0670  
0671  
0672  def main() -> None:
0673      base_data = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0674      base_data.index = pd.PeriodIndex(base_data.index, freq="M")
0675      features = build_oap_features(base_data.index)
0676      composites = build_oap_composites(features)
0677      direct_trend_factor = make_direct_trend_factor(composites)
0678      features.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_features.csv")
0679      composites.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_composites.csv")
0680      direct_trend_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_direct_momentum_factor.csv")
0681  
0682      structure = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "market_structure_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0683      structure.index = pd.PeriodIndex(structure.index, freq="M")
0684      core_structure = [
0685          "systemic_correlation_stress",
0686          "breadth_dispersion_stress",
0687          "volume_stress",
0688          "tail_shape_stress",
0689          "sector_linkage_stress",
0690      ]
0691      index_volume = ["index_volume_proxy_stress"]
0692      oap_columns = list(composites.columns)
0693      data = base_data.join(features, how="left").join(composites, how="left").join(
0694          structure, how="left"
0695      )
0696  
0697      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
0698      asset_returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns()
0699      signals = compute_regime_signals(macro, asset_returns)
0700      defensive = run_backtest(asset_returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
0701      neutral = pd.DataFrame({"p_up": 0.5}, index=data.index)
0702      baseline = run_factor_blend(
0703          asset_returns, signals, defensive, neutral, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.0)
0704      )
0705      data = data.loc[data.index.intersection(baseline.index)].copy()
0706      baseline_return = baseline.loc[data.index, "return"]
0707      data["final_loss3"] = (baseline_return < -0.03).astype(int)
0708      medium_targets = [
0709          ("oap_path_loss_2m_4", 2, -0.04),
0710          ("oap_path_loss_2m_5", 2, -0.05),
0711          ("oap_path_loss_3m_5", 3, -0.05),
0712          ("oap_path_loss_3m_6", 3, -0.06),
0713      ]
0714      for target, horizon, threshold in medium_targets:
0715          path_loss = forward_path_loss(baseline_return, horizon)
0716          data[target] = (path_loss < threshold).where(path_loss.notna()).astype(float)
0717  
0718      specifications = {
0719          "oap_momentum_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + ["oap_momentum_trend_stress"],
0720          "oap_reversal_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + ["oap_reversal_crowding_stress"],
0721          "oap_lowrisk_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + ["oap_low_risk_tail_stress"],
0722          "oap_liquidity_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + ["oap_liquidity_activity_stress"],
0723          "oap_all_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + oap_columns,
0724          "structure_plus_oap_domestic": DOMESTIC_FEATURES + core_structure + oap_columns,
0725          "structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic": (
0726              DOMESTIC_FEATURES + core_structure + index_volume + oap_columns
0727          ),
0728      }
0729      factors: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
0730      for name, selected in specifications.items():
0731          probability = walk_forward_probability(data, "final_loss3", selected)
0732          factor = make_factor(probability, data["final_loss3"], name)
0733          factors[name] = factor
0734          factor.to_csv(RESULTS / f"openassetpricing_{name}_factor.csv")
0735  
0736      baseline_calibration = performance_summary(baseline.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0737      winners: dict[str, tuple[float, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0738      calibration_rows: list[pd.DataFrame] = []
0739      for name, factor in factors.items():
0740          shift, path, table = choose_shift(
0741              factor, asset_returns, signals, defensive, baseline_calibration
0742          )
0743          winners[name] = (shift, path)
0744          table.insert(0, "Strategy", name)
0745          calibration_rows.append(table)
0746          path.to_csv(RESULTS / f"openassetpricing_{name}_backtest.csv")
0747  
0748      standalone_names = [name for name in specifications if name.startswith("oap_")]
0749      standalone_selection = []
0750      for name in standalone_names:
0751          shift, path = winners[name]
0752          metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0753          standalone_selection.append(
0754              {"Strategy": name, "max_shift": shift, **metrics.to_dict()}
0755          )
0756      oap_winner = (
0757          pd.DataFrame(standalone_selection)
0758          .sort_values(["Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False)
0759          .iloc[0]["Strategy"]
0760      )
0761      oap_factor = factors[str(oap_winner)]
0762  
0763      existing_structure_factor = load_factor("loss3_composite_domestic")
0764      existing_proxy_factor = load_factor("loss3_composite_plus_index_volume_domestic")
0765      existing_structure_vol = run_factor_vol_target(
0766          asset_returns,
0767          signals,
0768          defensive,
0769          existing_structure_factor,
0770          max_shift=0.15,
0771          target_vol=0.15,
0772      )
0773      existing_proxy_vol = run_factor_vol_target(
0774          asset_returns,
0775          signals,
0776          defensive,
0777          existing_proxy_factor,
0778          max_shift=0.20,
0779          target_vol=0.15,
0780      )
0781      existing_structure_calibration = performance_summary(
0782          existing_structure_vol.loc[:CAL_END, "return"]
0783      )
0784      existing_proxy_calibration = performance_summary(existing_proxy_vol.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0785  
0786      (
0787          medium_target,
0788          medium_horizon,
0789          medium_shift,
0790          medium_factor,
0791          medium_path,
0792          medium_grid,
0793      ) = choose_medium_horizon_oap(
0794          data,
0795          DOMESTIC_FEATURES + oap_columns,
0796          [(target, horizon) for target, horizon, _ in medium_targets],
0797          asset_returns,
0798          signals,
0799          defensive,
0800          existing_structure_calibration,
0801      )
0802      medium_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv")
0803      medium_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_backtest.csv")
0804  
0805      blend_weight, blend_shift, blend_factor, blend_path, blend_grid = choose_signal_blend(
0806          existing_structure_factor,
0807          oap_factor,
0808          asset_returns,
0809          signals,
0810          defensive,
0811          existing_structure_calibration,
0812          0.15,
0813      )
0814      (
0815          proxy_blend_weight,
0816          proxy_blend_shift,
0817          proxy_blend_factor,
0818          proxy_blend_path,
0819          proxy_blend_grid,
0820      ) = choose_signal_blend(
0821          existing_proxy_factor,
0822          oap_factor,
0823          asset_returns,

0826          existing_proxy_calibration,
0827          0.20,
0828      )

0830      blend_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_signal_blend_structure_backtest.csv")
0831      proxy_blend_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_signal_blend_proxy_factor.csv")
0832      proxy_blend_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_signal_blend_proxy_backtest.csv")
0833  
0834      (
0835          trend_structure_mode,
0836          trend_structure_weight,
0837          trend_structure_shift,
0838          trend_structure_factor,
0839          trend_structure_path,
0840          trend_structure_grid,
0841      ) = choose_risk_override_trend(
0842          existing_structure_factor,
0843          0.15,
0844          direct_trend_factor,
0845          asset_returns,
0846          signals,
0847          defensive,
0848          existing_structure_calibration,
0849      )
0850      (
0851          trend_proxy_mode,
0852          trend_proxy_weight,
0853          trend_proxy_shift,
0854          trend_proxy_factor,
0855          trend_proxy_path,
0856          trend_proxy_grid,
0857      ) = choose_risk_override_trend(
0858          existing_proxy_factor,
0859          0.20,
0860          direct_trend_factor,
0861          asset_returns,
0862          signals,
0863          defensive,
0864          existing_proxy_calibration,
0865      )
0866      trend_structure_factor.to_csv(
0867          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_structure_factor.csv"
0868      )
0869      trend_structure_path.to_csv(
0870          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_structure_backtest.csv"
0871      )
0872      trend_proxy_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_proxy_factor.csv")
0873      trend_proxy_path.to_csv(
0874          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_proxy_backtest.csv"
0875      )
0876  
0877      joint_structure_shift = winners["structure_plus_oap_domestic"][0]
0878      joint_structure_vol = run_factor_vol_target(
0879          asset_returns,
0880          signals,
0881          defensive,
0882          factors["structure_plus_oap_domestic"],
0883          max_shift=joint_structure_shift,
0884          target_vol=0.15,
0885      )
0886      joint_proxy_shift = winners["structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic"][0]
0887      joint_proxy_vol = run_factor_vol_target(
0888          asset_returns,
0889          signals,
0890          defensive,
0891          factors["structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic"],
0892          max_shift=joint_proxy_shift,
0893          target_vol=0.15,
0894      )
0895      (
0896          committee_method,
0897          committee_shift,
0898          committee_target_vol,
0899          committee_factor,
0900          committee_path,
0901          committee_grid,
0902      ) = choose_risk_committee(
0903          {
0904              "existing_structure": existing_structure_factor,
0905              "existing_index_volume": existing_proxy_factor,
0906              "joint_oap": factors["structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic"],
0907          },
0908          asset_returns,
0909          signals,
0910          defensive,
0911          existing_structure_calibration,
0912      )
0913      committee_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_committee_factor.csv")
0914      committee_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_committee_backtest.csv")
0915  
0916      standard: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {
0917          "FinalBlend": baseline,
0918          **{name: path for name, (_, path) in winners.items()},
0919          "ExistingStructureVol15": existing_structure_vol,
0920          "ExistingStructureIndexVolumeVol15": existing_proxy_vol,
0921          "JointStructureOAPVol15": joint_structure_vol,
0922          "JointStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": joint_proxy_vol,
0923          "SignalBlendStructureOAPVol15": blend_path,
0924          "SignalBlendStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": proxy_blend_path,
0925          "TrendOverrideStructureOAPVol15": trend_structure_path,
0926          "TrendOverrideStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": trend_proxy_path,
0927          "CalibratedOAPRiskCommittee": committee_path,
0928          "SelectedMediumHorizonOAPVol15": medium_path,
0929      }
0930      comparison_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0931      periods = (
0932          ("calibration_2007_2017", baseline.index.min(), CAL_END),
0933          ("locked_2018_2026", TEST_START, baseline.index.max()),
0934          ("full_2007_2026", baseline.index.min(), baseline.index.max()),
0935          ("subperiod_2007_2012", baseline.index.min(), pd.Period("2012-12", freq="M")),
0936          ("subperiod_2013_2017", pd.Period("2013-01", freq="M"), CAL_END),
0937          ("subperiod_2018_2021", TEST_START, pd.Period("2021-12", freq="M")),
0938          ("subperiod_2022_2026", pd.Period("2022-01", freq="M"), baseline.index.max()),

0941          for name, path in standard.items():
0942              comparison_rows.append(metric_record(label, name, path.loc[start:end]))
0943      comparison = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
0944      comparison.to_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_comparison.csv", index=False)
0945  
0946      cost_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0947      cost_strategies = {
0948          "ExistingStructureVol15": (existing_structure_factor, 0.15, 0.15),
0949          "ExistingStructureIndexVolumeVol15": (existing_proxy_factor, 0.20, 0.15),
0950          "JointStructureOAPVol15": (
0951              factors["structure_plus_oap_domestic"],
0952              joint_structure_shift,
0953              0.15,
0954          ),
0955          "JointStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": (
0956              factors["structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic"],
0957              joint_proxy_shift,
0958              0.15,
0959          ),
0960          "SignalBlendStructureOAPVol15": (blend_factor, blend_shift, 0.15),
0961          "SignalBlendStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": (
0962              proxy_blend_factor,
0963              proxy_blend_shift,
0964              0.15,
0965          ),
0966          "TrendOverrideStructureOAPVol15": (
0967              trend_structure_factor,
0968              trend_structure_shift,
0969              0.15,
0970          ),
0971          "TrendOverrideStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": (
0972              trend_proxy_factor,
0973              trend_proxy_shift,
0974              0.15,
0975          ),
0976          "CalibratedOAPRiskCommittee": (
0977              committee_factor,
0978              committee_shift,
0979              committee_target_vol,
0980          ),
0981          "SelectedMediumHorizonOAPVol15": (medium_factor, medium_shift, 0.15),
0982      }
0983      for cost in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
0984          for name, (factor, shift, target_vol) in cost_strategies.items():
0985              path = run_factor_vol_target(
0986                  asset_returns,
0987                  signals,
0988                  defensive,
0989                  factor,
0990                  max_shift=shift,
0991                  target_vol=target_vol,
0992                  cost_multiplier=cost,
0993              )
0994              cost_rows.append(metric_record(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_full", name, path))
0995              cost_rows.append(
0996                  metric_record(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_locked", name, path.loc[TEST_START:])
0997              )
0998      pd.DataFrame(cost_rows).to_csv(
0999          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_cost_robustness.csv", index=False
1000      )
1001  
1002      selected_locked = {
1003          "JointStructureOAPVol15": joint_structure_vol.loc[TEST_START:, "return"],
1004          "JointStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": joint_proxy_vol.loc[TEST_START:, "return"],
1005          "SignalBlendStructureOAPVol15": blend_path.loc[TEST_START:, "return"],
1006          "SignalBlendStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": proxy_blend_path.loc[
1007              TEST_START:, "return"
1008          ],
1009          "TrendOverrideStructureOAPVol15": trend_structure_path.loc[
1010              TEST_START:, "return"
1011          ],
1012          "TrendOverrideStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15": trend_proxy_path.loc[
1013              TEST_START:, "return"
1014          ],
1015          "CalibratedOAPRiskCommittee": committee_path.loc[TEST_START:, "return"],
1016          "SelectedMediumHorizonOAPVol15": medium_path.loc[TEST_START:, "return"],
1017      }
1018      bootstrap = {
1019          name: paired_block_bootstrap(
1020              existing_structure_vol.loc[TEST_START:, "return"], returns
1021          )
1022          for name, returns in selected_locked.items()
1023      }
1024      validation = {
1025          "source": OAP_SOURCE,
1026          "signal_map": OAP_SIGNAL_MAP,
1027          "selection": {
1028              "calibration_end": str(CAL_END),
1029              "locked_start": str(TEST_START),
1030              "standalone_oap_winner": str(oap_winner),
1031              "standalone_oap_shift": winners[str(oap_winner)][0],
1032              "joint_structure_shift": joint_structure_shift,
1033              "joint_proxy_shift": joint_proxy_shift,
1034              "signal_blend_structure_oap_weight": blend_weight,
1035              "signal_blend_structure_shift": blend_shift,
1036              "signal_blend_proxy_oap_weight": proxy_blend_weight,
1037              "signal_blend_proxy_shift": proxy_blend_shift,
1038              "trend_override_structure_mode": trend_structure_mode,
1039              "trend_override_structure_weight": trend_structure_weight,
1040              "trend_override_structure_shift": trend_structure_shift,
1041              "trend_override_proxy_mode": trend_proxy_mode,
1042              "trend_override_proxy_weight": trend_proxy_weight,
1043              "trend_override_proxy_shift": trend_proxy_shift,
1044              "committee_method": committee_method,
1045              "committee_shift": committee_shift,
1046              "committee_target_vol": committee_target_vol,
1047              "medium_horizon_target": medium_target,
1048              "medium_horizon_months": medium_horizon,
1049              "medium_horizon_shift": medium_shift,
1050          },
1051          "prediction": {
1052              name: {
1053                  "calibration": predictive_metrics(factor.loc[:CAL_END]),
1054                  "locked": predictive_metrics(factor, TEST_START),
1055              }
1056              for name, factor in factors.items()
1057          },
1058          "medium_horizon_prediction": {
1059              "calibration": predictive_metrics(medium_factor.loc[:CAL_END]),
1060              "locked": predictive_metrics(medium_factor, TEST_START),
1061          },
1062          "bootstrap_vs_existing_structure_vol15": bootstrap,
1063          "notes": [
1064              "All selection and tuning use data through 2017 only.",
1065              "2018-2026 is never used to select features, blend weights, or shift sizes.",
1066              "OAP signals are translated to KRX sector-index analogues; they are not exact stock-level replications.",
1067              "OAP Placebo/Dropped signals were excluded before testing.",
1068          ],
1069      }
1070      (RESULTS / "openassetpricing_validation.json").write_text(
1071          json.dumps(validation, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
1072      )
1073      pd.concat(calibration_rows, ignore_index=True).to_csv(
1074          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_calibration.csv", index=False
1075      )
1076      blend_grid.assign(existing="structure").to_csv(
1077          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_signal_blend_calibration.csv", index=False
1078      )
1079      proxy_blend_grid.assign(existing="structure_index_volume").to_csv(
1080          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_signal_blend_proxy_calibration.csv", index=False
1081      )
1082      trend_structure_grid.assign(existing="structure").to_csv(
1083          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_calibration.csv", index=False
1084      )
1085      trend_proxy_grid.assign(existing="structure_index_volume").to_csv(
1086          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_trend_override_proxy_calibration.csv", index=False
1087      )
1088      committee_grid.to_csv(
1089          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_committee_calibration.csv", index=False
1090      )
1091      medium_grid.to_csv(
1092          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_calibration.csv", index=False
1093      )
1094  
1095      print("\n=== OAP FEATURE COVERAGE ===")
1096      print(features.notna().mean().to_string(float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.3f}"))
1097      print("\n=== SELECTED SETTINGS ===")
1098      print(json.dumps(validation["selection"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
1099      print("\n=== KEY COMPARISON ===")
1100      key_names = [
1101          "FinalBlend",
1102          str(oap_winner),
1103          "structure_plus_oap_domestic",
1104          "structure_indexvolume_plus_oap_domestic",
1105          "ExistingStructureVol15",
1106          "ExistingStructureIndexVolumeVol15",
1107          "JointStructureOAPVol15",

1110          "SignalBlendStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15",
1111          "TrendOverrideStructureOAPVol15",
1112          "TrendOverrideStructureIndexVolumeOAPVol15",
1113          "CalibratedOAPRiskCommittee",
1114          "SelectedMediumHorizonOAPVol15",
1115      ]
1116      print(
1117          comparison[
1118              comparison["Period"].isin(
1119                  ["calibration_2007_2017", "locked_2018_2026", "full_2007_2026"]
1120              )
1121              & comparison["Strategy"].isin(key_names)
1122          ].to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
1123      )

0001  from __future__ import annotations

0011  from strategies.stage04_ml_feedback.regime_lightgbm_factor_experiment import (
0012      FactorBlendConfig,
0013      apply_factor_tilt,
0014      paired_block_bootstrap,
0015      run_factor_blend,
0016  )
0017  from strategies.core.regime_research import (
0018      ASSETS,
0019      StrategyConfig,
0020      compute_regime_signals,

0033  def load_factor(name: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
0034      frame = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / f"market_structure_{name}_factor.csv")
0035      frame["month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(frame["month"], freq="M")
0036      return frame.set_index("month")

0038  
0039  def run_factor_vol_target(
0040      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0041      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0042      defensive_path: pd.DataFrame,
0043      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0044      max_shift: float,
0045      target_vol: float = 0.15,
0046      cost_multiplier: float = 1.0,
0047      financing_rate: float = 0.04,
0048  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0049      months = signals.index.intersection(returns.index).intersection(defensive_path.index)
0050      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period | str]] = []
0051      pretrade = np.zeros(len(ASSETS))
0052      nav = 1.0
0053      peak = 1.0
0054      first_trade = True
0055      for month in months:
0056          hard = hard_regime_weights(signals.loc[month])
0057          defensive = defensive_path.loc[month, [f"w_{asset}" for asset in ASSETS]].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0058          base = 0.40 * hard + 0.60 * defensive
0059          probability = float(factor.loc[month, "p_up"]) if month in factor.index else 0.5
0060          score = float(np.clip((probability - 0.5) / 0.15, -1, 1))
0061          unlevered = apply_factor_tilt(base, score, max_shift)
0062  
0063          history = returns.loc[returns.index < month, ASSETS].tail(24)
0064          if len(history) >= 12:
0065              conditional = history.to_numpy(dtype=float) @ unlevered
0066              weights = np.exp(np.linspace(-2.0, 0.0, len(conditional)))
0067              weights /= weights.sum()
0068              mean = float(weights @ conditional)
0069              variance = float(weights @ (conditional - mean) ** 2)
0070              forecast_vol = math.sqrt(max(variance, 1e-8) * 12)
0071              leverage = float(np.clip(target_vol / forecast_vol, 0.50, 1.50))
0072          else:
0073              forecast_vol = np.nan
0074              leverage = 1.20
0075          asset_weights = leverage * unlevered
0076          debt_weight = 1.0 - leverage
0077          delta = asset_weights - pretrade
0078          turnover = np.abs(delta).sum() if first_trade else 0.5 * np.abs(delta).sum()
0079          trade_cost = np.abs(delta).sum() * 0.0015 * cost_multiplier
0080          fx_cost = (
0081              abs((asset_weights[2] + asset_weights[3]) - (pretrade[2] + pretrade[3]))
0082              * 0.0005
0083              * cost_multiplier
0084          )
0085          financing = debt_weight * ((1 + financing_rate) ** (1 / 12) - 1)
0086          asset_return = returns.loc[month, ASSETS].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0087          gross_return = float(asset_weights @ asset_return + financing)
0088          net_return = gross_return - trade_cost - fx_cost
0089          nav *= 1 + net_return
0090          peak = max(peak, nav)
0091          pretrade = asset_weights * (1 + asset_return) / (1 + gross_return)
0092          first_trade = False
0093          rows.append(
0094              {
0095                  "month": month,
0096                  "return": net_return,
0097                  "gross_return": gross_return,
0098                  "nav": nav,
0099                  "drawdown": nav / peak - 1,
0100                  "turnover": turnover,
0101                  "trade_cost": trade_cost,
0102                  "fx_cost": fx_cost,
0103                  "forecast_vol": forecast_vol,
0104                  "leverage": leverage,
0105                  "risk_score": score,
0106                  **{f"w_{asset}": asset_weights[i] for i, asset in enumerate(ASSETS)},
0107              }
0108          )
0109      return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")

0112  def record(test: str, strategy: str, backtest: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, float | str]:
0113      metrics = performance_summary(backtest["return"])
0114      return {
0115          "Test": test,
0116          "Strategy": strategy,
0117          **metrics.to_dict(),
0118          "AvgTurnover": float(backtest["turnover"].mean()),
0119      }

0122  def main() -> None:
0123      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
0124      returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns()
0125      signals = compute_regime_signals(macro, returns)
0126      defensive = run_backtest(returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
0127      factors = {
0128          "CompositeDomestic": (load_factor("loss3_composite_domestic"), 0.15),
0129          "VolumeDomestic": (load_factor("loss3_volume_domestic"), 0.20),
0130          "IndexVolumeRaw": (load_factor("loss3_index_volume_raw_domestic"), 0.20),
0131          "IndexVolumeComposite": (
0132              load_factor("loss3_index_volume_composite_domestic"),
0133              0.20,
0134          ),
0135          "CompositePlusIndexVolume": (
0136              load_factor("loss3_composite_plus_index_volume_domestic"),
0137              0.20,
0138          ),
0139          "CorrelationDomestic": (load_factor("loss3_corr_domestic"), 0.20),
0140          "BreadthDomestic": (load_factor("loss3_breadth_domestic"), 0.20),
0141          "TailShapeDomestic": (load_factor("loss3_tail_domestic"), 0.10),
0142      }
0143      neutral = next(iter(factors.values()))[0]
0144      baseline = run_factor_blend(
0145          returns, signals, defensive, neutral, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.0)
0146      )
0147      vol_target = run_vol_target(returns, signals, defensive, target_vol=0.15)
0148      standard: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {
0149          "FinalBlend": baseline,
0150          "VolTarget15": vol_target,
0151      }
0152      for name, (factor, shift) in factors.items():
0153          standard[name] = run_factor_blend(
0154              returns, signals, defensive, factor, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=shift)
0155          )
0156      combined = run_factor_vol_target(
0157          returns,
0158          signals,
0159          defensive,
0160          factors["CompositeDomestic"][0],
0161          max_shift=0.15,
0162          target_vol=0.15,
0163      )
0164      standard["CompositePlusVol15"] = combined
0165      proxy_combined = run_factor_vol_target(
0166          returns,
0167          signals,
0168          defensive,
0169          factors["CompositePlusIndexVolume"][0],
0170          max_shift=0.20,
0171          target_vol=0.15,
0172      )
0173      standard["CompositeIndexVolumePlusVol15"] = proxy_combined
0174  
0175      rows: list[dict[str, float | str]] = []
0176      for label, start, end in (
0177          ("calibration_2007_2017", "2007-01", "2017-12"),
0178          ("locked_2018_2026", "2018-01", "2026-12"),
0179          ("full_2007_2026", "2007-01", "2026-12"),
0180          ("subperiod_2007_2012", "2007-01", "2012-12"),
0181          ("subperiod_2013_2017", "2013-01", "2017-12"),
0182          ("subperiod_2018_2021", "2018-01", "2021-12"),
0183          ("subperiod_2022_2026", "2022-01", "2026-12"),
0184      ):
0185          for name, backtest in standard.items():
0186              rows.append(record(label, name, backtest.loc[start:end]))
0187  
0188      for cost in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 3.0):
0189          baseline_cost = run_factor_blend(
0190              returns,
0191              signals,
0192              defensive,
0193              neutral,
0194              FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.0),
0195              cost_multiplier=cost,
0196          )
0197          composite_cost = run_factor_blend(
0198              returns,
0199              signals,
0200              defensive,
0201              factors["CompositeDomestic"][0],
0202              FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.15),
0203              cost_multiplier=cost,
0204          )
0205          index_volume_raw_cost = run_factor_blend(
0206              returns,
0207              signals,
0208              defensive,
0209              factors["IndexVolumeRaw"][0],
0210              FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.20),
0211              cost_multiplier=cost,
0212          )
0213          index_volume_composite_cost = run_factor_blend(
0214              returns,
0215              signals,
0216              defensive,
0217              factors["IndexVolumeComposite"][0],
0218              FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.20),
0219              cost_multiplier=cost,
0220          )
0221          composite_index_volume_cost = run_factor_blend(
0222              returns,
0223              signals,
0224              defensive,
0225              factors["CompositePlusIndexVolume"][0],
0226              FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.20),
0227              cost_multiplier=cost,
0228          )
0229          combined_cost = run_factor_vol_target(
0230              returns,
0231              signals,
0232              defensive,
0233              factors["CompositeDomestic"][0],
0234              max_shift=0.15,
0235              target_vol=0.15,
0236              cost_multiplier=cost,
0237          )
0238          proxy_combined_cost = run_factor_vol_target(
0239              returns,
0240              signals,
0241              defensive,
0242              factors["CompositePlusIndexVolume"][0],
0243              max_shift=0.20,
0244              target_vol=0.15,
0245              cost_multiplier=cost,
0246          )
0247          for name, backtest in (
0248              ("FinalBlend", baseline_cost),
0249              ("CompositeDomestic", composite_cost),
0250              ("IndexVolumeRaw", index_volume_raw_cost),
0251              ("IndexVolumeComposite", index_volume_composite_cost),
0252              ("CompositePlusIndexVolume", composite_index_volume_cost),
0253              ("CompositePlusVol15", combined_cost),
0254              ("CompositeIndexVolumePlusVol15", proxy_combined_cost),
0255          ):
0256              rows.append(record(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_full", name, backtest.loc["2007-01":]))
0257              rows.append(record(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_locked", name, backtest.loc["2018-01":]))
0258  
0259      # Locked-period shift sensitivity is diagnostic only, never used for selection.
0260      for name in (
0261          "CompositeDomestic",
0262          "VolumeDomestic",
0263          "IndexVolumeRaw",
0264          "IndexVolumeComposite",
0265          "CompositePlusIndexVolume",
0266      ):
0267          factor = factors[name][0]
0268          for shift in (0.025, 0.05, 0.075, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20):
0269              backtest = run_factor_blend(
0270                  returns, signals, defensive, factor, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=shift)
0271              )
0272              rows.append(record(f"locked_shift_{shift:.3f}", name, backtest.loc["2018-01":]))
0273  
0274      output = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0275      output.to_csv(RESULTS / "market_structure_robustness.csv", index=False)
0276      baseline_locked = baseline.loc["2018-01":, "return"]
0277      bootstrap = {
0278          "CompositeDomestic": paired_block_bootstrap(
0279              baseline_locked, standard["CompositeDomestic"].loc["2018-01":, "return"]
0280          ),
0281          "CompositePlusVol15": paired_block_bootstrap(
0282              baseline_locked, combined.loc["2018-01":, "return"]
0283          ),
0284          "IndexVolumeRaw": paired_block_bootstrap(
0285              baseline_locked, standard["IndexVolumeRaw"].loc["2018-01":, "return"]
0286          ),
0287          "IndexVolumeComposite": paired_block_bootstrap(
0288              baseline_locked,
0289              standard["IndexVolumeComposite"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0290          ),
0291          "CompositePlusIndexVolume": paired_block_bootstrap(
0292              baseline_locked,
0293              standard["CompositePlusIndexVolume"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0294          ),
0295          "CompositeIndexVolumePlusVol15": paired_block_bootstrap(
0296              baseline_locked, proxy_combined.loc["2018-01":, "return"]
0297          ),
0298          "IndexVolumeRaw_vs_VolumeDomestic": paired_block_bootstrap(
0299              standard["VolumeDomestic"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0300              standard["IndexVolumeRaw"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0301          ),
0302          "CompositePlusIndexVolume_vs_CompositeDomestic": paired_block_bootstrap(
0303              standard["CompositeDomestic"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0304              standard["CompositePlusIndexVolume"].loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0305          ),
0306          "CompositeIndexVolumePlusVol15_vs_CompositePlusVol15": paired_block_bootstrap(
0307              combined.loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0308              proxy_combined.loc["2018-01":, "return"],
0309          ),
0310      }
0311      (RESULTS / "market_structure_robustness.json").write_text(
0312          json.dumps(bootstrap, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0313      )
0314      print(
0315          output.to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0316      )
0317      print("\n=== BOOTSTRAP ===")
0318      print(json.dumps(bootstrap, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
