0113  def load_vkospi_daily(path: Path = VKOSPI_PATH) -> pd.DataFrame:
0114      """Load the KRX export by position so Korean header encoding is irrelevant."""
0115      raw = pd.read_csv(path, encoding="utf-8-sig")
0116      if raw.shape[1] < 7:
0117          raise ValueError(f"VKOSPI file must contain seven columns: {path}")
0118      daily = raw.iloc[:, :7].copy()
0119      daily.columns = ["date", "close", "change", "return_pct", "open", "high", "low"]
0120      daily["date"] = pd.to_datetime(daily["date"], format="%Y/%m/%d", errors="coerce")
0121      for column in daily.columns[1:]:
0122          daily[column] = pd.to_numeric(
0123              daily[column].astype(str).str.replace(",", "", regex=False), errors="coerce"
0124          )
0125      daily = daily.dropna(subset=["date", "close"]).set_index("date").sort_index()
0126      daily = daily[~daily.index.duplicated(keep="last")]
0127      if daily.empty or not daily.index.is_monotonic_increasing:
0128          raise ValueError("VKOSPI data is empty or not chronologically sortable")
0129      return daily
0130  
0131  
0132  def _prior_zscore(values: pd.Series, window: int, minimum: int) -> float:
0133      values = values.dropna()
0134      if len(values) < minimum + 1:
0135          return np.nan
0136      current = float(values.iloc[-1])
0137      reference = values.iloc[:-1].tail(window)
0138      if len(reference) < minimum:
0139          return np.nan
0140      scale = float(reference.std(ddof=1))
0141      return (current - float(reference.mean())) / scale if scale > 0 else np.nan
0142  
0143  
0144  def _lagged_return(close: pd.Series, recent_lag: int, old_lag: int) -> float:
0145      """Match the SignalDoc convention: price(t-recent)/price(t-old)-1."""
0146      if len(close) <= old_lag:
0147          return np.nan
0148      recent = float(close.iloc[-recent_lag - 1]) if recent_lag else float(close.iloc[-1])
0149      old = float(close.iloc[-old_lag - 1])
0150      return recent / old - 1 if old != 0 else np.nan
0151  
0152  
0153  def load_kodex200_close() -> pd.Series:
0154      market = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "cache" / "market_daily.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
0155      close = market.loc[market["symbol"] == "KODEX200", ["date", "close"]].copy()
0156      close["close"] = pd.to_numeric(close["close"], errors="coerce")
0157      close = close.dropna().drop_duplicates("date", keep="last").set_index("date")["close"]
0158      return close.sort_index().rename("KODEX200")
0159  
0160  
0161  def build_vkospi_features(
0162      target_months: pd.PeriodIndex,
0163      daily: pd.DataFrame | None = None,
0164      kospi_close: pd.Series | None = None,
0165  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0166      """Build features known before each target month with a two-day safety buffer."""
0167      daily = load_vkospi_daily() if daily is None else daily.copy()
0168      kospi_close = load_kodex200_close() if kospi_close is None else kospi_close.copy()
0169      aligned_kospi = kospi_close.reindex(daily.index).ffill(limit=3)
0170      kospi_return = aligned_kospi.pct_change(fill_method=None)
0171      daily_return = daily["close"].pct_change(fill_method=None)
0172      previous_close = daily["close"].shift(1)
0173      intraday_range = (daily["high"] - daily["low"]).div(previous_close)
0174      close_location = (daily["close"] - daily["low"]).div(
0175          (daily["high"] - daily["low"]).replace(0, np.nan)
0176      )
0177      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period | pd.Timestamp]] = []
0178  
0179      for month in target_months:
0180          cutoff = month.to_timestamp(how="start") - pd.Timedelta(days=2)
0181          history = daily.loc[:cutoff]
0182          if len(history) < 63:
0183              continue
0184          last = history.iloc[-1]
0185          close = history["close"]
0186          returns = daily_return.loc[history.index]
0187          signal_month = month - 1
0188          month_view = history.loc[history.index.to_period("M") == signal_month]
0189          if month_view.empty:
0190              month_view = history.tail(21)
0191  
0192          row: dict[str, float | pd.Period | pd.Timestamp] = {
0193              "month": month,
0194              "vkospi_signal_date": history.index[-1],
0195              "vkospi_raw_close": float(last["close"]),
0196              "vkospi_raw_change": float(last["change"]),
0197              "vkospi_raw_return_pct": float(last["return_pct"]),
0198              "vkospi_raw_open": float(last["open"]),
0199              "vkospi_raw_high": float(last["high"]),
0200              "vkospi_raw_low": float(last["low"]),
0201          }
0202          for window in (5, 21, 63):
0203              row[f"vkospi_return_{window}"] = (
0204                  float(close.iloc[-1] / close.iloc[-window - 1] - 1)
0205                  if len(close) > window
0206                  else np.nan
0207              )
0208          for window in (5, 21):
0209              mean = float(close.tail(window).mean())
0210              row[f"vkospi_mean_ratio_{window}"] = float(close.iloc[-1] / mean - 1)
0211          row["vkospi_level_z_63"] = _prior_zscore(close, 63, 42)
0212          row["vkospi_level_z_252"] = _prior_zscore(close, 252, 126)
0213          recent_return = returns.tail(21).dropna()
0214          row["vkospi_return_vol_21"] = (
0215              float(recent_return.std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(252))
0216              if len(recent_return) >= 10
0217              else np.nan
0218          )
0219          row["vkospi_positive_fraction_21"] = (
0220              float((recent_return > 0).mean()) if len(recent_return) >= 10 else np.nan
0221          )
0222          row["vkospi_intraday_range_21"] = float(
0223              intraday_range.loc[history.index].tail(21).mean()
0224          )
0225          row["vkospi_close_location_21"] = float(
0226              close_location.loc[history.index].tail(21).mean()
0227          )
0228          row["vkospi_month_mean"] = float(month_view["close"].mean())
0229          row["vkospi_month_max"] = float(month_view["high"].max())
0230          row["vkospi_month_min"] = float(month_view["low"].min())
0231          row["vkospi_month_range"] = float(
0232              (month_view["high"].max() - month_view["low"].min())
0233              / month_view["close"].mean()
0234          )
0235          row["vkospi_month_return"] = float(
0236              month_view["close"].iloc[-1] / month_view["close"].iloc[0] - 1
0237          )
0238  
0239          # Open Asset Pricing SignalDoc analogues. These preserve the documented
0240          # lookback/skip-month definitions, translated from a stock cross-section
0241          # to the domestic implied-volatility series. Positive VKOSPI momentum
0242          # means rising fear, not positive equity-price momentum; the model learns
0243          # that direction and the direct factor below fixes it as higher risk.
0244          row["vkospi_oap_mom12m"] = _lagged_return(close, 21, 252)
0245          row["vkospi_oap_mom6m"] = _lagged_return(close, 21, 126)
0246          row["vkospi_oap_intmom"] = _lagged_return(close, 126, 252)
0247          row["vkospi_oap_streversal"] = _lagged_return(close, 0, 21)
0248          row["vkospi_oap_mrreversal"] = _lagged_return(close, 252, 378)
0249          row["vkospi_oap_lrreversal"] = _lagged_return(close, 252, 756)
0250          row["vkospi_oap_high52"] = float(close.iloc[-1] / close.tail(252).max())
0251          row["vkospi_oap_realized_vol_21"] = row["vkospi_return_vol_21"]
0252          row["vkospi_oap_max_return_21"] = (
0253              float(recent_return.max()) if len(recent_return) >= 10 else np.nan
0254          )
0255          row["vkospi_oap_return_skew_21"] = (
0256              float(recent_return.skew()) if len(recent_return) >= 10 else np.nan
0257          )
0258  
0259          # betaVIX analogue: sensitivity of KODEX200 daily returns to changes in
0260          # domestic implied volatility. IdioVol3F analogue: residual volatility
0261          # from the same domestic systematic-volatility regression.
0262          regression = pd.concat(
0263              [
0264                  kospi_return.loc[history.index].rename("kospi_return"),
0265                  daily["close"].diff().loc[history.index].rename("vkospi_change"),
0266              ],
0267              axis=1,
0268          ).tail(21).dropna()
0269          if len(regression) >= 15 and float(regression["vkospi_change"].var(ddof=1)) > 0:
0270              x = regression["vkospi_change"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0271              y = regression["kospi_return"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0272              design = np.column_stack([np.ones(len(x)), x])
0273              coefficients = np.linalg.lstsq(design, y, rcond=None)[0]
0274              residual = y - design @ coefficients
0275              row["vkospi_oap_beta_21"] = float(coefficients[1])
0276              row["vkospi_oap_residual_vol_21"] = float(
0277                  np.std(residual, ddof=1) * math.sqrt(252)
0278              )
0279          else:
0280              row["vkospi_oap_beta_21"] = np.nan
0281              row["vkospi_oap_residual_vol_21"] = np.nan
0282          rows.append(row)
0283  
0284      output = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")
0285      output.index = pd.PeriodIndex(output.index, freq="M")
0286      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0287  
0288  
0289  def build_vkospi_composites(data: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
0290      """Economically signed, expanding-safe monthly VKOSPI stress composites."""
0291      z_columns = [
0292          "vkospi_raw_close",
0293          "vkospi_month_mean",
0294          "vkospi_month_max",
0295          "vkospi_return_5",
0296          "vkospi_return_21",
0297          "vkospi_month_return",
0298          "vkospi_return_vol_21",
0299          "vkospi_intraday_range_21",
0300          "vkospi_month_range",
0301          "vkospi_vix_ratio",
0302      ]
0303      z = data[z_columns].apply(causal_zscore)
0304      output = pd.DataFrame(index=data.index)
0305      output["vkospi_level_stress"] = z[
0306          ["vkospi_raw_close", "vkospi_month_mean", "vkospi_month_max"]
0307      ].mean(axis=1)
0308      output["vkospi_momentum_stress"] = z[
0309          ["vkospi_return_5", "vkospi_return_21", "vkospi_month_return"]
0310      ].mean(axis=1)
0311      output["vkospi_range_stress"] = z[
0312          ["vkospi_return_vol_21", "vkospi_intraday_range_21", "vkospi_month_range"]
0313      ].mean(axis=1)
0314      output["vkospi_relative_vix_stress"] = z["vkospi_vix_ratio"]
0315      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0316  
0317  
0318  def direct_level_factor(data: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
0319      """A model-free VKOSPI risk factor using only the causal monthly z-score."""
0320      percentile = data["vkospi_level_stress"].expanding(min_periods=24).rank(pct=True)
0321      severity = ((percentile - 0.80) / 0.20).clip(0, 1)
0322      output = pd.DataFrame(index=data.index)
0323      output["risk_percentile"] = percentile
0324      output["risk_severity"] = severity
0325      output["p_up"] = 0.5 - 0.15 * severity
0326      output["score"] = -severity
0327      output["factor_name"] = "vkospi_direct_level"
0328      return output
0329  
0330  
0331  def metric_record(period: str, strategy: str, path: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, object]:
0332      metrics = performance_summary(path["return"])
0333      return {
0334          "Period": period,
0335          "Strategy": strategy,
0336          "Months": len(path),
0337          **metrics.to_dict(),
0338          "AvgTurnover": float(path["turnover"].mean()),
0339      }
0340  
0341  
0342  def univariate_audit(data: pd.DataFrame, columns: list[str], target: str) -> pd.DataFrame:
0343      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0344      for period, start, end in (
0345          ("calibration", data.index.min(), CAL_END),
0346          ("locked", TEST_START, data.index.max()),
0347      ):
0348          for column in columns:
0349              view = data.loc[start:end, [target, column]].dropna()
0350              if view.empty or view[target].nunique() < 2 or view[column].nunique() < 2:
0351                  continue
0352              auc = float(roc_auc_score(view[target].astype(int), view[column]))
0353              rows.append(
0354                  {
0355                      "Period": period,
0356                      "Target": target,
0357                      "Feature": column,
0358                      "AUC": auc,
0359                      "DirectionalAUC": max(auc, 1 - auc),
0360                      "HighMeansRisk": auc >= 0.5,
0361                      "Observations": len(view),
0362                      "Events": int(view[target].sum()),
0363                  }
0364              )
0365      return pd.DataFrame(rows)
0366  
0367  
0368  def _add_candidate_rows(
0369      rows: list[dict[str, object]],
0370      candidates: dict[str, tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]],
0371      name: str,
0372      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0373      path: pd.DataFrame,
0374      **parameters: object,
0375  ) -> None:
0376      candidates[name] = (factor, path)
0377      metrics = performance_summary(path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0378      rows.append(
0379          {
0380              "Strategy": name,
0381              **parameters,
0382              **metrics.to_dict(),
0383              "AvgTurnover": float(path.loc[:CAL_END, "turnover"].mean()),
0384          }
0385      )
0386  
0387  
0388  def main() -> None:
0389      base_data = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0390      base_data.index = pd.PeriodIndex(base_data.index, freq="M")
0391      vkospi = build_vkospi_features(base_data.index)
0392      if (vkospi["vkospi_signal_date"].dt.to_period("M") >= vkospi.index).any():
0393          raise AssertionError("VKOSPI feature timing leaked into the target month")
0394      structure = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "market_structure_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0395      structure.index = pd.PeriodIndex(structure.index, freq="M")
0396      data = base_data.join(vkospi, how="left").join(structure, how="left")
0397      data["vkospi_vix_ratio"] = data["vkospi_raw_close"].div(
0398          data["VIX_last"].replace(0, np.nan)
0399      )
0400      composites = build_vkospi_composites(data)
0401      data = data.join(composites, how="left")
0402  
0403      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
0404      asset_returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns()
0405      signals = compute_regime_signals(macro, asset_returns)
0406      defensive = run_backtest(asset_returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
0407      neutral = pd.DataFrame({"p_up": 0.5}, index=data.index)
0408      final_blend = run_factor_blend(
0409          asset_returns, signals, defensive, neutral, FactorBlendConfig(max_shift=0.0)
0410      )
0411      data = data.loc[data.index.intersection(final_blend.index)].copy()
0412      baseline_return = final_blend.loc[data.index, "return"]
0413      data["final_loss3"] = (baseline_return < -0.03).astype(float)
0414      data["final_loss4"] = (baseline_return < -0.04).astype(float)
0415      for name, threshold in (("path_loss_2m_4", -0.04), ("path_loss_2m_5", -0.05)):
0416          path_loss = forward_path_loss(baseline_return, 2)
0417          data[name] = (path_loss < threshold).where(path_loss.notna()).astype(float)
0418  
0419      model_specs: dict[str, tuple[str, int, list[str]]] = {
0420          "loss3_domestic_raw": ("final_loss3", 1, DOMESTIC_FEATURES + RAW_FEATURES),
0421          "loss3_domestic_derived": (
0422              "final_loss3",
0423              1,
0424              DOMESTIC_FEATURES + DERIVED_FEATURES + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0425          ),
0426          "loss3_domestic_all": (
0427              "final_loss3",
0428              1,
0429              DOMESTIC_FEATURES
0430              + RAW_FEATURES
0431              + DERIVED_FEATURES
0432              + OAP_FEATURES
0433              + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0434          ),
0435          "loss3_domestic_oap": (
0436              "final_loss3",
0437              1,
0438              DOMESTIC_FEATURES + OAP_FEATURES,
0439          ),
0440          "loss3_structure_all": (
0441              "final_loss3",
0442              1,
0443              DOMESTIC_FEATURES
0444              + STRUCTURE_FEATURES
0445              + RAW_FEATURES
0446              + DERIVED_FEATURES
0447              + OAP_FEATURES
0448              + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0449          ),
0450          "loss4_structure_all": (
0451              "final_loss4",
0452              1,
0453              DOMESTIC_FEATURES
0454              + STRUCTURE_FEATURES
0455              + RAW_FEATURES
0456              + DERIVED_FEATURES
0457              + OAP_FEATURES
0458              + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0459          ),
0460          "path2m4_structure_all": (
0461              "path_loss_2m_4",
0462              2,
0463              DOMESTIC_FEATURES
0464              + STRUCTURE_FEATURES
0465              + RAW_FEATURES
0466              + DERIVED_FEATURES
0467              + OAP_FEATURES
0468              + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0469          ),
0470          "path2m5_structure_all": (
0471              "path_loss_2m_5",
0472              2,
0473              DOMESTIC_FEATURES
0474              + STRUCTURE_FEATURES
0475              + RAW_FEATURES
0476              + DERIVED_FEATURES
0477              + OAP_FEATURES
0478              + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0479          ),
0480      }
0481  
0482      factors: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {"direct_level": direct_level_factor(data)}
0483      prediction_report: dict[str, object] = {}
0484      for name, (target, embargo, columns) in model_specs.items():
0485          if embargo == 1:
0486              probability = walk_forward_probability(data, target, columns)
0487          else:
0488              probability = walk_forward_probability_embargo(
0489                  data, target, columns, embargo_months=embargo
0490              )
0491          factor = make_factor(probability, data[target], name)
0492          factors[name] = factor
0493          factor.to_csv(RESULTS / f"vkospi_{name}_factor.csv")
0494          prediction_report[name] = {
0495              "target": target,
0496              "embargo_months": embargo,
0497              "feature_count": len(columns),
0498              "calibration": predictive_metrics(factor.loc[:CAL_END]),
0499              "locked": predictive_metrics(factor, TEST_START),
0500          }
0501  
0502      reference_factor = pd.read_csv(
0503          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0504      )
0505      reference_factor.index = pd.PeriodIndex(reference_factor.index, freq="M")
0506      reference_path = run_factor_vol_target(
0507          asset_returns,
0508          signals,
0509          defensive,
0510          reference_factor,
0511          max_shift=0.20,
0512          target_vol=0.15,
0513      )
0514      reference_metrics = performance_summary(reference_path.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0515  
0516      calibration_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0517      candidates: dict[str, tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]] = {}
0518      _add_candidate_rows(
0519          calibration_rows,
0520          candidates,
0521          "ReferenceMediumHorizonOAPVol15",
0522          reference_factor,
0523          reference_path,
0524          source="reference",
0525          vkospi_weight=0.0,
0526          max_shift=0.20,
0527          target_vol=0.15,
0528      )
0529      for factor_name, factor in factors.items():
0530          for shift in (0.10, 0.15, 0.20, 0.25):
0531              for target_vol in (0.145, 0.15, 0.155):
0532                  name = f"VKOSPI_{factor_name}_s{shift:.3f}_v{target_vol:.3f}"
0533                  path = run_factor_vol_target(
0534                      asset_returns,
0535                      signals,
0536                      defensive,
0537                      factor,
0538                      max_shift=shift,
0539                      target_vol=target_vol,
0540                  )
0541                  _add_candidate_rows(
0542                      calibration_rows,
0543                      candidates,
0544                      name,
0545                      factor,
0546                      path,
0547                      source=factor_name,
0548                      vkospi_weight=1.0,
0549                      max_shift=shift,
0550                      target_vol=target_vol,
0551                  )
0552  
0553          for weight in (0.25, 0.50, 0.75):
0554              blended = blend_risk_factors(reference_factor, factor, weight)
0555              for shift in (0.15, 0.20, 0.25):
0556                  for target_vol in (0.145, 0.15, 0.155):
0557                      name = (
0558                          f"Blend_{factor_name}_w{weight:.2f}_s{shift:.3f}_v{target_vol:.3f}"
0559                      )
0560                      path = run_factor_vol_target(
0561                          asset_returns,
0562                          signals,
0563                          defensive,
0564                          blended,
0565                          max_shift=shift,
0566                          target_vol=target_vol,
0567                      )
0568                      _add_candidate_rows(
0569                          calibration_rows,
0570                          candidates,
0571                          name,
0572                          blended,
0573                          path,
0574                          source=factor_name,
0575                          vkospi_weight=weight,
0576                          max_shift=shift,
0577                          target_vol=target_vol,
0578                      )
0579  
0580      calibration = pd.DataFrame(calibration_rows)
0581      eligible = calibration[
0582          (calibration["CAGR"] >= float(reference_metrics["CAGR"]) - 1e-12)
0583          & (calibration["Sharpe"] >= float(reference_metrics["Sharpe"]) - 1e-12)
0584          & (calibration["MDD"] >= float(reference_metrics["MDD"]) - 1e-12)
0585      ].copy()
0586      for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD"):
0587          eligible[f"{metric}Rank"] = eligible[metric].rank(pct=True, method="average")
0588      eligible["MultiObjectiveScore"] = eligible[
0589          ["CAGRRank", "SharpeRank", "MDDRank"]
0590      ].mean(axis=1)
0591      winner_row = eligible.sort_values(
0592          ["MultiObjectiveScore", "Calmar", "Sharpe", "CAGR"], ascending=False
0593      ).iloc[0]
0594      winner_name = str(winner_row["Strategy"])
0595      winner_factor, winner_path = candidates[winner_name]
0596  
0597      reference_locked = reference_path.loc[TEST_START:]
0598      winner_locked = winner_path.loc[TEST_START:]
0599      reference_locked_metrics = performance_summary(reference_locked["return"])
0600      winner_locked_metrics = performance_summary(winner_locked["return"])
0601      locked_deltas = {
0602          metric: float(winner_locked_metrics[metric] - reference_locked_metrics[metric])
0603          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0604      }
0605      # The locked period is an acceptance gate, never a way to choose another
0606      # candidate. A failed candidate leaves the previously validated reference
0607      # untouched, preventing a research improvement from degrading production.
0608      improves_all_three = bool(
0609          locked_deltas["CAGR"] >= 0
0610          and locked_deltas["Sharpe"] >= 0
0611          and locked_deltas["MDD"] >= 0
0612          and (
0613              locked_deltas["CAGR"] > 0
0614              or locked_deltas["Sharpe"] > 0
0615              or locked_deltas["MDD"] > 0
0616          )
0617      )
0618      deployed_name = winner_name if improves_all_three else "ReferenceMediumHorizonOAPVol15"
0619      deployed_factor = winner_factor if improves_all_three else reference_factor
0620      deployed_path = winner_path if improves_all_three else reference_path
0621  
0622      comparison_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0623      selected_paths = {
0624          "FinalBlend": final_blend,
0625          "ReferenceMediumHorizonOAPVol15": reference_path,
0626          "VKOSPICalibrationCandidate": winner_path,
0627          "DeployedStrategy": deployed_path,
0628      }
0629      for period, start, end in (
0630          ("calibration_2007_2017", final_blend.index.min(), CAL_END),
0631          ("locked_2018_2026", TEST_START, final_blend.index.max()),
0632          ("full_2007_2026", final_blend.index.min(), final_blend.index.max()),
0633      ):
0634          for strategy, path in selected_paths.items():
0635              comparison_rows.append(metric_record(period, strategy, path.loc[start:end]))
0636      comparison = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
0637  
0638      audit = univariate_audit(
0639          data,
0640          RAW_FEATURES + DERIVED_FEATURES + OAP_FEATURES + COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0641          "final_loss3",
0642      )
0643  
0644      vkospi.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_features.csv")
0645      composites.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_composites.csv")
0646      audit.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_univariate_audit.csv", index=False)
0647      calibration.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_calibration.csv", index=False)
0648      comparison.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_comparison.csv", index=False)
0649      winner_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_candidate_factor.csv")
0650      winner_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_candidate_backtest.csv")
0651      deployed_factor.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_selected_factor.csv")
0652      deployed_path.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_selected_backtest.csv")
0653  
0654      report = {
0655          "data": {
0656              "path": str(VKOSPI_PATH),
0657              "interpretation": VKOSPI_INTERPRETATION,
0658              "daily_start": str(load_vkospi_daily().index.min().date()),
0659              "daily_end": str(load_vkospi_daily().index.max().date()),
0660              "daily_rows": len(load_vkospi_daily()),
0661              "raw_features": RAW_FEATURES,
0662              "derived_features": DERIVED_FEATURES,
0663              "open_asset_pricing_source": OAP_SOURCE,
0664              "open_asset_pricing_signal_map": OAP_SIGNAL_MAP,
0665              "open_asset_pricing_analog_features": OAP_FEATURES,
0666              "composite_features": COMPOSITE_FEATURES,
0667              "timing": "target-month start minus two calendar days; no target-month observation",
0668          },
0669          "selection": {
0670              "calibration_end": str(CAL_END),
0671              "locked_start": str(TEST_START),
0672              "rule": "CAGR, Sharpe, and MDD must all match or beat the reference; equal-weight percentile rank selects among eligible candidates",
0673              "reference": "ReferenceMediumHorizonOAPVol15",
0674              "calibration_candidate": winner_name,
0675              "candidate_parameters": {
0676                  key: (float(winner_row[key]) if key != "source" else str(winner_row[key]))
0677                  for key in ("source", "vkospi_weight", "max_shift", "target_vol")
0678              },
0679              "eligible_candidates": len(eligible),
0680              "total_candidates": len(calibration),
0681              "locked_acceptance_rule": "candidate must match or improve CAGR, Sharpe, and MDD, with at least one strict improvement",
0682              "candidate_promoted": improves_all_three,
0683              "deployed_strategy": deployed_name,
0684          },
0685          "locked_test": {
0686              "reference": reference_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0687              "calibration_candidate": winner_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0688              "candidate_deltas": locked_deltas,
0689              "improves_all_three": improves_all_three,
0690              "deployed": performance_summary(deployed_path.loc[TEST_START:, "return"]).to_dict(),
0691              "bootstrap": paired_block_bootstrap(
0692                  reference_locked["return"], winner_locked["return"]
0693              ),
0694          },
0695          "prediction": prediction_report,
0696          "notes": [
0697              "All model, blend, shift, and volatility-target selection uses data through 2017 only.",
0698              "The 2018-2026 locked period never chooses a candidate or hyperparameters; it is only a one-way promotion gate.",
0699              "A locked-test failure automatically retains the prior validated reference strategy.",
0700              "Raw KRX fields and engineered VKOSPI fields are tested separately before being combined.",
0701              "Open Asset Pricing price/risk signals are time-series analogues, not exact stock-level replications.",
0702              "Open Asset Pricing volume/liquidity signals are excluded because the VKOSPI export has no volume field.",
0703              "Historical simulation does not guarantee future performance.",
0704          ],
0705      }
0706      (RESULTS / "vkospi_validation.json").write_text(
0707          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0708      )
0709  
0710      print("\n=== VKOSPI COVERAGE ===")
0711      print(vkospi.notna().mean().sort_values().to_string(float_format=lambda x: f"{x:.3f}"))
0712      print("\n=== CALIBRATION CANDIDATE ===")
0713      print(winner_row.to_string())
0714      print("\n=== COMPARISON ===")
0715      print(
0716          comparison[
0717              ["Period", "Strategy", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]
0718          ].to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda x: f"{x:.4f}")
0719      )
0720      print("\n=== LOCKED DELTAS VS REFERENCE ===")
0721      print(json.dumps(locked_deltas, indent=2))
0722      print(f"\nPROMOTED={improves_all_three} DEPLOYED={deployed_name}")
0723

0726      main()

0025  def get_path(directory: Path, filename: str) -> Path:
0026      """Resolve Korean filenames robustly across NFC/NFD filesystems."""
0027      target = unicodedata.normalize("NFC", filename)
0028      for path in directory.iterdir():
0029          if unicodedata.normalize("NFC", path.name) == target:
0030              return path
0031      raise FileNotFoundError(filename)

0033  
0034  def performance_summary(returns: pd.Series) -> pd.Series:
0035      """Return the monthly performance metrics used by the reference strategy."""
0036      r = pd.Series(returns).dropna()
0037      wealth = (1 + r).cumprod()
0038      years = len(r) / 12
0039      cagr = wealth.iloc[-1] ** (1 / years) - 1 if years > 0 else np.nan
0040      std = r.std(ddof=1)
0041      volatility = std * math.sqrt(12)
0042      sharpe = r.mean() / std * math.sqrt(12) if std > 0 else np.nan
0043      drawdown = wealth / wealth.cummax() - 1
0044      mdd = drawdown.min()
0045      calmar = cagr / abs(mdd) if mdd < 0 else np.nan
0046      downside = np.sqrt(np.mean(np.minimum(r, 0) ** 2)) * math.sqrt(12)
0047      sortino = r.mean() * 12 / downside if downside > 0 else np.nan
0048      return pd.Series(
0049          {
0050              "Months": len(r),
0051              "CAGR": cagr,
0052              "Volatility": volatility,
0053              "Sharpe": sharpe,
0054              "Sortino": sortino,
0055              "MDD": mdd,
0056              "Calmar": calmar,
0057              "FinalMultiple": wealth.iloc[-1],
0058              "PositiveMonths": (r > 0).mean(),
0059          }
0060      )

0063  @dataclass(frozen=True)
0064  class DynamicRiskConfig:
0065      mode: str
0066      level_threshold: float
0067      momentum_window: int
0068      spike_threshold: float
0069      max_risk_transfer: float
0070      bond_share: float = 0.50
0071      rebalance_band: float = 0.05
0072      financing_rate: float = 0.04
0073  
0074      @property
0075      def name(self) -> str:
0076          return (
0077              f"{self.mode}_lt{self.level_threshold:.2f}_mw{self.momentum_window}"
0078              f"_st{self.spike_threshold:.2f}_rt{self.max_risk_transfer:.2f}"
0079              f"_bs{self.bond_share:.2f}_rb{self.rebalance_band:.2f}"
0080          )

0083  def load_vkospi_daily(path: Path = VKOSPI_PATH) -> pd.DataFrame:
0084      raw = pd.read_csv(path, encoding="utf-8-sig")
0085      if raw.shape[1] < 7:
0086          raise ValueError(f"VKOSPI file must contain seven columns: {path}")
0087      daily = raw.iloc[:, :7].copy()
0088      daily.columns = ["date", "close", "change", "return_pct", "open", "high", "low"]
0089      daily["date"] = pd.to_datetime(
0090          daily["date"], format="%Y/%m/%d", errors="coerce"
0091      )
0092      for column in daily.columns[1:]:
0093          daily[column] = pd.to_numeric(
0094              daily[column].astype(str).str.replace(",", "", regex=False),
0095              errors="coerce",
0096          )
0097      daily = daily.dropna(subset=["date", "close"]).set_index("date").sort_index()
0098      return daily[~daily.index.duplicated(keep="last")]

0101  def load_daily_open_levels() -> pd.DataFrame:
0102      market = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "cache" / "market_daily.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
0103      with sqlite3.connect(get_path(RAW_DIR, "compass.db")) as connection:
0104          proxy = pd.read_sql(
0105              "select date, open, close from etf_prices where symbol = ? order by date",
0106              connection,
0107              params=("1028",),
0108          )
0109      proxy["date"] = pd.to_datetime(proxy["date"])
0110      proxy[["open", "close"]] = proxy[["open", "close"]].apply(
0111          pd.to_numeric, errors="coerce"
0112      )
0113      actual = market.loc[
0114          (market["symbol"] == "KODEX200")
0115          & market["open"].notna()
0116          & (market["date"] > pd.Timestamp("2009-03-31"))
0117      ].copy()
0118      first_actual = actual["date"].min()
0119      actual_anchor = float(actual.loc[actual["date"] == first_actual, "open"].iloc[0])
0120      nearest = proxy.iloc[(proxy["date"] - first_actual).abs().argsort()[:1]]
0121      proxy["open"] *= actual_anchor / float(nearest["open"].iloc[0])
0122      proxy = proxy.loc[proxy["date"] < first_actual]
0123      kodex = pd.concat(
0124          [proxy[["date", "open"]], actual[["date", "open"]]], ignore_index=True
0125      )
0126      kodex = (
0127          kodex.sort_values("date")
0128          .drop_duplicates("date", keep="last")
0129          .set_index("date")["open"]
0130      )
0131  
0132      bond = pd.read_csv(get_path(RAW_DIR, "krx_bond_index.csv"), encoding="cp949")
0133      bond.index = pd.to_datetime(bond.iloc[:, 0])
0134      bond_level = pd.to_numeric(
0135          bond.iloc[:, 1].astype(str).str.replace(",", "", regex=False), errors="coerce"
0136      ).rename("BOND")
0137  
0138      pivot_open = market.pivot_table(
0139          index="date", columns="symbol", values="open", aggfunc="last"
0140      ).sort_index()
0141      pivot_close = market.pivot_table(
0142          index="date", columns="symbol", values="close", aggfunc="last"
0143      ).sort_index()
0144      fx = pivot_close["USDKRW"].reindex(
0145          pd.date_range(pivot_close.index.min(), pivot_close.index.max(), freq="D")
0146      ).ffill()
0147      gld = (pivot_open["GLD"] * fx.reindex(pivot_open.index).to_numpy()).rename("GLD")
0148      uso = (pivot_open["USO"] * fx.reindex(pivot_open.index).to_numpy()).rename("USO")
0149      levels = pd.concat(
0150          [kodex.rename("KODEX200"), bond_level, gld, uso], axis=1, sort=False
0151      ).sort_index()
0152      calendar = pd.date_range(levels.index.min(), levels.index.max(), freq="B")
0153      return levels.reindex(calendar).ffill(limit=5)[ASSETS]

0155  
0156  def causal_percentile(series: pd.Series, window: int = 252, minimum: int = 126) -> pd.Series:
0157      def last_rank(values: np.ndarray) -> float:
0158          finite = values[np.isfinite(values)]
0159          if len(finite) < minimum:
0160              return np.nan
0161          return float((np.sum(finite[:-1] <= finite[-1]) + 1) / len(finite))
0162  
0163      return series.rolling(window + 1, min_periods=minimum).apply(last_rank, raw=True)

0166  def build_daily_vkospi_signals() -> pd.DataFrame:
0167      daily = load_vkospi_daily()
0168      close = daily["close"]
0169      output = pd.DataFrame(index=daily.index)
0170      output["vkospi_close"] = close
0171      output["level_percentile"] = causal_percentile(close)
0172      for window in (5, 10, 21):
0173          output[f"momentum_{window}"] = close.pct_change(window, fill_method=None)
0174      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)

0177  def load_reference_weights() -> pd.DataFrame:
0178      reference = pd.read_csv(REFERENCE_PATH, index_col=0)
0179      reference.index = pd.PeriodIndex(reference.index, freq="M")
0180      required = [f"w_{asset}" for asset in ASSETS]
0181      missing = [column for column in required if column not in reference]
0182      if missing:
0183          raise ValueError(f"Reference strategy is missing weights: {missing}")
0184      return reference

0186  
0187  def prepare_arrays(
0188      levels: pd.DataFrame,
0189      reference: pd.DataFrame,
0190      vkospi_signals: pd.DataFrame,
0191  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0192      forward = levels.shift(-1).div(levels).sub(1.0)
0193      dates = levels.index[
0194          (levels.index.to_period("M") >= reference.index.min())
0195          & (levels.index.to_period("M") <= reference.index.max())
0196      ]
0197      valid_dates: list[pd.Timestamp] = []
0198      returns: list[np.ndarray] = []
0199      base_weights: list[np.ndarray] = []
0200      months: list[pd.Period] = []
0201      level_percentile: list[float] = []
0202      momentum = {window: [] for window in (5, 10, 21)}
0203      signal_dates: list[pd.Timestamp | pd.NaT] = []
0204      weight_columns = [f"w_{asset}" for asset in ASSETS]
0205  
0206      for date in dates:
0207          month = date.to_period("M")
0208          if month not in reference.index:
0209              continue
0210          asset_return = forward.loc[date, ASSETS].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0211          if not np.isfinite(asset_return).all():
0212              continue
0213          cutoff = date - pd.Timedelta(days=1)
0214          known = vkospi_signals.loc[:cutoff]
0215          signal = known.iloc[-1] if not known.empty else pd.Series(dtype=float)
0216          valid_dates.append(date)
0217          returns.append(asset_return)
0218          base_weights.append(reference.loc[month, weight_columns].to_numpy(dtype=float))
0219          months.append(month)
0220          signal_dates.append(known.index[-1] if not known.empty else pd.NaT)
0221          level_percentile.append(float(signal.get("level_percentile", np.nan)))
0222          for window in momentum:
0223              momentum[window].append(float(signal.get(f"momentum_{window}", np.nan)))
0224  
0225      return {
0226          "dates": pd.DatetimeIndex(valid_dates),
0227          "returns": np.asarray(returns),
0228          "base_weights": np.asarray(base_weights),
0229          "months": pd.PeriodIndex(months, freq="M"),
0230          "signal_dates": pd.DatetimeIndex(signal_dates),
0231          "level_percentile": np.asarray(level_percentile),
0232          "momentum": {window: np.asarray(values) for window, values in momentum.items()},
0233      }
0234  
0235  
0236  def _stress_series(arrays: dict[str, object], cfg: DynamicRiskConfig) -> np.ndarray:
0237      percentile = np.asarray(arrays["level_percentile"], dtype=float)
0238      momentum = np.asarray(arrays["momentum"][cfg.momentum_window], dtype=float)
0239      level = np.clip(
0240          (percentile - cfg.level_threshold) / max(1 - cfg.level_threshold, 1e-6), 0, 1
0241      )
0242      spike = np.clip(
0243          (momentum - cfg.spike_threshold) / 0.25,
0244          0,
0245          1,
0246      )
0247      level = np.nan_to_num(level, nan=0.0)
0248      spike = np.nan_to_num(spike, nan=0.0)
0249      if cfg.mode == "level":
0250          return level
0251      if cfg.mode == "momentum":
0252          return spike
0253      if cfg.mode == "max":
0254          return np.maximum(level, spike)
0255      if cfg.mode == "mean":
0256          return 0.5 * (level + spike)
0257      raise ValueError(f"Unknown stress mode: {cfg.mode}")
0258  
0259  
0260  def simulate(
0261      arrays: dict[str, object],
0262      cfg: DynamicRiskConfig | None,
0263      start: pd.Period | None = None,
0264      end: pd.Period | None = None,
0265      cost_multiplier: float = 1.0,
0266      keep_daily: bool = True,
0267      stress_override: np.ndarray | None = None,
0268  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0269      dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(arrays["dates"])
0270      months = pd.PeriodIndex(arrays["months"], freq="M")
0271      mask = np.ones(len(dates), dtype=bool)
0272      if start is not None:
0273          mask &= months >= start
0274      if end is not None:
0275          mask &= months <= end
0276      positions = np.flatnonzero(mask)
0277      if stress_override is not None:
0278          stress = np.asarray(stress_override, dtype=float)
0279          if stress.shape != (len(dates),):
0280              raise ValueError(
0281                  f"stress_override must have shape {(len(dates),)}, got {stress.shape}"
0282              )
0283          stress = np.nan_to_num(stress, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0).clip(0, 1)
0284      else:
0285          stress = np.zeros(len(dates)) if cfg is None else _stress_series(arrays, cfg)
0286      returns = np.asarray(arrays["returns"], dtype=float)
0287      base_weights = np.asarray(arrays["base_weights"], dtype=float)
0288      signal_dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(arrays["signal_dates"])
0289  
0290      pretrade = np.zeros(len(ASSETS))
0291      previous_month: pd.Period | None = None
0292      nav = 1.0
0293      peak = 1.0
0294      first_trade = True
0295      rf_daily = (1 + (cfg.financing_rate if cfg else 0.04)) ** (1 / 252) - 1
0296      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0297  
0298      for position in positions:
0299          date = dates[position]
0300          month = months[position]
0301          base = base_weights[position].copy()
0302          severity = float(stress[position])
0303          transfer_fraction = 0.0 if cfg is None else cfg.max_risk_transfer * severity
0304          desired = base.copy()
0305          removed_equity = desired[0] * transfer_fraction
0306          removed_oil = desired[3] * transfer_fraction
0307          desired[0] -= removed_equity
0308          desired[3] -= removed_oil
0309          removed = removed_equity + removed_oil
0310          bond_share = 0.50 if cfg is None else cfg.bond_share
0311          desired[1] += removed * bond_share
0312          desired[2] += removed * (1 - bond_share)
0313  
0314          month_boundary = previous_month is None or month != previous_month
0315          band = math.inf if cfg is None else cfg.rebalance_band
0316          desired_turnover = 0.5 * float(np.abs(desired - pretrade).sum())
0317          rebalance = month_boundary or desired_turnover >= band
0318          weights = desired if rebalance else pretrade.copy()
0319          delta = weights - pretrade
0320          turnover = float(np.abs(delta).sum()) if first_trade else 0.5 * float(
0321              np.abs(delta).sum()
0322          )
0323          trade_cost = float(np.abs(delta).sum()) * 0.0015 * cost_multiplier
0324          fx_cost = (
0325              abs((weights[2] + weights[3]) - (pretrade[2] + pretrade[3]))
0326              * 0.0005
0327              * cost_multiplier
0328          )
0329          debt_weight = 1.0 - float(weights.sum())
0330          asset_return = returns[position]
0331          gross_return = float(weights @ asset_return + debt_weight * rf_daily)
0332          net_return = gross_return - trade_cost - fx_cost
0333          nav *= 1 + net_return
0334          peak = max(peak, nav)
0335          pretrade = weights * (1 + asset_return) / (1 + gross_return)
0336          rows.append(
0337              {
0338                  "date": date,
0339                  "month": month,
0340                  "return": net_return,
0341                  "gross_return": gross_return,
0342                  "nav": nav,
0343                  "drawdown": nav / peak - 1,
0344                  "turnover": turnover,
0345                  "trade_cost": trade_cost,
0346                  "fx_cost": fx_cost,
0347                  "stress": severity,
0348                  "transfer_fraction": transfer_fraction,
0349                  "signal_date": signal_dates[position],
0350                  **{f"w_{asset}": weights[i] for i, asset in enumerate(ASSETS)},
0351              }
0352          )
0353          previous_month = month
0354          first_trade = False
0355  
0356      daily = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("date")
0357      monthly = daily.groupby("month").agg(
0358          return_factor=("return", lambda x: float(np.prod(1 + x))),
0359          gross_factor=("gross_return", lambda x: float(np.prod(1 + x))),
0360          turnover=("turnover", "sum"),
0361          trade_cost=("trade_cost", "sum"),
0362          fx_cost=("fx_cost", "sum"),
0363          avg_stress=("stress", "mean"),
0364          max_stress=("stress", "max"),
0365          avg_transfer=("transfer_fraction", "mean"),
0366      )
0367      monthly["return"] = monthly["return_factor"] - 1
0368      monthly["gross_return"] = monthly["gross_factor"] - 1
0369      wealth = (1 + monthly["return"]).cumprod()
0370      monthly["nav"] = wealth
0371      monthly["drawdown"] = wealth / wealth.cummax() - 1
0372      return (daily if keep_daily else pd.DataFrame()), monthly
0373  
0374  
0375  def metric_row(period: str, strategy: str, monthly: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, object]:
0376      metrics = performance_summary(monthly["return"])
0377      return {
0378          "Period": period,
0379          "Strategy": strategy,
0380          **metrics.to_dict(),
0381          "AvgTurnover": float(monthly["turnover"].mean()),
0382          "TotalCost": float(monthly[["trade_cost", "fx_cost"]].sum().sum()),
0383      }

0385  
0386  def reconcile_to_monthly_reference(
0387      monthly_reference: pd.DataFrame,
0388      daily_baseline: pd.DataFrame,
0389      daily_overlay: pd.DataFrame,
0390  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0391      """Apply only the daily overlay's relative return to the validated monthly path.
0392  
0393      The daily reconstruction differs slightly from the original monthly engine
0394      because financing and costs compound at a different frequency. Taking the
0395      overlay/base relative factor isolates the incremental VKOSPI allocation
0396      effect without pretending that this frequency-conversion gap is alpha.
0397      """
0398      common = (
0399          monthly_reference.index.intersection(daily_baseline.index)
0400          .intersection(daily_overlay.index)
0401          .sort_values()
0402      )
0403      relative_factor = (1 + daily_overlay.loc[common, "return"]).div(
0404          1 + daily_baseline.loc[common, "return"]
0405      )
0406      output = pd.DataFrame(index=common)
0407      output["reference_return"] = monthly_reference.loc[common, "return"]
0408      output["overlay_relative_return"] = relative_factor - 1
0409      output["return"] = (1 + output["reference_return"]) * relative_factor - 1
0410      output["gross_return"] = output["return"]
0411      output["turnover"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "turnover"]
0412      output["trade_cost"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "trade_cost"]
0413      output["fx_cost"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "fx_cost"]
0414      output["avg_stress"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "avg_stress"]
0415      output["max_stress"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "max_stress"]
0416      output["avg_transfer"] = daily_overlay.loc[common, "avg_transfer"]
0417      wealth = (1 + output["return"]).cumprod()
0418      output["nav"] = wealth
0419      output["drawdown"] = wealth / wealth.cummax() - 1
0420      return output
0421  
0422  
0423  def paired_multiobjective_bootstrap(
0424      baseline: pd.Series,
0425      candidate: pd.Series,
0426      block_length: int = 6,
0427      simulations: int = 5_000,
0428  ) -> dict[str, float]:
0429      common = baseline.index.intersection(candidate.index)
0430      matrix = np.column_stack([baseline.loc[common], candidate.loc[common]]).astype(float)
0431      count = len(matrix)
0432      rng = np.random.default_rng(20260827 + block_length)
0433      deltas = np.empty((simulations, 3), dtype=float)
0434      for simulation in range(simulations):
0435          indices: list[int] = []
0436          while len(indices) < count:
0437              start = int(rng.integers(0, count - block_length + 1))
0438              indices.extend(range(start, start + block_length))
0439          draw = matrix[np.asarray(indices[:count])]
0440          metrics = []
0441          for column in range(2):
0442              values = draw[:, column]
0443              years = count / 12
0444              cagr = float(np.prod(1 + values) ** (1 / years) - 1)
0445              sharpe = float(values.mean() / values.std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(12))
0446              wealth = np.cumprod(1 + values)
0447              mdd = float(np.min(wealth / np.maximum.accumulate(wealth) - 1))
0448              metrics.append((cagr, sharpe, mdd))
0449          deltas[simulation] = np.asarray(metrics[1]) - np.asarray(metrics[0])
0450      names = ("cagr", "sharpe", "mdd")
0451      output: dict[str, float] = {
0452          f"probability_{name}_improves": float(np.mean(deltas[:, index] > 0))
0453          for index, name in enumerate(names)
0454      }
0455      output["probability_all_three_improve"] = float(np.mean((deltas > 0).all(axis=1)))
0456      for index, name in enumerate(names):
0457          output[f"{name}_delta_p05"] = float(np.quantile(deltas[:, index], 0.05))
0458          output[f"{name}_delta_median"] = float(np.median(deltas[:, index]))
0459          output[f"{name}_delta_p95"] = float(np.quantile(deltas[:, index], 0.95))
0460      return output
0461  
0462  
0463  def candidate_configs() -> list[DynamicRiskConfig]:
0464      configs: dict[str, DynamicRiskConfig] = {}
0465      for mode in ("level", "momentum", "max", "mean"):
0466          level_values = (0.70, 0.80, 0.90) if mode != "momentum" else (0.80,)
0467          momentum_windows = (5, 10, 21) if mode != "level" else (10,)
0468          spike_values = (0.05, 0.10, 0.15) if mode != "level" else (0.10,)
0469          for level, window, spike, risk_cut, band in itertools.product(
0470              level_values,
0471              momentum_windows,
0472              spike_values,
0473              (0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00),
0474              (0.05, 0.10),
0475          ):
0476              cfg = DynamicRiskConfig(mode, level, window, spike, risk_cut, 0.50, band)
0477              configs[cfg.name] = cfg
0478      return list(configs.values())
0479  
0480  
0481  def main() -> None:
0482      levels = load_daily_open_levels()
0483      reference = load_reference_weights()
0484      vkospi_signals = build_daily_vkospi_signals()
0485      arrays = prepare_arrays(levels, reference, vkospi_signals)
0486      if (pd.DatetimeIndex(arrays["signal_dates"]) >= pd.DatetimeIndex(arrays["dates"])).any():
0487          raise AssertionError("VKOSPI daily signal uses same-day or future information")
0488  
0489      _, baseline_full = simulate(arrays, None, keep_daily=False)
0490      baseline_cal = baseline_full.loc[:CAL_END]
0491      baseline_validation = baseline_full.loc[CAL_VALIDATION_START:CAL_END]
0492      baseline_cal_metrics = performance_summary(baseline_cal["return"])
0493      baseline_validation_metrics = performance_summary(baseline_validation["return"])
0494  
0495      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0496      configs = candidate_configs()
0497      for number, cfg in enumerate(configs, start=1):
0498          _, monthly = simulate(arrays, cfg, end=CAL_END, keep_daily=False)
0499          full_metrics = performance_summary(monthly["return"])
0500          validation_metrics = performance_summary(
0501              monthly.loc[CAL_VALIDATION_START:CAL_END, "return"]
0502          )
0503          rows.append(
0504              {
0505                  "name": cfg.name,
0506                  "stage": "coarse",
0507                  **asdict(cfg),
0508                  **{f"Full_{key}": float(full_metrics[key]) for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")},
0509                  **{
0510                      f"Validation_{key}": float(validation_metrics[key])
0511                      for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0512                  },
0513                  "AvgTurnover": float(monthly["turnover"].mean()),
0514                  "TotalCost": float(monthly[["trade_cost", "fx_cost"]].sum().sum()),
0515              }
0516          )
0517          if number % 100 == 0:
0518              print(f"calibrated {number}/{len(configs)}")
0519  
0520      calibration = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0521      full_gate = (
0522          (calibration["Full_CAGR"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["CAGR"]))
0523          & (calibration["Full_Sharpe"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["Sharpe"]))
0524          & (calibration["Full_MDD"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["MDD"]))
0525      )
0526      validation_gate = (
0527          (calibration["Validation_CAGR"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["CAGR"]))
0528          & (calibration["Validation_Sharpe"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["Sharpe"]))
0529          & (calibration["Validation_MDD"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["MDD"]))
0530      )
0531      eligible = calibration.loc[full_gate & validation_gate].copy()
0532      if eligible.empty:
0533          raise RuntimeError("No VKOSPI dynamic overlay improved all three calibration objectives")
0534  
0535      # Refine only the coarse candidates that passed every objective in both
0536      # calibration windows. This keeps the search local and prevents the locked
0537      # test from choosing the defensive destination or trading-band parameters.
0538      refinement: dict[str, DynamicRiskConfig] = {}
0539      for _, seed in eligible.iterrows():
0540          for bond_share, risk_transfer, band in itertools.product(
0541              (0.00, 0.25, 0.50, 0.75, 1.00),
0542              (0.125, 0.25, 0.375),
0543              (0.075, 0.10, 0.15),
0544          ):
0545              cfg = DynamicRiskConfig(
0546                  mode=str(seed["mode"]),
0547                  level_threshold=float(seed["level_threshold"]),
0548                  momentum_window=int(seed["momentum_window"]),
0549                  spike_threshold=float(seed["spike_threshold"]),
0550                  max_risk_transfer=risk_transfer,
0551                  bond_share=bond_share,
0552                  rebalance_band=band,
0553              )
0554              refinement[cfg.name] = cfg
0555  
0556      refinement_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0557      for number, cfg in enumerate(refinement.values(), start=1):
0558          _, monthly = simulate(arrays, cfg, end=CAL_END, keep_daily=False)
0559          full_metrics = performance_summary(monthly["return"])
0560          validation_metrics = performance_summary(
0561              monthly.loc[CAL_VALIDATION_START:CAL_END, "return"]
0562          )
0563          refinement_rows.append(
0564              {
0565                  "name": cfg.name,
0566                  "stage": "refined",
0567                  **asdict(cfg),
0568                  **{
0569                      f"Full_{key}": float(full_metrics[key])
0570                      for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0571                  },
0572                  **{
0573                      f"Validation_{key}": float(validation_metrics[key])
0574                      for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0575                  },
0576                  "AvgTurnover": float(monthly["turnover"].mean()),
0577                  "TotalCost": float(
0578                      monthly[["trade_cost", "fx_cost"]].sum().sum()
0579                  ),
0580              }
0581          )
0582          if number % 100 == 0:
0583              print(f"refined {number}/{len(refinement)}")
0584  
0585      calibration = (
0586          pd.concat([calibration, pd.DataFrame(refinement_rows)], ignore_index=True)
0587          .sort_values("stage")
0588          .drop_duplicates("name", keep="last")
0589          .reset_index(drop=True)
0590      )
0591      full_gate = (
0592          (calibration["Full_CAGR"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["CAGR"]))
0593          & (calibration["Full_Sharpe"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["Sharpe"]))
0594          & (calibration["Full_MDD"] >= float(baseline_cal_metrics["MDD"]))
0595      )
0596      validation_gate = (
0597          (calibration["Validation_CAGR"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["CAGR"]))
0598          & (calibration["Validation_Sharpe"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["Sharpe"]))
0599          & (calibration["Validation_MDD"] >= float(baseline_validation_metrics["MDD"]))
0600      )
0601      eligible = calibration.loc[full_gate & validation_gate].copy()
0602      rank_columns = []
0603      for prefix in ("Full", "Validation"):
0604          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD"):
0605              rank = f"{prefix}_{metric}_rank"
0606              eligible[rank] = eligible[f"{prefix}_{metric}"].rank(pct=True)
0607              rank_columns.append(rank)
0608      eligible["MultiObjectiveScore"] = eligible[rank_columns].mean(axis=1)
0609      winner_row = eligible.sort_values(
0610          ["MultiObjectiveScore", "Validation_Calmar", "Full_Calmar"], ascending=False
0611      ).iloc[0]
0612      winner = DynamicRiskConfig(
0613          mode=str(winner_row["mode"]),
0614          level_threshold=float(winner_row["level_threshold"]),
0615          momentum_window=int(winner_row["momentum_window"]),
0616          spike_threshold=float(winner_row["spike_threshold"]),
0617          max_risk_transfer=float(winner_row["max_risk_transfer"]),
0618          bond_share=float(winner_row["bond_share"]),
0619          rebalance_band=float(winner_row["rebalance_band"]),
0620          financing_rate=float(winner_row["financing_rate"]),
0621      )
0622  
0623      winner_daily, winner_monthly = simulate(arrays, winner)
0624      reconciled_monthly = reconcile_to_monthly_reference(
0625          reference, baseline_full, winner_monthly
0626      )
0627      comparison_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0628      for period, start, end in (
0629          ("calibration_2007_2017", baseline_full.index.min(), CAL_END),
0630          ("validation_2013_2017", CAL_VALIDATION_START, CAL_END),
0631          ("locked_2018_2026", TEST_START, baseline_full.index.max()),
0632          ("full_2007_2026", baseline_full.index.min(), baseline_full.index.max()),
0633      ):
0634          comparison_rows.append(metric_row(period, "ReferenceDaily", baseline_full.loc[start:end]))
0635          comparison_rows.append(
0636              metric_row(period, "VKOSPIDynamicActual", winner_monthly.loc[start:end])
0637          )
0638          comparison_rows.append(
0639              metric_row(period, "ReferenceMonthly", reference.loc[start:end])
0640          )
0641          comparison_rows.append(
0642              metric_row(
0643                  period,
0644                  "VKOSPIDynamicReconciled",
0645                  reconciled_monthly.loc[start:end],
0646              )
0647          )
0648      comparison = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
0649  
0650      base_locked = baseline_full.loc[TEST_START:]
0651      winner_locked = winner_monthly.loc[TEST_START:]
0652      base_locked_metrics = performance_summary(base_locked["return"])
0653      winner_locked_metrics = performance_summary(winner_locked["return"])
0654      locked_deltas = {
0655          metric: float(winner_locked_metrics[metric] - base_locked_metrics[metric])
0656          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0657      }
0658      reference_monthly_locked = reference.loc[TEST_START:]
0659      reconciled_locked = reconciled_monthly.loc[TEST_START:]
0660      reference_monthly_locked_metrics = performance_summary(
0661          reference_monthly_locked["return"]
0662      )
0663      reconciled_locked_metrics = performance_summary(reconciled_locked["return"])
0664      reconciled_locked_deltas = {
0665          metric: float(
0666              reconciled_locked_metrics[metric] - reference_monthly_locked_metrics[metric]
0667          )
0668          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0669      }
0670      actual_pass = bool(
0671          locked_deltas["CAGR"] > 0
0672          and locked_deltas["Sharpe"] > 0
0673          and locked_deltas["MDD"] >= 0
0674      )
0675      reconciled_pass = bool(
0676          reconciled_locked_deltas["CAGR"] > 0
0677          and reconciled_locked_deltas["Sharpe"] > 0
0678          and reconciled_locked_deltas["MDD"] >= 0
0679      )
0680      promoted = actual_pass and reconciled_pass
0681  
0682      cost_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0683      for cost in (0.5, 1.0, 1.5, 2.0):
0684          _, base_cost = simulate(arrays, None, cost_multiplier=cost, keep_daily=False)
0685          _, winner_cost = simulate(arrays, winner, cost_multiplier=cost, keep_daily=False)
0686          cost_rows.append(metric_row(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_locked", "ReferenceDaily", base_cost.loc[TEST_START:]))
0687          cost_rows.append(metric_row(f"cost_{cost:.1f}x_locked", "VKOSPIDynamic", winner_cost.loc[TEST_START:]))
0688  
0689      subperiod_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0690      for start, end in (
0691          (pd.Period("2018-01", "M"), pd.Period("2020-12", "M")),
0692          (pd.Period("2021-01", "M"), pd.Period("2023-12", "M")),
0693          (pd.Period("2024-01", "M"), winner_monthly.index.max()),
0694      ):
0695          label = f"{start}_{end}"
0696          subperiod_rows.append(metric_row(label, "ReferenceDaily", baseline_full.loc[start:end]))
0697          subperiod_rows.append(
0698              metric_row(label, "VKOSPIDynamicActual", winner_monthly.loc[start:end])
0699          )
0700          subperiod_rows.append(
0701              metric_row(label, "ReferenceMonthly", reference.loc[start:end])
0702          )
0703          subperiod_rows.append(
0704              metric_row(
0705                  label,
0706                  "VKOSPIDynamicReconciled",
0707                  reconciled_monthly.loc[start:end],
0708              )
0709          )
0710  
0711      calibration.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_calibration.csv", index=False)
0712      comparison.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_comparison.csv", index=False)
0713      pd.DataFrame(cost_rows).to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_cost_sensitivity.csv", index=False)
0714      pd.DataFrame(subperiod_rows).to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_subperiods.csv", index=False)
0715      winner_daily.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_daily.csv")
0716      winner_monthly.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_monthly.csv")
0717      reconciled_monthly.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv")
0718      report = {
0719          "method": {
0720              "interpretation": "VKOSPI is the Korean equity implied-volatility index (domestic VIX)",
0721              "timing": "previous VKOSPI close only; position earns next open-to-open return",
0722              "overlay": "transfer a stress-scaled fraction of KODEX200 and USO to BOND and GLD",
0723              "reconciliation": "validated monthly reference multiplied by dynamic/base daily relative return; isolates overlay alpha from frequency conversion",
0724              "selection": "all CAGR, Sharpe, and MDD objectives must improve in both 2007-2017 and 2013-2017",
0725          },
0726          "winner": asdict(winner),
0727          "eligible_candidates": len(eligible),
0728          "total_candidates": len(calibration),
0729          "locked": {
0730              "actual_daily": {
0731                  "reference": base_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0732                  "candidate": winner_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0733                  "deltas": locked_deltas,
0734                  "passes_all_three": actual_pass,
0735              },
0736              "monthly_reference_reconciled": {
0737                  "reference": reference_monthly_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0738                  "candidate": reconciled_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0739                  "deltas": reconciled_locked_deltas,
0740                  "passes_all_three": reconciled_pass,
0741              },
0742              "promoted": promoted,
0743              "actual_daily_bootstrap": paired_multiobjective_bootstrap(
0744                  base_locked["return"], winner_locked["return"]
0745              ),
0746              "reconciled_multiobjective_bootstrap": paired_multiobjective_bootstrap(
0747                  reference_monthly_locked["return"], reconciled_locked["return"]
0748              ),
0749          },
0750      }
0751      (RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_validation.json").write_text(
0752          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0753      )
0754  
0755      print("\n=== DAILY RECONSTRUCTION VS MONTHLY REFERENCE ===")
0756      monthly_reference = performance_summary(reference.loc[baseline_full.index, "return"])
0757      print(
0758          pd.DataFrame(
0759              {"DailyReconstruction": performance_summary(baseline_full["return"]), "MonthlyReference": monthly_reference}
0760          ).loc[["CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar"]]
0761      )
0762      print("\n=== WINNER ===")
0763      print(winner)
0764      print(f"eligible={len(eligible)}/{len(calibration)}")
0765      print("\n=== COMPARISON ===")
0766      print(
0767          comparison[["Period", "Strategy", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]]
0768          .to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0769      )
0770      print("\n=== LOCKED DELTAS ===")
0771      print(json.dumps({"actual_daily": locked_deltas, "reconciled": reconciled_locked_deltas}, indent=2))
0772      print(f"PROMOTED={promoted}")
0773  
0774  
0775  if __name__ == "__main__":
0776      main()

0035  @dataclass(frozen=True)
0036  class RobustStressConfig:
0037      mode: str
0038      level_threshold: float
0039      shock_threshold: float
0040      max_risk_transfer: float
0041      bond_share: float
0042      rebalance_band: float
0043      financing_rate: float = 0.04
0044  
0045      @property
0046      def name(self) -> str:
0047          return (
0048              f"{self.mode}_lt{self.level_threshold:.2f}_st{self.shock_threshold:.2f}"
0049              f"_rt{self.max_risk_transfer:.2f}_bs{self.bond_share:.2f}"
0050              f"_rb{self.rebalance_band:.2f}"
0051          )
0052  
0053      def dynamic_config(self) -> DynamicRiskConfig:
0054          return DynamicRiskConfig(
0055              mode="level",
0056              level_threshold=self.level_threshold,
0057              momentum_window=5,
0058              spike_threshold=0.0,
0059              max_risk_transfer=self.max_risk_transfer,
0060              bond_share=self.bond_share,
0061              rebalance_band=self.rebalance_band,
0062              financing_rate=self.financing_rate,
0063          )
