0066  def robust_zscore(series: pd.Series, window: int, minimum: int) -> pd.Series:
0067      median = series.rolling(window, min_periods=minimum).median()
0068      mad = (series - median).abs().rolling(window, min_periods=minimum).median()
0069      return ((series - median) / (1.4826 * mad.replace(0, np.nan))).clip(-6, 6)
0070  
0071  
0072  def build_robust_daily_features() -> pd.DataFrame:
0073      daily = load_vkospi_daily()
0074      close = daily["close"].astype(float)
0075      log_close = np.log(close.where(close > 0))
0076      log_return = log_close.diff()
0077      output = pd.DataFrame(index=daily.index)
0078      output["close"] = close
0079      output["percentile_126"] = causal_percentile(close, window=126, minimum=84)
0080      output["percentile_252"] = causal_percentile(close, window=252, minimum=126)
0081      output["robust_z_63"] = robust_zscore(log_close, 63, 42)
0082      output["robust_z_252"] = robust_zscore(log_close, 252, 126)
0083      for window in (5, 10, 21):
0084          scale = log_return.rolling(63, min_periods=42).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(window)
0085          output[f"shock_{window}"] = (
0086              log_close.diff(window) / scale.replace(0, np.nan)
0087          ).clip(-8, 8)
0088      output["acceleration_5"] = log_close.diff(5) - log_close.diff(5).shift(5)
0089      acceleration_scale = log_return.rolling(63, min_periods=42).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(5)
0090      output["acceleration_z5"] = (
0091          output["acceleration_5"] / acceleration_scale.replace(0, np.nan)
0092      ).clip(-8, 8)
0093  
0094      high = daily["high"].astype(float).where(daily["high"].astype(float) > 0, close)
0095      low = daily["low"].astype(float).where(daily["low"].astype(float) > 0, close)
0096      high21 = high.rolling(21, min_periods=10).max()
0097      low21 = low.rolling(21, min_periods=10).min()
0098      output["distance_high21"] = close / high21 - 1
0099      output["close_location21"] = (
0100          (close - low21) / (high21 - low21).replace(0, np.nan)
0101      ).clip(0, 1)
0102      output["positive_fraction5"] = (log_return > 0).rolling(5, min_periods=3).mean()
0103      output["positive_fraction21"] = (log_return > 0).rolling(21, min_periods=10).mean()
0104      output["fast_slow"] = log_close.diff(5) - 5 / 21 * log_close.diff(21)
0105      return output.replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)
0106  
0107  
0108  def align_features_to_arrays(
0109      features: pd.DataFrame, arrays: dict[str, object]
0110  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0111      signal_dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(arrays["signal_dates"])
0112      aligned = features.reindex(signal_dates)
0113      aligned.index = pd.RangeIndex(len(aligned))
0114      return aligned
0115  
0116  
0117  def stress_from_features(
0118      aligned: pd.DataFrame,
0119      mode: str,
0120      level_threshold: float,
0121      shock_threshold: float,
0122  ) -> np.ndarray:
0123      percentile = aligned["percentile_252"].fillna(aligned["percentile_126"])
0124      level = np.clip(
0125          (percentile.to_numpy(dtype=float) - level_threshold)
0126          / max(1 - level_threshold, 1e-6),
0127          0,
0128          1,
0129      )
0130      shock_raw = aligned["shock_5"].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0131      shock = np.clip((shock_raw - shock_threshold) / 2.5, 0, 1)
0132      acceleration = np.clip(
0133          (aligned["acceleration_z5"].to_numpy(dtype=float) - shock_threshold) / 2.5,
0134          0,
0135          1,
0136      )
0137      confirmation = np.nan_to_num(
0138          aligned["close_location21"].to_numpy(dtype=float), nan=0.5
0139      ).clip(0, 1)
0140      distance = np.nan_to_num(
0141          aligned["distance_high21"].to_numpy(dtype=float), nan=0.0
0142      )
0143      falling = np.clip(-shock_raw / 2.5, 0, 1)
0144      exhaustion = np.clip(level * (-distance).clip(0, 0.5) / 0.5 * falling, 0, 1)
0145      if mode == "robust_mean":
0146          stress = 0.50 * level + 0.50 * shock
0147      elif mode == "robust_max":
0148          stress = np.maximum(level, shock)
0149      elif mode == "confirmed":
0150          stress = (0.45 * level + 0.55 * shock) * (0.35 + 0.65 * confirmation)
0151      elif mode == "acceleration":
0152          stress = 0.40 * level + 0.35 * shock + 0.25 * acceleration
0153      elif mode == "exhaustion_adjusted":
0154          stress = (0.50 * level + 0.50 * shock) * (1 - 0.75 * exhaustion)
0155      else:
0156          raise ValueError(mode)
0157      return np.nan_to_num(stress, nan=0.0, posinf=1.0, neginf=0.0).clip(0, 1)
0158  
0159  
0160  def metric_row(
0161      period: str,
0162      strategy: str,
0163      monthly: pd.DataFrame,
0164  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0165      metrics = performance_summary(monthly["return"])
0166      return {
0167          "Period": period,
0168          "Strategy": strategy,
0169          **metrics.to_dict(),
0170          "AvgTurnover": float(monthly["turnover"].mean()),
0171          "AvgStress": float(monthly.get("avg_stress", pd.Series(0, index=monthly.index)).mean()),
0172          "AvgTransfer": float(monthly.get("avg_transfer", pd.Series(0, index=monthly.index)).mean()),
0173      }
0174  
0175  
0176  def main() -> None:
0177      reference = load_reference_weights()
0178      levels = load_daily_open_levels()
0179      arrays = prepare_arrays(levels, reference, build_daily_vkospi_signals())
0180      robust_features = align_features_to_arrays(build_robust_daily_features(), arrays)
0181      _, baseline_monthly = simulate(arrays, None, keep_daily=False)
0182      existing_report = json.loads(
0183          (RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
0184      )
0185      existing_cfg = DynamicRiskConfig(**existing_report["winner"])
0186      _, existing_monthly = simulate(arrays, existing_cfg, keep_daily=False)
0187      existing_cal = performance_summary(existing_monthly.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0188      existing_validation = performance_summary(
0189          existing_monthly.loc[VALIDATION_START:CAL_END, "return"]
0190      )
0191  
0192      stress_cache: dict[tuple[str, float, float], np.ndarray] = {}
0193      for mode, level_threshold, shock_threshold in itertools.product(
0194          ("robust_mean", "robust_max", "confirmed", "acceleration", "exhaustion_adjusted"),
0195          (0.70, 0.80, 0.90),
0196          (0.0, 0.5, 1.0),
0197      ):
0198          stress_cache[(mode, level_threshold, shock_threshold)] = stress_from_features(
0199              robust_features, mode, level_threshold, shock_threshold
0200          )
0201  
0202      rows = []
0203      paths: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
0204      configs = []
0205      for mode, level_threshold, shock_threshold, transfer, bond_share, band in itertools.product(
0206          ("robust_mean", "robust_max", "confirmed", "acceleration", "exhaustion_adjusted"),
0207          (0.70, 0.80, 0.90),
0208          (0.0, 0.5, 1.0),
0209          (0.15, 0.25, 0.35),
0210          (0.0, 0.5),
0211          (0.10, 0.15, 0.20),
0212      ):
0213          config = RobustStressConfig(
0214              mode,
0215              level_threshold,
0216              shock_threshold,
0217              transfer,
0218              bond_share,
0219              band,
0220          )
0221          stress = stress_cache[(mode, level_threshold, shock_threshold)]
0222          _, monthly = simulate(
0223              arrays,
0224              config.dynamic_config(),
0225              keep_daily=False,
0226              stress_override=stress,
0227          )
0228          paths[config.name] = monthly
0229          configs.append(config)
0230          cal = performance_summary(monthly.loc[:CAL_END, "return"])
0231          val = performance_summary(monthly.loc[VALIDATION_START:CAL_END, "return"])
0232          rows.append(
0233              {
0234                  "Config": config.name,
0235                  **asdict(config),
0236                  **{f"Cal_{key}": value for key, value in cal.to_dict().items()},
0237                  **{f"Validation_{key}": value for key, value in val.to_dict().items()},
0238                  "Cal_CAGRDelta": float(cal["CAGR"] - existing_cal["CAGR"]),
0239                  "Cal_SharpeDelta": float(cal["Sharpe"] - existing_cal["Sharpe"]),
0240                  "Cal_MDDDelta": float(cal["MDD"] - existing_cal["MDD"]),
0241                  "Validation_CAGRDelta": float(
0242                      val["CAGR"] - existing_validation["CAGR"]
0243                  ),
0244                  "Validation_SharpeDelta": float(
0245                      val["Sharpe"] - existing_validation["Sharpe"]
0246                  ),
0247                  "Validation_MDDDelta": float(
0248                      val["MDD"] - existing_validation["MDD"]
0249                  ),
0250                  "AvgTurnover": float(monthly.loc[:CAL_END, "turnover"].mean()),
0251                  "AvgStress": float(monthly.loc[:CAL_END, "avg_stress"].mean()),
0252              }
0253          )
0254      calibration = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0255      for metric in (
0256          "Cal_CAGR",
0257          "Cal_Sharpe",
0258          "Cal_MDD",
0259          "Cal_Calmar",
0260          "Validation_CAGR",
0261          "Validation_Sharpe",
0262          "Validation_MDD",
0263          "Validation_Calmar",
0264      ):
0265          calibration[f"Rank_{metric}"] = calibration[metric].rank(pct=True)
0266      calibration["MultiObjectiveScore"] = calibration[
0267          [column for column in calibration if column.startswith("Rank_")]
0268      ].mean(axis=1)
0269      strict = calibration.loc[
0270          (calibration["Cal_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0271          & (calibration["Cal_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0272          & (calibration["Cal_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0273          & (calibration["Validation_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0274          & (calibration["Validation_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0275          & (calibration["Validation_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0276          & (calibration["AvgStress"] > 0.002)
0277      ]
0278      selection_rule = "all three improve versus existing dynamic in 2007-2017 and 2013-2017"
0279      eligible = strict
0280      if eligible.empty:
0281          eligible = calibration.loc[
0282              (calibration["Cal_CAGR"] >= 0.995 * existing_cal["CAGR"])
0283              & (calibration["Cal_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0284              & (calibration["Cal_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0285              & (calibration["Validation_CAGR"] >= 0.995 * existing_validation["CAGR"])
0286              & (calibration["Validation_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0287              & (calibration["Validation_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0288              & (calibration["AvgStress"] > 0.002)
0289          ]
0290          selection_rule = "Sharpe and MDD improve with at least 99.5% CAGR retention in both pre-2018 windows"
0291      if eligible.empty:
0292          eligible = calibration.loc[calibration["AvgStress"] > 0.002]
0293          selection_rule = "highest pre-2018 multiobjective rank; no strict improvement candidate"
0294      winner_row = eligible.sort_values(
0295          ["MultiObjectiveScore", "Cal_Sharpe", "Validation_Sharpe", "Cal_CAGR"],
0296          ascending=False,
0297      ).iloc[0]
0298      calibration["Selected"] = False
0299      calibration.loc[winner_row.name, "Selected"] = True
0300      winner = RobustStressConfig(
0301          mode=str(winner_row["mode"]),
0302          level_threshold=float(winner_row["level_threshold"]),
0303          shock_threshold=float(winner_row["shock_threshold"]),
0304          max_risk_transfer=float(winner_row["max_risk_transfer"]),
0305          bond_share=float(winner_row["bond_share"]),
0306          rebalance_band=float(winner_row["rebalance_band"]),
0307          financing_rate=float(winner_row["financing_rate"]),
0308      )
0309      winner_stress = stress_cache[
0310          (winner.mode, winner.level_threshold, winner.shock_threshold)
0311      ]
0312      winner_daily, winner_monthly = simulate(
0313          arrays,
0314          winner.dynamic_config(),
0315          keep_daily=True,
0316          stress_override=winner_stress,
0317      )
0318  
0319      existing_reconciled = pd.read_csv(
0320          RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col="month"
0321      )
0322      existing_reconciled.index = pd.PeriodIndex(existing_reconciled.index, freq="M")
0323      winner_reconciled = reconcile_to_monthly_reference(
0324          reference, baseline_monthly, winner_monthly
0325      )
0326      comparison_rows = []
0327      periods = (
0328          ("calibration_2007_2017", reference.index.min(), CAL_END),
0329          ("validation_2013_2017", VALIDATION_START, CAL_END),
0330          ("locked_2018_2026", TEST_START, reference.index.max()),
0331          ("full_2007_2026", reference.index.min(), reference.index.max()),
0332      )
0333      for period, start, end in periods:
0334          for strategy, monthly in (
0335              ("ExistingDynamicActual", existing_monthly),
0336              ("RobustDynamicActual", winner_monthly),
0337              ("ExistingDynamicReconciled", existing_reconciled),
0338              ("RobustDynamicReconciled", winner_reconciled),
0339          ):
0340              comparison_rows.append(metric_row(period, strategy, monthly.loc[start:end]))
0341      comparison = pd.DataFrame(comparison_rows)
0342  
0343      existing_locked = existing_reconciled.loc[TEST_START:]
0344      robust_locked = winner_reconciled.loc[TEST_START:]
0345      existing_locked_metrics = performance_summary(existing_locked["return"])
0346      robust_locked_metrics = performance_summary(robust_locked["return"])
0347      locked_deltas = {
0348          metric: float(robust_locked_metrics[metric] - existing_locked_metrics[metric])
0349          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0350      }
0351      cost_rows = []
0352      for multiplier in (1.0, 2.0):
0353          _, existing_cost = simulate(
0354              arrays, existing_cfg, cost_multiplier=multiplier, keep_daily=False
0355          )
0356          _, robust_cost = simulate(
0357              arrays,
0358              winner.dynamic_config(),
0359              cost_multiplier=multiplier,
0360              keep_daily=False,
0361              stress_override=winner_stress,
0362          )
0363          cost_rows.append(
0364              metric_row(
0365                  f"cost_{multiplier:.1f}x_locked",
0366                  "ExistingDynamic",
0367                  existing_cost.loc[TEST_START:],
0368              )
0369          )
0370          cost_rows.append(
0371              metric_row(
0372                  f"cost_{multiplier:.1f}x_locked",
0373                  "RobustDynamic",
0374                  robust_cost.loc[TEST_START:],
0375              )
0376          )
0377  
0378      report = {
0379          "objective": "Improve the existing VKOSPI dynamic overlay with robust daily transformations",
0380          "processing": {
0381              "level": "causal 126/252-day percentiles and rolling median/MAD z-scores",
0382              "shock": "5/10/21-day log changes normalized by trailing 63-day volatility",
0383              "path": "acceleration, 21-day high distance, close location and fast-minus-slow change",
0384              "states": "confirmation and exhaustion-adjusted continuous stress variants",
0385          },
0386          "calibration_end": str(CAL_END),
0387          "locked_start": str(TEST_START),
0388          "candidate_count": int(len(calibration)),
0389          "strict_eligible_count": int(len(strict)),
0390          "selection_rule": selection_rule,
0391          "winner": asdict(winner),
0392          "locked": {
0393              "existing": existing_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0394              "robust": robust_locked_metrics.to_dict(),
0395              "deltas": locked_deltas,
0396              "passes_all_three": bool(
0397                  locked_deltas["CAGR"] > 0
0398                  and locked_deltas["Sharpe"] > 0
0399                  and locked_deltas["MDD"] >= 0
0400              ),
0401              "bootstrap": paired_multiobjective_bootstrap(
0402                  existing_locked["return"], robust_locked["return"]
0403              ),
0404          },
0405      }
0406      calibration.to_csv(
0407          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_calibration.csv", index=False
0408      )
0409      comparison.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_comparison.csv", index=False)
0410      pd.DataFrame(cost_rows).to_csv(
0411          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_cost_sensitivity.csv", index=False
0412      )
0413      winner_daily.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_daily.csv")
0414      winner_monthly.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_monthly.csv")
0415      winner_reconciled.to_csv(
0416          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv"
0417      )
0418      (RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_validation.json").write_text(
0419          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0420      )
0421  
0422      print("\n=== WINNER ===")
0423      print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
0424      print("\n=== COMPARISON ===")
0425      print(
0426          comparison[
0427              ["Period", "Strategy", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]
0428          ].to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0429      )
0430      print("\n=== COST SENSITIVITY ===")
0431      print(
0432          pd.DataFrame(cost_rows)[
0433              ["Period", "Strategy", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]
0434          ].to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0435      )
0436  
0437  
0438  if __name__ == "__main__":
0439      main()

0075  def build_no_sjm_components(returns: pd.DataFrame) -> pd.DataFrame:
0076      """Build the causal macro component panel shared by no-SJM variants."""
0077      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
0078      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period]] = []
0079      for target_month in returns.index:
0080          signal_month = target_month - 1
0081          history = macro.loc[: signal_month.to_timestamp("M")]
0082          if len(history) < 24:
0083              continue
0084          features = history.iloc[-1]
0085          rows.append(
0086              {
0087                  "target_month": target_month,
0088                  "signal_month": signal_month,
0089                  "growth_level": float(
0090                      features["growth"][["GDP_level", "Export_level", "BSI_level"]].mean()
0091                  ),
0092                  "growth_d3": float(
0093                      features["growth"][
0094                          ["GDP_level_d3", "Export_level_d3", "BSI_level_d3"]
0095                      ].mean()
0096                  ),
0097                  "inflation_level": float(
0098                      features["inflation"][
0099                          ["CPI_level", "PPI_level", "ImportPrice_level"]
0100                      ].mean()
0101                  ),
0102                  "inflation_d3": float(
0103                      features["inflation"][
0104                          ["CPI_level_d3", "PPI_level_d3", "ImportPrice_level_d3"]
0105                      ].mean()
0106                  ),
0107                  # The probability function has one shared interface. These
0108                  # placeholders are deliberately ignored when sjm_weight=0.
0109                  "growth_sjm": 0.5,
0110                  "inflation_sjm": 0.5,
0111              }
0112          )
0113      components = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("target_month")
0114      components.index = pd.PeriodIndex(components.index, freq="M")
0115      return components
0116  
0117  
0118  def build_no_sjm_signals(
0119      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0120  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0121      """Keep the transparent macro composite and set the SJM contribution to zero."""
0122      components = build_no_sjm_components(returns)
0123      probabilities = _macro_probabilities(
0124          components,
0125          d3_weight=0.20,
0126          sigmoid_scale=0.55,
0127          sjm_weight=0.0,
0128          current_weight=0.85,
0129      )
0130      signals = build_macro_signals(probabilities, components)
0131      assert (signals["signal_month"] < signals.index).all()
0132      assert np.isfinite(probabilities.to_numpy(dtype=float)).all()
0133      return signals, probabilities

0136  def _daily_asset_returns() -> pd.DataFrame:
0137      market = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "cache" / "market_daily.csv", parse_dates=["date"])
0138      levels = market.pivot_table(
0139          index="date", columns="symbol", values="close", aggfunc="last"
0140      ).sort_index()
0141      levels["GLD"] = levels["GLD"] * levels["USDKRW"]
0142      levels["USO"] = levels["USO"] * levels["USDKRW"]
0143      bond = pd.read_csv(ROOT / "raw_data" / "krx_bond_index.csv", encoding="cp949")
0144      bond.index = pd.to_datetime(bond.iloc[:, 0])
0145      bond_level = pd.to_numeric(
0146          bond.iloc[:, 1].astype(str).str.replace(",", "", regex=False),
0147          errors="coerce",
0148      )
0149      daily_levels = pd.concat(
0150          [
0151              levels["KODEX200"],
0152              bond_level.rename("BOND"),
0153              levels["GLD"],
0154              levels["USO"],
0155          ],
0156          axis=1,
0157      ).sort_index()
0158      daily_levels.columns = ASSETS
0159      daily_levels = daily_levels.reindex(
0160          pd.date_range(daily_levels.index.min(), daily_levels.index.max(), freq="B")
0161      ).ffill(limit=5)
0162      return daily_levels.pct_change(fill_method=None).replace([np.inf, -np.inf], np.nan)

0164  
0165  def build_domestic_features(
0166      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0167      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0168  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0169      """Rebuild the 12 deployed domestic inputs under the no-SJM regime path."""
0170      daily_returns = _daily_asset_returns()
0171      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0172      for month in signals.index.intersection(returns.index):
0173          history = returns.loc[returns.index < month, ASSETS]
0174          if len(history) < 3:
0175              continue
0176          signal = signals.loc[month]
0177          base = hard_regime_weights(signal)
0178          proxy = pd.Series(history.to_numpy(dtype=float) @ base, index=history.index)
0179          cutoff = month.to_timestamp(how="start") - pd.Timedelta(days=2)
0180          daily_history = daily_returns.loc[:cutoff, ASSETS].dropna(how="all")
0181          daily_proxy = pd.Series(
0182              daily_history.fillna(0.0).to_numpy(dtype=float) @ base,
0183              index=daily_history.index,
0184          )
0185  
0186          row: dict[str, object] = {
0187              "month": month,
0188              "base_KODEX200": float(base[0]),
0189              "base_BOND": float(base[1]),
0190              "base_GLD": float(base[2]),
0191              "base_USO": float(base[3]),
0192              "p_inflation_high": float(signal["p_inflation_high"]),
0193              "proxy_mom1": float((1 + proxy.tail(1)).prod() - 1),
0194              "proxy_mom6": float((1 + proxy.tail(6)).prod() - 1),
0195              "proxy_vol6": float(proxy.tail(6).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(12)),
0196              "daily_mom21": float((1 + daily_proxy.tail(21)).prod() - 1),
0197              "daily_mom252": float((1 + daily_proxy.tail(252)).prod() - 1),
0198              "daily_vol21": float(daily_proxy.tail(21).std(ddof=1) * math.sqrt(252)),
0199              "daily_downvol21": float(
0200                  np.sqrt(np.mean(np.minimum(daily_proxy.tail(21), 0.0) ** 2))
0201                  * math.sqrt(252)
0202              ),
0203          }
0204          correlation = daily_history.tail(63)[["KODEX200", "GLD", "USO"]].corr()
0205          values = correlation.to_numpy()[np.triu_indices(3, 1)]
0206          row["daily_mean_corr63"] = float(np.nanmean(values))
0207          rows.append(row)
0208  
0209      output = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")
0210      output.index = pd.PeriodIndex(output.index, freq="M")
0211      missing = [column for column in DOMESTIC_FEATURES if column not in output]
0212      if missing:
0213          raise ValueError(f"Missing domestic inputs: {missing}")
0214      return output

0216  
0217  def run_neutral_factor_blend(
0218      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0219      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0220      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0221      cost_multiplier: float = 1.0,
0222  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0223      """Reproduce the fixed 40% hard + 60% SLSQP, 1.2x baseline used by the label."""
0224      months = signals.index.intersection(returns.index).intersection(defensive.index)
0225      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0226      pretrade = np.zeros(len(ASSETS))
0227      first_trade = True
0228      nav = 1.0
0229      peak = 1.0
0230      for month in months:
0231          hard = hard_regime_weights(signals.loc[month])
0232          defensive_weights = defensive.loc[
0233              month, [f"w_{asset}" for asset in ASSETS]
0234          ].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0235          unlevered = 0.40 * hard + 0.60 * defensive_weights
0236          weights = 1.20 * unlevered
0237          debt_weight = -0.20
0238          delta = weights - pretrade
0239          turnover = np.abs(delta).sum() if first_trade else 0.5 * np.abs(delta).sum()
0240          trade_cost = np.abs(delta).sum() * 0.0015 * cost_multiplier
0241          fx_cost = (
0242              abs((weights[2] + weights[3]) - (pretrade[2] + pretrade[3]))
0243              * 0.0005
0244              * cost_multiplier
0245          )
0246          financing = debt_weight * ((1 + 0.04) ** (1 / 12) - 1)
0247          asset_return = returns.loc[month, ASSETS].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0248          gross_return = float(weights @ asset_return + financing)
0249          net_return = gross_return - trade_cost - fx_cost
0250          nav *= 1 + net_return
0251          peak = max(peak, nav)
0252          pretrade = weights * (1 + asset_return) / (1 + gross_return)
0253          first_trade = False
0254          rows.append(
0255              {
0256                  "month": month,
0257                  "return": net_return,
0258                  "gross_return": gross_return,
0259                  "nav": nav,
0260                  "drawdown": nav / peak - 1,
0261                  "turnover": float(turnover),
0262                  "trade_cost": float(trade_cost),
0263                  "fx_cost": float(fx_cost),
0264                  **{f"w_{asset}": float(weights[index]) for index, asset in enumerate(ASSETS)},
0265              }
0266          )
0267      return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")
0268  
0269  
0270  def forward_path_loss(returns: pd.Series, horizon: int = 2) -> pd.Series:
0271      output = pd.Series(np.nan, index=returns.index, dtype=float)
0272      for position in range(len(returns)):
0273          future = returns.iloc[position : position + horizon]
0274          if len(future) == horizon:
0275              output.iloc[position] = float(((1 + future).cumprod() - 1).min())
0276      return output
0277  
0278  
0279  def balanced_logistic_spec() -> dict[str, object]:
0280      return {
0281          "candidate": "l2_liblinear_c0.1_balanced",
0282          "penalty": "l2",
0283          "solver": "liblinear",
0284          "C": 0.10,
0285          "class_weight": "balanced",
0286          "l1_ratio": np.nan,
0287      }
0288  
0289  
0290  def prediction_metrics(
0291      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0292      start: pd.Period | None,
0293      end: pd.Period | None,
0294  ) -> dict[str, float | int]:
0295      view = factor
0296      if start is not None:
0297          view = view.loc[start:]
0298      if end is not None:
0299          view = view.loc[:end]
0300      view = view.dropna(subset=["p_tail_raw", "tail_event"])
0301      y = view["tail_event"].astype(int)
0302      probability = view["p_tail_raw"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0303      return {
0304          "observations": int(len(view)),
0305          "events": int(y.sum()),
0306          "event_rate": float(y.mean()),
0307          "roc_auc": float(roc_auc_score(y, probability)) if y.nunique() > 1 else np.nan,
0308          "average_precision": (
0309              float(average_precision_score(y, probability)) if y.nunique() > 1 else np.nan
0310          ),
0311          "brier_score": float(brier_score_loss(y, probability)),
0312      }
0313  
0314  
0315  def metric_record(
0316      period: str,
0317      strategy: str,
0318      path: pd.DataFrame,
0319      start: pd.Period | None,
0320      end: pd.Period | None,
0321  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0322      view = path
0323      if start is not None:
0324          view = view.loc[start:]
0325      if end is not None:
0326          view = view.loc[:end]
0327      metrics = performance_summary(view["return"])
0328      return {
0329          "Period": period,
0330          "Strategy": strategy,
0331          "Months": int(len(view)),
0332          **{key: float(value) for key, value in metrics.to_dict().items()},
0333          "AvgTurnover": float(view["turnover"].mean()) if "turnover" in view else np.nan,
0334      }
0335  
0336  
0337  def fixed_robust_overlay(
0338      reference: pd.DataFrame,
0339  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0340      levels = load_daily_open_levels()
0341      arrays = prepare_arrays(levels, reference, build_daily_vkospi_signals())
0342      features = align_features_to_arrays(build_robust_daily_features(), arrays)
0343      deployed_report = json.loads(
0344          (RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
0345      )
0346      config = RobustStressConfig(**deployed_report["winner"])
0347      stress = stress_from_features(
0348          features,
0349          config.mode,
0350          config.level_threshold,
0351          config.shock_threshold,
0352      )
0353      _, neutral_monthly = simulate(arrays, None, keep_daily=False)
0354      daily, overlay_monthly = simulate(
0355          arrays,
0356          config.dynamic_config(),
0357          keep_daily=True,
0358          stress_override=stress,
0359      )
0360      reconciled = reconcile_to_monthly_reference(
0361          reference,
0362          neutral_monthly,
0363          overlay_monthly,
0364      )
0365      valid_signal = daily["signal_date"].notna()
0366      assert (
0367          daily.index[valid_signal].to_numpy()
0368          > pd.DatetimeIndex(daily.loc[valid_signal, "signal_date"]).to_numpy()
0369      ).all()
0370      return daily, overlay_monthly, reconciled
0371  
0372  
0373  def audit_deployed_reproduction(returns: pd.DataFrame) -> dict[str, float | int]:
0374      """Prove that the variant pipeline changes SJM, not unrelated implementation details."""
0375      signals = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "regime_signals.csv", index_col=0)
0376      signals.index = pd.PeriodIndex(signals.index, freq="M")
0377      defensive = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "proposed_backtest.csv", index_col=0)
0378      defensive.index = pd.PeriodIndex(defensive.index, freq="M")
0379      rebuilt_features = build_domestic_features(signals, returns)
0380      stored_features = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0381      stored_features.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_features.index, freq="M")
0382      feature_index = rebuilt_features.index.intersection(stored_features.index)
0383      feature_difference = (
0384          rebuilt_features.loc[feature_index, DOMESTIC_FEATURES]
0385          - stored_features.loc[feature_index, DOMESTIC_FEATURES]
0386      ).abs()
0387  
0388      composites = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0389      composites.index = pd.PeriodIndex(composites.index, freq="M")
0390      neutral = run_neutral_factor_blend(returns, signals, defensive)
0391      data = rebuilt_features[DOMESTIC_FEATURES].join(
0392          composites[OAP_COMPOSITES], how="left"
0393      )
0394      data = data.loc[data.index.intersection(neutral.index)].copy()
0395      path_loss = forward_path_loss(neutral.loc[data.index, "return"], horizon=2)
0396      data["tail_event"] = (path_loss < -0.05).where(path_loss.notna()).astype(float)
0397      probability, _ = fit_logistic_candidate(data, balanced_logistic_spec())
0398      factor = make_tail_factor(probability, data["tail_event"])
0399      stored_factor = pd.read_csv(
0400          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0401      )
0402      stored_factor.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_factor.index, freq="M")
0403      factor_index = factor.index.intersection(stored_factor.index)
0404      label_view = pd.concat(
0405          [factor.loc[factor_index, "tail_event"], stored_factor.loc[factor_index, "tail_event"]],
0406          axis=1,
0407      ).dropna()
0408      probability_view = pd.concat(
0409          [
0410              factor.loc[factor_index, "p_tail_raw"],
0411              stored_factor.loc[factor_index, "p_tail_raw"],
0412          ],
0413          axis=1,
0414      ).dropna()
0415  
0416      medium = run_factor_vol_target(
0417          returns,
0418          signals,
0419          defensive,
0420          factor,
0421          max_shift=0.20,
0422          target_vol=0.15,
0423      )
0424      stored_medium = pd.read_csv(
0425          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_backtest.csv", index_col=0
0426      )
0427      stored_medium.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_medium.index, freq="M")
0428      medium_index = medium.index.intersection(stored_medium.index)
0429      _, _, final = fixed_robust_overlay(medium)
0430      stored_final = pd.read_csv(
0431          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col=0
0432      )
0433      stored_final.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_final.index, freq="M")
0434      final_index = final.index.intersection(stored_final.index)
0435  
0436      audit = {
0437          "feature_observations": int(len(feature_index)),
0438          "max_absolute_domestic_feature_difference": float(
0439              feature_difference.to_numpy(dtype=float).max()
0440          ),
0441          "label_observations": int(len(label_view)),
0442          "label_disagreements": int(
0443              (label_view.iloc[:, 0] != label_view.iloc[:, 1]).sum()
0444          ),
0445          "probability_observations": int(len(probability_view)),
0446          "max_absolute_probability_difference": float(
0447              (probability_view.iloc[:, 0] - probability_view.iloc[:, 1]).abs().max()
0448          ),
0449          "medium_return_observations": int(len(medium_index)),
0450          "max_absolute_medium_return_difference": float(
0451              (
0452                  medium.loc[medium_index, "return"]
0453                  - stored_medium.loc[medium_index, "return"]
0454              ).abs().max()
0455          ),
0456          "final_return_observations": int(len(final_index)),
0457          "max_absolute_final_return_difference": float(
0458              (
0459                  final.loc[final_index, "return"]
0460                  - stored_final.loc[final_index, "return"]
0461              ).abs().max()
0462          ),
0463      }
0464      assert audit["feature_observations"] > 0
0465      assert audit["max_absolute_domestic_feature_difference"] < 1e-12
0466      assert audit["label_disagreements"] == 0
0467      assert audit["max_absolute_probability_difference"] < 1e-12
0468      assert audit["max_absolute_medium_return_difference"] < 1e-12
0469      assert audit["max_absolute_final_return_difference"] < 1e-12
0470      return audit
0471  
0472  
0473  def main() -> None:
0474      returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns(False)
0475      reproduction_audit = audit_deployed_reproduction(returns)
0476      signals, macro_probabilities = build_no_sjm_signals(returns)
0477      defensive = run_backtest(returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
0478      domestic = build_domestic_features(signals, returns)
0479      composites = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0480      composites.index = pd.PeriodIndex(composites.index, freq="M")
0481  
0482      neutral_baseline = run_neutral_factor_blend(returns, signals, defensive)
0483      data = domestic[DOMESTIC_FEATURES].join(composites[OAP_COMPOSITES], how="left")
0484      data = data.loc[data.index.intersection(neutral_baseline.index)].copy()
0485      path_loss = forward_path_loss(neutral_baseline.loc[data.index, "return"], horizon=2)
0486      data["tail_event"] = (path_loss < -0.05).where(path_loss.notna()).astype(float)
0487      probability, fit_stats = fit_logistic_candidate(data, balanced_logistic_spec())
0488      factor = make_tail_factor(probability, data["tail_event"])
0489      medium = run_factor_vol_target(
0490          returns,
0491          signals,
0492          defensive,
0493          factor,
0494          max_shift=0.20,
0495          target_vol=0.15,
0496      )
0497      final_daily, final_monthly, final_reconciled = fixed_robust_overlay(medium)
0498  
0499      deployed_medium = pd.read_csv(
0500          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_backtest.csv", index_col=0
0501      )
0502      deployed_medium.index = pd.PeriodIndex(deployed_medium.index, freq="M")
0503      deployed_final = pd.read_csv(
0504          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col=0
0505      )
0506      deployed_final.index = pd.PeriodIndex(deployed_final.index, freq="M")
0507      deployed_proposed = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "proposed_backtest.csv", index_col=0)
0508      deployed_proposed.index = pd.PeriodIndex(deployed_proposed.index, freq="M")
0509  
0510      paths = {
0511          "NoSJM_ProposedBase": defensive,
0512          "Deployed_SJM10_ProposedBase": deployed_proposed,
0513          "NoSJM_BalancedLogisticMedium": medium,
0514          "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogisticMedium": deployed_medium,
0515          "NoSJM_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI": final_reconciled,
0516          "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI": deployed_final,
0517      }
0518      rows = [
0519          metric_record(period, strategy, path, start, end)
0520          for period, start, end in PERIODS
0521          for strategy, path in paths.items()
0522      ]
0523      comparison = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0524  
0525      locked_current = deployed_final.loc[TEST_START:, "return"]
0526      locked_variant = final_reconciled.loc[TEST_START:, "return"]
0527      common = locked_current.index.intersection(locked_variant.index)
0528      bootstrap = paired_multiobjective_bootstrap(
0529          locked_current.loc[common],
0530          locked_variant.loc[common],
0531      )
0532      locked_rows = comparison.loc[
0533          comparison["Period"].eq("locked_2018_2026")
0534          & comparison["Strategy"].isin(
0535              [
0536                  "NoSJM_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI",
0537                  "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI",
0538              ]
0539          )
0540      ].set_index("Strategy")
0541      variant_locked = locked_rows.loc["NoSJM_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI"]
0542      deployed_locked = locked_rows.loc[
0543          "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI"
0544      ]
0545      locked_delta = {
0546          metric: float(variant_locked[metric] - deployed_locked[metric])
0547          for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0548      }
0549  
0550      prelock_deltas: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
0551      for period in ("calibration_2007_2017", "validation_2013_2017"):
0552          period_rows = comparison.loc[
0553              comparison["Period"].eq(period)
0554              & comparison["Strategy"].isin(
0555                  [
0556                      "NoSJM_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI",
0557                      "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI",
0558                  ]
0559              )
0560          ].set_index("Strategy")
0561          variant_row = period_rows.loc["NoSJM_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI"]
0562          deployed_row = period_rows.loc[
0563              "Deployed_SJM10_BalancedLogistic_RobustVKOSPI"
0564          ]
0565          prelock_deltas[period] = {
0566              metric: float(variant_row[metric] - deployed_row[metric])
0567              for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD")
0568          }
0569      strict_prelock_pass = all(
0570          values["CAGR"] > 0 and values["Sharpe"] > 0 and values["MDD"] >= 0
0571          for values in prelock_deltas.values()
0572      )
0573  
0574      stored_factor = pd.read_csv(
0575          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0576      )
0577      stored_factor.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_factor.index, freq="M")
0578      label_view = factor[["tail_event", "p_tail_raw"]].join(
0579          stored_factor[["tail_event", "p_tail_raw"]],
0580          how="inner",
0581          lsuffix="_no_sjm",
0582          rsuffix="_deployed",
0583      )
0584      comparable_labels = label_view.dropna(subset=["tail_event_no_sjm", "tail_event_deployed"])
0585      comparable_probability = label_view.dropna(subset=["p_tail_raw_no_sjm", "p_tail_raw_deployed"])
0586  
0587      signals.to_csv(SIGNALS_PATH)
0588      domestic.to_csv(FEATURES_PATH)
0589      factor.to_csv(FACTOR_PATH)
0590      medium.to_csv(MEDIUM_PATH)
0591      final_daily.to_csv(FINAL_DAILY_PATH)
0592      final_monthly.to_csv(FINAL_MONTHLY_PATH)
0593      final_reconciled.to_csv(FINAL_RECONCILED_PATH)
0594      comparison.to_csv(COMPARISON_PATH, index=False)
0595  
0596      report = {
0597          "implementation": {
0598              "macro": {
0599                  "d3_weight": 0.20,
0600                  "sigmoid_scale": 0.55,
0601                  "sjm_weight": 0.0,
0602                  "current_weight": 0.85,
0603                  "features": [
0604                      "GDP_level",
0605                      "GDP_level_d3",
0606                      "Export_level",
0607                      "Export_level_d3",
0608                      "BSI_level",
0609                      "BSI_level_d3",
0610                      "CPI_level",
0611                      "CPI_level_d3",
0612                      "PPI_level",
0613                      "PPI_level_d3",
0614                      "ImportPrice_level",
0615                      "ImportPrice_level_d3",
0616                  ],
0617              },
0618              "logistic": {
0619                  **balanced_logistic_spec(),
0620                  "l1_ratio": None,
0621                  "features": TAIL_FEATURES,
0622                  "minimum_training_months": 36,
0623                  "embargo_months": 2,
0624                  "positive_guard": 4,
0625                  "negative_guard": 12,
0626                  "target": "two-month forward path minimum below -5%",
0627                  "max_shift": 0.20,
0628                  "target_vol": 0.15,
0629                  "fit_stats": fit_stats,
0630              },
0631              "vkospi_overlay": json.loads(
0632                  (RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(
0633                      encoding="utf-8"
0634                  )
0635              )["winner"],
0636          },
0637          "data": {
0638              "signal_months": int(len(signals)),
0639              "feature_months": int(len(domestic)),
0640              "tail_events_calibration": int(data.loc[:CAL_END, "tail_event"].sum()),
0641              "tail_events_locked": int(data.loc[TEST_START:, "tail_event"].sum()),
0642              "label_comparison_observations": int(len(comparable_labels)),
0643              "label_agreement_with_deployed": float(
0644                  (
0645                      comparable_labels["tail_event_no_sjm"]
0646                      == comparable_labels["tail_event_deployed"]
0647                  ).mean()
0648              ),
0649              "probability_comparison_observations": int(len(comparable_probability)),
0650              "probability_correlation_with_deployed": float(
0651                  comparable_probability[
0652                      ["p_tail_raw_no_sjm", "p_tail_raw_deployed"]
0653                  ].corr().iloc[0, 1]
0654              ),
0655          },
0656          "prediction": {
0657              "calibration_2007_2017": prediction_metrics(factor, None, CAL_END),
0658              "validation_2013_2017": prediction_metrics(
0659                  factor, VALIDATION_START, CAL_END
0660              ),
0661              "locked_2018_2026": prediction_metrics(factor, TEST_START, None),
0662          },
0663          "locked_final": {
0664              "variant": {
0665                  key: float(variant_locked[key])
0666                  for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0667              },
0668              "deployed": {
0669                  key: float(deployed_locked[key])
0670                  for key in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar")
0671              },
0672              "delta_variant_minus_deployed": locked_delta,
0673              "bootstrap": bootstrap,
0674          },
0675          "prelock_gate": {
0676              "deltas_variant_minus_deployed": prelock_deltas,
0677              "requires_cagr_sharpe_mdd_improve_in_both_windows": True,
0678              "passes": bool(strict_prelock_pass),
0679          },
0680          "macro_probability_range": {
0681              "growth_min": float(macro_probabilities["growth"].min()),
0682              "growth_max": float(macro_probabilities["growth"].max()),
0683              "inflation_min": float(macro_probabilities["inflation"].min()),
0684              "inflation_max": float(macro_probabilities["inflation"].max()),
0685          },
0686          "deployed_reproduction_audit": reproduction_audit,
0687          "comparison": json.loads(comparison.to_json(orient="records")),
0688          "selection_note": (
0689              "This is a post-lock ablation requested by the user. All downstream "
0690              "features and labels are recomputed, but locked results are not used "
0691              "to tune C, class weighting, thresholds, or the VKOSPI overlay."
0692          ),
0693          "deployment_status": (
0694              "Implemented as an isolated candidate with separate result files. "
0695              "Existing deployed result files were not overwritten."
0696          ),
0697      }
0698      REPORT_PATH.write_text(
0699          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0700      )
0701  
0702      print("=== BALANCED LOGISTIC + NO SJM ===")
0703      print("deployed reproduction", json.dumps(reproduction_audit, indent=2))
0704      print(json.dumps(report["locked_final"], ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
0705      print("\n=== PERIOD COMPARISON ===")
0706      print(
0707          comparison[
0708              ["Period", "Strategy", "Months", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar"]
0709          ].to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.6f}")
0710      )
0711      print("\nsaved", REPORT_PATH)
0712  
0713  
0714  if __name__ == "__main__":
0715      main()

0013  
0014  def diagnostic_components(
0015      features: pd.DataFrame, level_threshold: float, shock_threshold: float
0016  ) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
0017      percentile = features["percentile_252"].fillna(features["percentile_126"])
0018      level = np.clip(
0019          (percentile.to_numpy(dtype=float) - level_threshold)
0020          / max(1 - level_threshold, 1e-6),
0021          0,
0022          1,
0023      )
0024      shock = np.clip(
0025          (features["shock_5"].to_numpy(dtype=float) - shock_threshold) / 2.5,
0026          0,
0027          1,
0028      )
0029      acceleration = np.clip(
0030          (features["acceleration_z5"].to_numpy(dtype=float) - shock_threshold) / 2.5,
0031          0,
0032          1,
0033      )
0034      level_shock = np.nan_to_num((0.40 * level + 0.35 * shock) / 0.75).clip(0, 1)
0035      selected = np.nan_to_num((0.40 * level + 0.35 * shock + 0.25 * acceleration).clip(0, 1))
0036      return {
0037          "LevelOnly": np.nan_to_num(level).clip(0, 1),
0038          "ShockOnly": np.nan_to_num(shock).clip(0, 1),
0039          "AccelerationOnly": np.nan_to_num(acceleration).clip(0, 1),
0040          "LevelShockRenormalized": level_shock,
0041          "SelectedAccelerationBlend": selected,
0042      }
0043  
0044  
0045  def performance_row(
0046      experiment: str, period: str, monthly: pd.DataFrame
0047  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0048      metrics = base.performance_summary(monthly["return"])
0049      return {
0050          "Experiment": experiment,
0051          "Period": period,
0052          **metrics.to_dict(),
0053          "AvgTurnover": float(monthly["turnover"].mean()),
0054          "AvgStress": float(monthly["avg_stress"].mean()),
0055          "AvgTransfer": float(monthly["avg_transfer"].mean()),
0056      }

0058  
0059  def main() -> None:
0060      reference = base.load_reference_weights()
0061      arrays = base.prepare_arrays(
0062          base.load_daily_open_levels(), reference, base.build_daily_vkospi_signals()
0063      )
0064      features = robust.align_features_to_arrays(
0065          robust.build_robust_daily_features(), arrays
0066      )
0067      old_report = json.loads(
0068          (RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
0069      )
0070      new_report = json.loads(
0071          (RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(
0072              encoding="utf-8"
0073          )
0074      )
0075      old_config = base.DynamicRiskConfig(**old_report["winner"])
0076      winner_spec = robust.RobustStressConfig(**new_report["winner"])
0077      winner_config = winner_spec.dynamic_config()
0078      components = diagnostic_components(
0079          features, winner_spec.level_threshold, winner_spec.shock_threshold
0080      )
0081      selected_stress = components["SelectedAccelerationBlend"]
0082  
0083      _, neutral = base.simulate(arrays, None, keep_daily=False)
0084      old_daily, old_actual = base.simulate(arrays, old_config, keep_daily=True)
0085  
0086      step_configs = {
0087          "1_ExistingDynamic": (old_config, None),
0088          "2_RobustSignal_OldPolicy": (
0089              replace(winner_config, max_risk_transfer=0.25, rebalance_band=0.15),
0090              selected_stress,
0091          ),
0092          "3_RobustSignal_Transfer35": (
0093              replace(winner_config, max_risk_transfer=0.35, rebalance_band=0.15),
0094              selected_stress,
0095          ),
0096          "4_RobustWinner_Band20": (winner_config, selected_stress),
0097      }
0098      reconciled_paths: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {}
0099      daily_paths: dict[str, pd.DataFrame] = {"1_ExistingDynamic": old_daily}
0100      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0101      for name, (config, stress) in step_configs.items():
0102          if name == "1_ExistingDynamic":
0103              actual = old_actual
0104              daily = old_daily
0105          else:
0106              daily, actual = base.simulate(
0107                  arrays, config, keep_daily=True, stress_override=stress
0108              )
0109          daily_paths[name] = daily
0110          reconciled = base.reconcile_to_monthly_reference(reference, neutral, actual)
0111          reconciled_paths[name] = reconciled
0112          for period, start, end in (
0113              ("locked_2018_2026", base.TEST_START, reference.index.max()),
0114              ("full_2007_2026", reference.index.min(), reference.index.max()),
0115          ):
0116              rows.append(performance_row(name, period, reconciled.loc[start:end]))
0117  
0118      component_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0119      for name, stress in components.items():
0120          _, actual = base.simulate(
0121              arrays, winner_config, keep_daily=False, stress_override=stress
0122          )
0123          reconciled = base.reconcile_to_monthly_reference(reference, neutral, actual)
0124          for period, start, end in (
0125              ("calibration_2007_2017", reference.index.min(), base.CAL_END),
0126              ("locked_2018_2026", base.TEST_START, reference.index.max()),
0127          ):
0128              component_rows.append(
0129                  performance_row(name, period, reconciled.loc[start:end])
0130              )
0131  
0132      old_locked_daily = daily_paths["1_ExistingDynamic"].loc[
0133          daily_paths["1_ExistingDynamic"].index.to_period("M") >= base.TEST_START
0134      ]
0135      new_locked_daily = daily_paths["4_RobustWinner_Band20"].loc[
0136          daily_paths["4_RobustWinner_Band20"].index.to_period("M") >= base.TEST_START
0137      ]
0138      signal_stats = pd.DataFrame(
0139          {
0140              "ExistingDynamic": {
0141                  "TradingDays": len(old_locked_daily),
0142                  "StressPositiveDays": int((old_locked_daily["stress"] > 0).sum()),
0143                  "StressAbove025Days": int((old_locked_daily["stress"] >= 0.25).sum()),
0144                  "TransferPositiveDays": int(
0145                      (old_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"] > 0).sum()
0146                  ),
0147                  "AverageStress": float(old_locked_daily["stress"].mean()),
0148                  "MaximumStress": float(old_locked_daily["stress"].max()),
0149                  "AverageTransfer": float(
0150                      old_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"].mean()
0151                  ),
0152                  "MaximumTransfer": float(
0153                      old_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"].max()
0154                  ),
0155                  "AverageTurnover": float(old_locked_daily["turnover"].mean()),
0156              },
0157              "RobustWinner": {
0158                  "TradingDays": len(new_locked_daily),
0159                  "StressPositiveDays": int((new_locked_daily["stress"] > 0).sum()),
0160                  "StressAbove025Days": int((new_locked_daily["stress"] >= 0.25).sum()),
0161                  "TransferPositiveDays": int(
0162                      (new_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"] > 0).sum()
0163                  ),
0164                  "AverageStress": float(new_locked_daily["stress"].mean()),
0165                  "MaximumStress": float(new_locked_daily["stress"].max()),
0166                  "AverageTransfer": float(
0167                      new_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"].mean()
0168                  ),
0169                  "MaximumTransfer": float(
0170                      new_locked_daily["transfer_fraction"].max()
0171                  ),
0172                  "AverageTurnover": float(new_locked_daily["turnover"].mean()),
0173              },
0174          }
0175      ).rename_axis("Statistic").reset_index()
0176  
0177      old_monthly = reconciled_paths["1_ExistingDynamic"].loc[base.TEST_START:].copy()
0178      new_monthly = reconciled_paths["4_RobustWinner_Band20"].loc[base.TEST_START:].copy()
0179      common = old_monthly.index.intersection(new_monthly.index)
0180      contributions = pd.DataFrame(index=common)
0181      contributions["ExistingReturn"] = old_monthly.loc[common, "return"]
0182      contributions["RobustReturn"] = new_monthly.loc[common, "return"]
0183      contributions["ReturnDelta"] = (
0184          contributions["RobustReturn"] - contributions["ExistingReturn"]
0185      )
0186      contributions["RelativeFactor"] = (
0187          (1 + contributions["RobustReturn"]) / (1 + contributions["ExistingReturn"])
0188      )
0189      contributions["CumulativeRelative"] = contributions["RelativeFactor"].cumprod() - 1
0190      contributions["ExistingAvgStress"] = old_monthly.loc[common, "avg_stress"]
0191      contributions["RobustAvgStress"] = new_monthly.loc[common, "avg_stress"]
0192      contributions["ExistingAvgTransfer"] = old_monthly.loc[common, "avg_transfer"]
0193      contributions["RobustAvgTransfer"] = new_monthly.loc[common, "avg_transfer"]
0194      contributions.index.name = "Month"
0195  
0196      pd.DataFrame(rows).to_csv(
0197          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_stepwise_attribution.csv", index=False
0198      )
0199      pd.DataFrame(component_rows).to_csv(
0200          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_component_ablation.csv", index=False
0201      )
0202      signal_stats.to_csv(
0203          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_signal_statistics.csv", index=False
0204      )
0205      contributions.to_csv(
0206          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_monthly_contribution.csv"
0207      )
0208      print("\n=== STEPWISE LOCKED ===")
0209      print(
0210          pd.DataFrame(rows)
0211          .loc[lambda frame: frame["Period"] == "locked_2018_2026"]
0212          [["Experiment", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar", "AvgTurnover"]]
0213          .to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.5f}")
0214      )
0215      print("\n=== COMPONENT LOCKED ===")
0216      print(
0217          pd.DataFrame(component_rows)
0218          .loc[lambda frame: frame["Period"] == "locked_2018_2026"]
0219          [["Experiment", "CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD", "Calmar"]]
0220          .to_string(index=False, float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.5f}")
0221      )
0222      print("\n=== SIGNAL STATISTICS ===")
0223      print(signal_stats.to_string(index=False))
0224      print("\n=== TOP/BOTTOM MONTHS ===")
0225      ranked = contributions.sort_values("ReturnDelta")
0226      print(
0227          pd.concat([ranked.head(5), ranked.tail(5)])[
0228              ["ExistingReturn", "RobustReturn", "ReturnDelta", "RobustAvgStress"]
0229          ].to_string(float_format=lambda value: f"{value:.4f}")
0230      )
0231  
0232  
0233  if __name__ == "__main__":
0234      main()

0053  
0054  def make_model() -> Pipeline:
0055      """Reproduce the deployed balanced L2 logistic pipeline without heavy imports."""
0056      return Pipeline(
0057          [
0058              ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median")),
0059              ("scale", StandardScaler()),
0060              (
0061                  "model",
0062                  LogisticRegression(
0063                      C=0.1,
0064                      class_weight="balanced",
0065                      max_iter=2000,
0066                      solver="liblinear",
0067                  ),
0068              ),
0069          ]
0070      )

0072  
0073  def _metric_or_nan(function, y: pd.Series, values: pd.Series) -> float:
0074      if len(y) == 0 or y.nunique() < 2:
0075          return float("nan")
0076      return float(function(y, values))
0077  
0078  
0079  def _ece(y: pd.Series, probability: pd.Series, bins: int = 5) -> float:
0080      frame = pd.DataFrame({"y": y, "p": probability}).dropna()
0081      if frame.empty:
0082          return float("nan")
0083      labels = pd.cut(frame["p"], np.linspace(0, 1, bins + 1), include_lowest=True)
0084      value = 0.0
0085      for _, group in frame.groupby(labels, observed=True):
0086          if len(group):
0087              value += len(group) / len(frame) * abs(group["y"].mean() - group["p"].mean())
0088      return float(value)
0089  
0090  
0091  def _macro_components() -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0092      macro, core = load_macro_data()
0093      signals = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "regime_signals.csv", index_col=0)
0094      signals.index = pd.PeriodIndex(signals.index, freq="M")
0095      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period]] = []
0096      for target_month, signal in signals.iterrows():
0097          signal_month = pd.Period(str(signal["signal_month"]), freq="M")
0098          features = macro.loc[signal_month.to_timestamp("M")]
0099          rows.append(
0100              {
0101                  "target_month": target_month,
0102                  "signal_month": signal_month,
0103                  "growth_level": float(
0104                      features["growth"][["GDP_level", "Export_level", "BSI_level"]].mean()
0105                  ),
0106                  "growth_d3": float(
0107                      features["growth"][["GDP_level_d3", "Export_level_d3", "BSI_level_d3"]].mean()
0108                  ),
0109                  "inflation_level": float(
0110                      features["inflation"][["CPI_level", "PPI_level", "ImportPrice_level"]].mean()
0111                  ),
0112                  "inflation_d3": float(
0113                      features["inflation"][["CPI_level_d3", "PPI_level_d3", "ImportPrice_level_d3"]].mean()
0114                  ),
0115                  "growth_sjm": float(signal["p_growth_sjm"]),
0116                  "inflation_sjm": float(signal["p_inflation_sjm"]),
0117              }
0118          )
0119      components = pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("target_month")
0120  
0121      realized = pd.DataFrame(
0122          {
0123              "growth": core[["GDP", "Export", "BSI"]].mean(axis=1),
0124              "inflation": core[["CPI", "PPI", "ImportPrice"]].mean(axis=1),
0125          }
0126      )
0127      target = pd.DataFrame(index=realized.index, dtype=float)
0128      for name in ("growth", "inflation"):
0129          future = pd.concat([realized[name].shift(-step) for step in (1, 2, 3)], axis=1)
0130          target[name] = (future.mean(axis=1) >= 0).where(future.notna().sum(axis=1) == 3)
0131      return components, target
0132  
0133  
0134  def _macro_probabilities(
0135      components: pd.DataFrame,
0136      d3_weight: float,
0137      sigmoid_scale: float,
0138      sjm_weight: float,
0139      current_weight: float,
0140  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0141      output = pd.DataFrame(index=components.index, dtype=float)
0142      previous = {"growth": 0.5, "inflation": 0.5}
0143      for target_month, row in components.iterrows():
0144          for name in ("growth", "inflation"):
0145              composite = float(
0146                  expit((row[f"{name}_level"] + d3_weight * row[f"{name}_d3"]) / sigmoid_scale)
0147              )
0148              raw = sjm_weight * row[f"{name}_sjm"] + (1 - sjm_weight) * composite
0149              probability = current_weight * raw + (1 - current_weight) * previous[name]
0150              output.loc[target_month, name] = probability
0151              previous[name] = probability
0152      return output
0153  
0154  
0155  def _macro_metric_rows(
0156      probabilities: pd.DataFrame,
0157      components: pd.DataFrame,
0158      targets: pd.DataFrame,
0159      parameter: str,
0160      value: float,
0161      settings: dict[str, float],
0162  ) -> list[dict[str, object]]:
0163      joined = probabilities.copy()
0164      for name in ("growth", "inflation"):
0165          joined[f"y_{name}"] = [
0166              targets.loc[signal_month.to_timestamp("M"), name]
0167              if signal_month.to_timestamp("M") in targets.index
0168              else np.nan
0169              for signal_month in components["signal_month"]
0170          ]
0171      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0172      for period, mask in (
0173          ("calibration_through_2017", joined.index <= CAL_END),
0174          ("locked_2018_2026", joined.index >= TEST_START),
0175      ):
0176          view = joined.loc[mask].dropna()
0177          record: dict[str, object] = {
0178              "parameter": parameter,
0179              "value": value,
0180              "period": period,
0181              "observations": len(view),
0182              **settings,
0183          }
0184          for name in ("growth", "inflation"):
0185              y = view[f"y_{name}"].astype(int)
0186              p = view[name].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0187              record[f"{name}_auc"] = _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, y, p)
0188              record[f"{name}_brier"] = float(brier_score_loss(y, p))
0189              record[f"{name}_balanced_accuracy"] = float(
0190                  balanced_accuracy_score(y, p >= 0.5)
0191              )
0192          record["quadrant_accuracy"] = float(
0193              (
0194                  (view["growth"] >= 0.5) == view["y_growth"].astype(bool)
0195              ).mul((view["inflation"] >= 0.5) == view["y_inflation"].astype(bool)).mean()
0196          )
0197          record["mean_brier"] = float(
0198              (record["growth_brier"] + record["inflation_brier"]) / 2
0199          )
0200          rows.append(record)
0201      return rows
0202  
0203  
0204  def macro_constant_sensitivity() -> pd.DataFrame:
0205      components, targets = _macro_components()
0206      base = {
0207          "d3_weight": 0.20,
0208          "sigmoid_scale": 0.55,
0209          "sjm_weight": 0.10,
0210          "current_weight": 0.85,
0211      }
0212      variants: list[tuple[str, float, dict[str, float]]] = [("deployed", np.nan, base.copy())]
0213      grids = {
0214          "d3_weight": (0.0, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30, 0.40),
0215          "sigmoid_scale": (0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75),
0216          "sjm_weight": (0.0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15, 0.20),
0217          "current_weight": (0.70, 0.80, 0.85, 0.90, 1.00),
0218      }
0219      for parameter, values in grids.items():
0220          for value in values:
0221              if math.isclose(value, base[parameter]):
0222                  continue
0223              settings = base.copy()
0224              settings[parameter] = value
0225              variants.append((parameter, value, settings))
0226      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0227      for parameter, value, settings in variants:
0228          probabilities = _macro_probabilities(components, **settings)
0229          rows.extend(
0230              _macro_metric_rows(
0231                  probabilities,
0232                  components,
0233                  targets,
0234                  parameter,
0235                  value,
0236                  settings,
0237              )
0238          )
0239      return pd.DataFrame(rows)
0240  
0241  
0242  def _tail_data() -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame]:
0243      domestic = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0244      composites = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0245      factor = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0)
0246      for frame in (domestic, composites, factor):
0247          frame.index = pd.PeriodIndex(frame.index, freq="M")
0248      data = domestic[DOMESTIC_FEATURES].join(composites[OAP_COMPOSITES]).join(
0249          factor[["tail_event"]]
0250      )
0251      probability = pd.Series(np.nan, index=data.index, dtype=float)
0252      coefficient_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0253      template = make_model()
0254      for number, month in enumerate(data.index):
0255          train_end = number - 2
0256          if train_end < 36:
0257              continue
0258          train = data.iloc[:train_end].dropna(subset=["tail_event"])
0259          y = train["tail_event"].astype(int)
0260          if y.sum() < 4 or (len(y) - y.sum()) < 12:
0261              continue
0262          model = clone(template)
0263          model.fit(train[TAIL_FEATURES], y)
0264          probability.loc[month] = float(model.predict_proba(data.loc[[month], TAIL_FEATURES])[:, 1][0])
0265          coefficients = model.named_steps["model"].coef_[0]
0266          for feature, coefficient in zip(TAIL_FEATURES, coefficients):
0267              coefficient_rows.append(
0268                  {"month": month, "feature": feature, "coefficient": float(coefficient)}
0269              )
0270      coefficient_frame = pd.DataFrame(coefficient_rows)
0271      if not coefficient_frame.empty:
0272          coefficient_frame["month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(coefficient_frame["month"], freq="M")
0273      data["refit_probability"] = probability
0274      return data, coefficient_frame
0275  
0276  
0277  def tail_feature_diagnostics() -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, dict[str, float]]:
0278      data, coefficients = _tail_data()
0279      stored = pd.read_csv(
0280          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0281      )
0282      stored.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored.index, freq="M")
0283      comparison = data["refit_probability"].dropna().to_frame("refit").join(
0284          stored[["p_tail_raw"]].rename(columns={"p_tail_raw": "stored"}), how="inner"
0285      ).dropna()
0286      reproduction = {
0287          "observations": int(len(comparison)),
0288          "max_absolute_probability_difference": float(
0289              (comparison["refit"] - comparison["stored"]).abs().max()
0290          ),
0291      }
0292      feature_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0293      prediction_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0294      calibration_rate = float(
0295          data.loc[data.index <= CAL_END].dropna(subset=["refit_probability", "tail_event"])[
0296              "tail_event"
0297          ].mean()
0298      )
0299      for period, mask in (
0300          ("calibration_through_2017", data.index <= CAL_END),
0301          ("locked_2018_2026", data.index >= TEST_START),
0302      ):
0303          view = data.loc[mask].dropna(subset=["refit_probability", "tail_event"])
0304          y = view["tail_event"].astype(int)
0305          p = view["refit_probability"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0306          cutoff = float(p.quantile(0.80))
0307          selected = p >= cutoff
0308          prediction_rows.append(
0309              {
0310                  "period": period,
0311                  "observations": len(view),
0312                  "events": int(y.sum()),
0313                  "event_rate": float(y.mean()),
0314                  "roc_auc": _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, y, p),
0315                  "average_precision": _metric_or_nan(average_precision_score, y, p),
0316                  "brier_score": float(brier_score_loss(y, p)),
0317                  "log_loss": float(log_loss(y, p, labels=[0, 1])),
0318                  "ece_5bin": _ece(y, p, bins=5),
0319                  "calibration_prevalence_brier": float(
0320                      np.mean((y - calibration_rate) ** 2)
0321                  ),
0322                  "oracle_period_prevalence_brier": float(y.mean() * (1 - y.mean())),
0323                  "recall_at_top_20pct": float(y[selected].sum() / max(y.sum(), 1)),
0324                  "precision_at_top_20pct": float(y[selected].mean()),
0325                  "mean_probability_event": float(p[y == 1].mean()),
0326                  "mean_probability_nonevent": float(p[y == 0].mean()),
0327              }
0328          )
0329          period_coefficients = coefficients.loc[
0330              (coefficients["month"] <= CAL_END)
0331              if period.startswith("calibration")
0332              else (coefficients["month"] >= TEST_START)
0333          ]
0334          for feature in TAIL_FEATURES:
0335              feature_view = view[[feature, "tail_event"]].dropna()
0336              fy = feature_view["tail_event"].astype(int)
0337              raw_auc = _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, fy, feature_view[feature])
0338              values = period_coefficients.loc[
0339                  period_coefficients["feature"] == feature, "coefficient"
0340              ]
0341              median_coefficient = float(values.median()) if len(values) else np.nan
0342              median_sign = np.sign(median_coefficient)
0343              sign_stability = (
0344                  float((np.sign(values) == median_sign).mean()) if len(values) else np.nan
0345              )
0346              feature_rows.append(
0347                  {
0348                      "period": period,
0349                      "feature": feature,
0350                      "group": "domestic" if feature in DOMESTIC_FEATURES else "oap_composite",
0351                      "observations": len(feature_view),
0352                      "missing_rate": float(view[feature].isna().mean()),
0353                      "raw_univariate_auc": raw_auc,
0354                      "direction_free_auc": max(raw_auc, 1 - raw_auc)
0355                      if np.isfinite(raw_auc)
0356                      else np.nan,
0357                      "median_standardized_logit_coefficient": median_coefficient,
0358                      "median_absolute_coefficient": abs(median_coefficient)
0359                      if np.isfinite(median_coefficient)
0360                      else np.nan,
0361                      "coefficient_sign_stability": sign_stability,
0362                  }
0363              )
0364      return pd.DataFrame(feature_rows), pd.DataFrame(prediction_rows), reproduction
0365

0367  def extended_performance() -> pd.DataFrame:
0368      existing = pd.read_csv(
0369          RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col="month"
0370      )
0371      robust = pd.read_csv(
0372          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col="month"

0400      )
