0435  def overfitting_diagnostics(performance: pd.DataFrame) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
0436      calibration = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_calibration.csv")
0437      report = json.loads(
0438          (RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_validation.json").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
0439      )
0440      winner = calibration.loc[calibration["Selected"]].iloc[0]
0441      axes = [
0442          "mode",
0443          "level_threshold",
0444          "shock_threshold",
0445          "max_risk_transfer",
0446          "bond_share",
0447          "rebalance_band",
0448      ]
0449      distance = pd.Series(0, index=calibration.index)
0450      for axis in axes:
0451          distance += calibration[axis].astype(str).ne(str(winner[axis])).astype(int)
0452      neighborhood = calibration.loc[distance <= 1].copy()
0453      neighborhood["grid_distance_from_winner"] = distance.loc[neighborhood.index]
0454      neighborhood["strict_pass"] = (
0455          (neighborhood["Cal_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0456          & (neighborhood["Cal_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0457          & (neighborhood["Cal_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0458          & (neighborhood["Validation_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0459          & (neighborhood["Validation_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0460          & (neighborhood["Validation_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0461          & (neighborhood["AvgStress"] > 0.002)
0462      )
0463      neighborhood = neighborhood.sort_values("MultiObjectiveScore", ascending=False)
0464  
0465      strict = calibration.loc[
0466          (calibration["Cal_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0467          & (calibration["Cal_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0468          & (calibration["Cal_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0469          & (calibration["Validation_CAGRDelta"] > 0)
0470          & (calibration["Validation_SharpeDelta"] > 0)
0471          & (calibration["Validation_MDDDelta"] >= 0)
0472          & (calibration["AvgStress"] > 0.002)
0473      ].sort_values("MultiObjectiveScore", ascending=False)
0474      runner = strict.iloc[1] if len(strict) > 1 else None
0475  
0476      existing = pd.read_csv(
0477          RESULTS / "vkospi_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col="month"
0478      )["return"]
0479      robust = pd.read_csv(
0480          RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_reconciled_monthly.csv", index_col="month"
0481      )["return"]
0482      existing.index = pd.PeriodIndex(existing.index, freq="M")
0483      robust.index = pd.PeriodIndex(robust.index, freq="M")
0484      yearly = pd.DataFrame({"existing": existing, "robust": robust}).loc[TEST_START:]
0485      annual = yearly.groupby(yearly.index.year).apply(
0486          lambda frame: pd.Series(
0487              {
0488                  "existing": (1 + frame["existing"]).prod() - 1,
0489                  "robust": (1 + frame["robust"]).prod() - 1,
0490              }
0491          )
0492      )
0493      annual["delta"] = annual["robust"] - annual["existing"]
0494  
0495      available = performance.loc[
0496          performance["period"].isin(
0497              [
0498                  "early_calibration_2007_04_2012_12",
0499                  "validation_2013_01_2017_12",
0500                  "locked_early_2018_01_2021_12",
0501                  "locked_late_2022_01_2026_07",
0502              ]
0503          )
0504      ].copy()
0505      pivot = available.pivot(index="period", columns="strategy", values=["CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD"])
0506      subperiod_deltas: dict[str, dict[str, float]] = {}
0507      for period in pivot.index:
0508          subperiod_deltas[period] = {
0509              metric: float(
0510                  pivot.loc[period, (metric, "RobustVKOSPIDynamic")]
0511                  - pivot.loc[period, (metric, "ExistingVKOSPIDynamic")]
0512              )
0513              for metric in ("CAGR", "Sharpe", "MDD")
0514          }
0515  
0516      output = {
0517          "candidate_count": int(len(calibration)),
0518          "strict_pass_count": int(len(strict)),
0519          "strict_pass_rate": float(len(strict) / len(calibration)),
0520          "selection_windows": {
0521              "outer": "2007-04 through 2017-12",
0522              "inner_validation": "2013-01 through 2017-12",
0523              "warning": "The inner window is nested inside the outer window, so the two gates are not independent tests.",
0524          },
0525          "winner_score": float(winner["MultiObjectiveScore"]),
0526          "runner_up_score": float(runner["MultiObjectiveScore"]) if runner is not None else None,
0527          "winner_runner_score_gap": float(
0528              winner["MultiObjectiveScore"] - runner["MultiObjectiveScore"]
0529          )
0530          if runner is not None
0531          else None,
0532          "one_axis_neighborhood_count": int(len(neighborhood)),
0533          "one_axis_neighborhood_strict_count": int(neighborhood["strict_pass"].sum()),
0534          "locked_bootstrap": report["locked"]["bootstrap"],
0535          "locked_years_robust_outperformed": int((annual["delta"] > 0).sum()),
0536          "locked_years_total": int(len(annual)),
0537          "subperiod_deltas_robust_minus_existing": subperiod_deltas,
0538          "controls_present": [
0539              "all coded selection ends at 2017-12",
0540              "2018-01 onward is a locked evaluation window",
0541              "strict CAGR, Sharpe and MDD gates are required in both pre-lock windows",
0542              "paired six-month block bootstrap and doubled-cost audit are reported",
0543          ],
0544          "residual_overfitting_risks": [
0545              "810 candidates create multiple-comparison selection pressure",
0546              "the inner 2013-2017 window is a subset of 2007-2017, not an independent validation set",
0547              "the winning score is only slightly above the runner-up score",
0548              "the macro constants and within-mode stress weights were fixed heuristics, not preregistered estimates",
0549              "revised macro data and repeated project-level experimentation can create researcher degrees of freedom",
0550              "no deflated Sharpe ratio, White reality check, CSCV/PBO, or external-market replication has been completed",
0551          ],
0552          "conclusion": "Code-level leakage controls are present, but overfitting cannot be ruled out. Evidence is suggestive rather than confirmatory.",
0553      }
0554      annual.reset_index(names="year").to_csv(
0555          RESULTS / "vkospi_locked_annual_relative_performance.csv", index=False
0556      )
0557      return neighborhood, output
0558  
0559  
0560  def main() -> None:
0561      macro = macro_constant_sensitivity()
0562      feature, prediction, reproduction = tail_feature_diagnostics()
0563      performance = extended_performance()
0564      neighborhood, overfit = overfitting_diagnostics(performance)
0565      overfit["tail_probability_reproduction"] = reproduction
0566  
0567      macro.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_macro_constant_sensitivity.csv", index=False)
0568      feature.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_tail_feature_diagnostics.csv", index=False)
0569      prediction.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_tail_prediction_diagnostics.csv", index=False)
0570      performance.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_extended_period_performance.csv", index=False)
0571      neighborhood.to_csv(RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_grid_neighborhood.csv", index=False)
0572      (RESULTS / "vkospi_overfitting_diagnostics.json").write_text(
0573          json.dumps(overfit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
0574      )
0575      print("macro sensitivity", macro.shape)
0576      print("tail features", feature.shape)
0577      print("tail prediction", prediction.shape)
0578      print("period performance", performance.shape)
0579      print("grid neighborhood", neighborhood.shape)
0580      print(json.dumps(overfit, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2))
0581  
0582  
0583  if __name__ == "__main__":
0584      main()

0071  def causal_percentile(values: pd.Series, lookback: int = 60) -> pd.Series:
0072      """Map a value to its trailing empirical percentile without using the current row."""
0073      result = pd.Series(np.nan, index=values.index, dtype=float)
0074      history: list[float] = []
0075      for index, value in values.items():
0076          if not np.isfinite(value):
0077              continue
0078          reference = np.asarray(history[-lookback:], dtype=float)
0079          if len(reference) >= 12:
0080              result.loc[index] = (
0081                  float(np.sum(reference <= value)) + 1.0
0082              ) / (len(reference) + 1.0)
0083          else:
0084              result.loc[index] = 0.5
0085          history.append(float(value))
0086      return result
0087  
0088  
0089  def _transfer(
0090      weights: np.ndarray,
0091      donors: list[int],
0092      receivers: list[int],
0093      amount: float,
0094  ) -> np.ndarray:
0095      """Copy of the deployed factor-tilt transfer, kept local for Colab portability."""
0096      output = weights.copy()
0097      floors = np.array([0.02, 0.05, 0.02, 0.00])
0098      available = np.maximum(output[donors] - floors[donors], 0)
0099      transfer = min(float(amount), float(available.sum()))
0100      if transfer <= 0:
0101          return output
0102      output[donors] -= transfer * available / available.sum()
0103      receiver_base = np.maximum(output[receivers], 0.02)
0104      output[receivers] += transfer * receiver_base / receiver_base.sum()
0105      return output / output.sum()
0106  
0107  
0108  def apply_factor_tilt(base: np.ndarray, score: float, max_shift: float) -> np.ndarray:
0109      if not np.isfinite(score) or max_shift <= 0:
0110          return base.copy()
0111      if score >= 0:
0112          return _transfer(base, donors=[1, 2], receivers=[0], amount=max_shift * score)
0113      return _transfer(base, donors=[0, 3], receivers=[1, 2], amount=max_shift * -score)
0114  
0115  
0116  def run_factor_vol_target(
0117      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0118      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0119      defensive_path: pd.DataFrame,
0120      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0121      max_shift: float,
0122      target_vol: float = 0.15,
0123      cost_multiplier: float = 1.0,
0124      financing_rate: float = 0.04,
0125      leverage_min: float = 0.50,
0126      leverage_max: float = 1.50,
0127      initial_leverage: float = 1.20,
0128  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0129      """Reproduce the deployed medium-horizon allocation without heavy optional imports."""
0130      if not 0 < leverage_min <= leverage_max:
0131          raise ValueError("leverage bounds must satisfy 0 < minimum <= maximum")
0132      initial_leverage = float(np.clip(initial_leverage, leverage_min, leverage_max))
0133      months = signals.index.intersection(returns.index).intersection(defensive_path.index)
0134      rows: list[dict[str, float | pd.Period | str]] = []
0135      pretrade = np.zeros(len(ASSETS))
0136      nav = 1.0
0137      peak = 1.0
0138      first_trade = True
0139      for month in months:
0140          hard = hard_regime_weights(signals.loc[month])
0141          defensive = defensive_path.loc[
0142              month, [f"w_{asset}" for asset in ASSETS]
0143          ].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0144          base = 0.40 * hard + 0.60 * defensive
0145          probability = float(factor.loc[month, "p_up"]) if month in factor.index else 0.5
0146          score = float(np.clip((probability - 0.5) / 0.15, -1, 1))
0147          unlevered = apply_factor_tilt(base, score, max_shift)
0148  
0149          history = returns.loc[returns.index < month, ASSETS].tail(24)
0150          if len(history) >= 12:
0151              conditional = history.to_numpy(dtype=float) @ unlevered
0152              observation_weights = np.exp(np.linspace(-2.0, 0.0, len(conditional)))
0153              observation_weights /= observation_weights.sum()
0154              mean = float(observation_weights @ conditional)
0155              variance = float(observation_weights @ (conditional - mean) ** 2)
0156              forecast_vol = math.sqrt(max(variance, 1e-8) * 12)
0157              leverage = float(
0158                  np.clip(target_vol / forecast_vol, leverage_min, leverage_max)
0159              )
0160          else:
0161              forecast_vol = np.nan
0162              leverage = initial_leverage
0163          asset_weights = leverage * unlevered
0164          debt_weight = 1.0 - leverage
0165          delta = asset_weights - pretrade
0166          turnover = np.abs(delta).sum() if first_trade else 0.5 * np.abs(delta).sum()
0167          trade_cost = np.abs(delta).sum() * 0.0015 * cost_multiplier
0168          fx_cost = (
0169              abs((asset_weights[2] + asset_weights[3]) - (pretrade[2] + pretrade[3]))
0170              * 0.0005
0171              * cost_multiplier
0172          )
0173          financing = debt_weight * ((1 + financing_rate) ** (1 / 12) - 1)
0174          asset_return = returns.loc[month, ASSETS].to_numpy(dtype=float)
0175          gross_return = float(asset_weights @ asset_return + financing)
0176          net_return = gross_return - trade_cost - fx_cost
0177          nav *= 1 + net_return
0178          peak = max(peak, nav)
0179          pretrade = asset_weights * (1 + asset_return) / (1 + gross_return)
0180          first_trade = False
0181          rows.append(
0182              {
0183                  "month": month,
0184                  "return": net_return,
0185                  "gross_return": gross_return,
0186                  "nav": nav,
0187                  "drawdown": nav / peak - 1,
0188                  "turnover": turnover,
0189                  "trade_cost": trade_cost,
0190                  "fx_cost": fx_cost,
0191                  "forecast_vol": forecast_vol,
0192                  "leverage": leverage,
0193                  "risk_score": score,
0194                  **{
0195                      f"w_{asset}": asset_weights[index]
0196                      for index, asset in enumerate(ASSETS)
0197                  },
0198              }
0199          )
0200      return pd.DataFrame(rows).set_index("month")
0201

0203  def _period_view(frame: pd.DataFrame | pd.Series, start: pd.Period | None, end: pd.Period | None):
0204      output = frame
0205      if start is not None:
0206          output = output.loc[start:]
0207      if end is not None:
0208          output = output.loc[:end]
0209      return output

0211  
0212  def _metric_or_nan(function, y: pd.Series, values: pd.Series) -> float:
0213      if len(y) == 0 or y.nunique() < 2:
0214          return float("nan")
0215      return float(function(y, values))
0216  
0217  
0218  def moving_block_draws(
0219      length: int,
0220      simulations: int = 2000,
0221      block_length: int = 6,
0222      seed: int = RNG_SEED,
0223  ) -> np.ndarray:
0224      rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
0225      output = np.empty((simulations, length), dtype=int)
0226      for row in range(simulations):
0227          indices: list[int] = []
0228          while len(indices) < length:
0229              start = int(rng.integers(0, max(length - block_length + 1, 1)))
0230              indices.extend(range(start, min(start + block_length, length)))
0231          output[row] = np.asarray(indices[:length], dtype=int)
0232      return output
0233  
0234  
0235  def _sharpe_matrix(values: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
0236      mean = np.nanmean(values, axis=1)
0237      std = np.nanstd(values, axis=1, ddof=1)
0238      return np.divide(mean, std, out=np.full_like(mean, np.nan), where=std > 0) * math.sqrt(12)
0239  
0240  
0241  def add_locked_bootstrap(
0242      summary: pd.DataFrame,
0243      loss_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0244      return_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0245      deployed_id: str,
0246      simulations: int = 2000,
0247  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0248      candidates = list(summary.index)
0249      loss = loss_matrix[candidates].dropna(how="any")
0250      returns = return_matrix[candidates].dropna(how="any")
0251      loss = loss.loc[loss.index >= TEST_START]
0252      returns = returns.loc[returns.index >= TEST_START]
0253  
0254      loss_draws = moving_block_draws(len(loss), simulations, seed=RNG_SEED + 1)
0255      return_draws = moving_block_draws(len(returns), simulations, seed=RNG_SEED + 2)
0256      loss_values = loss.to_numpy(dtype=float)[loss_draws].mean(axis=1)
0257      return_values = returns.to_numpy(dtype=float)[return_draws]
0258      sharpes = _sharpe_matrix(return_values)
0259  
0260      deployed_column = candidates.index(deployed_id)
0261      brier_delta = loss_values - loss_values[:, [deployed_column]]
0262      sharpe_delta = sharpes - sharpes[:, [deployed_column]]
0263      summary = summary.copy()
0264      summary["locked_boot_probability_brier_better_than_deployed"] = np.mean(
0265          brier_delta < 0, axis=0
0266      )
0267      summary["locked_boot_probability_sharpe_better_than_deployed"] = np.mean(
0268          sharpe_delta > 0, axis=0
0269      )
0270      summary["locked_boot_sharpe_delta_p05"] = np.nanquantile(sharpe_delta, 0.05, axis=0)
0271      summary["locked_boot_sharpe_delta_median"] = np.nanmedian(sharpe_delta, axis=0)
0272      summary["locked_boot_sharpe_delta_p95"] = np.nanquantile(sharpe_delta, 0.95, axis=0)
0273      return summary
0274  
0275  
0276  def cscv_pbo(
0277      matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0278      kind: str,
0279      partitions: int = 8,
0280  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0281      clean = matrix.dropna(how="any")
0282      blocks = [np.asarray(block, dtype=int) for block in np.array_split(np.arange(len(clean)), partitions)]
0283      candidates = list(clean.columns)
0284      values = clean.to_numpy(dtype=float)
0285      selected: list[str] = []
0286      oos_percentiles: list[float] = []
0287      logits: list[float] = []
0288  
0289      def scores(rows: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
0290          block = values[rows]
0291          if kind == "sharpe":
0292              std = block.std(axis=0, ddof=1)
0293              return np.divide(
0294                  block.mean(axis=0),
0295                  std,
0296                  out=np.full(block.shape[1], -np.inf),
0297                  where=std > 0,
0298              ) * math.sqrt(12)
0299          if kind == "mean":
0300              return block.mean(axis=0)
0301          raise ValueError(kind)
0302  
0303      half = partitions // 2
0304      for chosen in itertools.combinations(range(partitions), half):
0305          chosen_set = set(chosen)
0306          train_rows = np.concatenate([blocks[index] for index in chosen])
0307          test_rows = np.concatenate(
0308              [blocks[index] for index in range(partitions) if index not in chosen_set]
0309          )
0310          train_scores = scores(train_rows)
0311          winner = int(np.nanargmax(train_scores))
0312          test_scores = scores(test_rows)
0313          ranks = rankdata(test_scores, method="average")
0314          percentile = float((ranks[winner] - 0.5) / len(candidates))
0315          bounded = float(np.clip(percentile, 1e-6, 1 - 1e-6))
0316          selected.append(candidates[winner])
0317          oos_percentiles.append(percentile)
0318          logits.append(float(math.log(bounded / (1 - bounded))))
0319  
0320      counts = pd.Series(selected).value_counts()
0321      return {
0322          "kind": kind,
0323          "partitions": partitions,
0324          "splits": len(selected),
0325          "pbo": float(np.mean(np.asarray(logits) <= 0)),
0326          "median_oos_percentile": float(np.median(oos_percentiles)),
0327          "median_logit": float(np.median(logits)),
0328          "most_selected_candidate": str(counts.index[0]),
0329          "most_selected_share": float(counts.iloc[0] / len(selected)),
0330      }
0331  
0332  
0333  def _rank_score(frame: pd.DataFrame, high: list[str], low: list[str]) -> pd.Series:
0334      ranks: list[pd.Series] = []
0335      for column in high:
0336          ranks.append(frame[column].rank(pct=True, method="average"))
0337      for column in low:
0338          ranks.append((-frame[column]).rank(pct=True, method="average"))
0339      return pd.concat(ranks, axis=1).mean(axis=1)
0340  
0341  
0342  def _rank_correlation(first: pd.Series, second: pd.Series) -> float:
0343      joined = pd.concat([first, second], axis=1).dropna()
0344      if len(joined) < 3 or joined.iloc[:, 0].nunique() < 2 or joined.iloc[:, 1].nunique() < 2:
0345          return float("nan")
0346      return float(spearmanr(joined.iloc[:, 0], joined.iloc[:, 1]).statistic)
0347  
0348  
0349  def logistic_specs() -> list[dict[str, object]]:
0350      specs: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0351      c_values = (0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 1.00, 3.00)
0352      for penalty, solver, class_weight in (
0353          ("l2", "liblinear", "balanced"),
0354          ("l1", "liblinear", "balanced"),
0355          ("l2", "lbfgs", "balanced"),
0356          ("l2", "liblinear", None),
0357      ):
0358          for c_value in c_values:
0359              weight_name = "balanced" if class_weight == "balanced" else "none"
0360              specs.append(
0361                  {
0362                      "candidate": f"{penalty}_{solver}_c{c_value:g}_{weight_name}",
0363                      "penalty": penalty,
0364                      "solver": solver,
0365                      "C": c_value,
0366                      "class_weight": class_weight,
0367                      "l1_ratio": np.nan,
0368                  }
0369              )
0370      for c_value in (0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 1.00):
0371          specs.append(
0372              {
0373                  "candidate": f"elasticnet_saga_c{c_value:g}_r0.5_balanced",
0374                  "penalty": "elasticnet",
0375                  "solver": "saga",
0376                  "C": c_value,
0377                  "class_weight": "balanced",
0378                  "l1_ratio": 0.5,
0379              }
0380          )
0381      return specs
0382  
0383  
0384  def make_logistic_model(spec: dict[str, object]) -> Pipeline:
0385      arguments: dict[str, object] = {
0386          "C": float(spec["C"]),
0387          "penalty": str(spec["penalty"]),
0388          "solver": str(spec["solver"]),
0389          "class_weight": spec["class_weight"],
0390          "max_iter": 5000,
0391          "random_state": RNG_SEED,
0392      }
0393      if spec["penalty"] == "elasticnet":
0394          arguments["l1_ratio"] = float(spec["l1_ratio"])
0395      return Pipeline(
0396          [
0397              ("imputer", SimpleImputer(strategy="median")),
0398              ("scale", StandardScaler()),
0399              ("model", LogisticRegression(**arguments)),
0400          ]
0401      )
0402  
0403  
0404  def tail_data() -> pd.DataFrame:
0405      domestic = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "hard_crash_features.csv", index_col=0)
0406      composites = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "openassetpricing_composites.csv", index_col=0)
0407      factor = pd.read_csv(
0408          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0409      )
0410      for frame in (domestic, composites, factor):
0411          frame.index = pd.PeriodIndex(frame.index, freq="M")
0412      return domestic[DOMESTIC_FEATURES].join(composites[OAP_COMPOSITES]).join(
0413          factor[["tail_event"]]
0414      )
0415  
0416  
0417  def fit_logistic_candidate(
0418      data: pd.DataFrame,
0419      spec: dict[str, object],
0420  ) -> tuple[pd.Series, dict[str, float]]:
0421      probability = pd.Series(np.nan, index=data.index, dtype=float)
0422      l1_norms: list[float] = []
0423      l2_norms: list[float] = []
0424      nonzero_counts: list[int] = []
0425      convergence_warnings = 0
0426      for number, month in enumerate(data.index):
0427          train_end = number - 2
0428          if train_end < 36:
0429              continue
0430          train = data.iloc[:train_end].dropna(subset=["tail_event"])
0431          y = train["tail_event"].astype(int)
0432          if y.sum() < 4 or (len(y) - y.sum()) < 12:
0433              continue
0434          model = make_logistic_model(spec)
0435          with warnings.catch_warnings(record=True) as caught:
0436              warnings.simplefilter("always", ConvergenceWarning)
0437              model.fit(train[TAIL_FEATURES], y)
0438          convergence_warnings += sum(
0439              issubclass(item.category, ConvergenceWarning) for item in caught
0440          )
0441          probability.loc[month] = float(
0442              model.predict_proba(data.loc[[month], TAIL_FEATURES])[:, 1][0]
0443          )
0444          coefficient = model.named_steps["model"].coef_[0]
0445          l1_norms.append(float(np.abs(coefficient).sum()))
0446          l2_norms.append(float(np.sqrt(np.square(coefficient).sum())))
0447          nonzero_counts.append(int(np.sum(np.abs(coefficient) > 1e-8)))
0448      return probability, {
0449          "fit_count": float(len(l1_norms)),
0450          "convergence_warning_count": float(convergence_warnings),
0451          "median_coefficient_l1_norm": float(np.median(l1_norms)),
0452          "median_coefficient_l2_norm": float(np.median(l2_norms)),
0453          "median_nonzero_coefficients": float(np.median(nonzero_counts)),
0454      }
0455  
0456  
0457  def make_tail_factor(probability: pd.Series, target: pd.Series) -> pd.DataFrame:
0458      output = pd.DataFrame({"p_tail_raw": probability, "tail_event": target})
0459      output["risk_percentile"] = causal_percentile(output["p_tail_raw"])
0460      output["risk_severity"] = (
0461          (output["risk_percentile"] - 0.80) / 0.20
0462      ).clip(0, 1)
0463      output["p_up"] = 0.50 - 0.15 * output["risk_severity"]
0464      output["score"] = -output["risk_severity"]
0465      return output
0466  
0467  
0468  def logistic_period_record(
0469      candidate: str,
0470      spec: dict[str, object],
0471      fit_stats: dict[str, float],
0472      factor: pd.DataFrame,
0473      backtest: pd.DataFrame,
0474      period: str,
0475      start: pd.Period | None,
0476      end: pd.Period | None,
0477  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0478      view = _period_view(factor, start, end).dropna(
0479          subset=["p_tail_raw", "tail_event"]
0480      )
0481      y = view["tail_event"].astype(int)
0482      p = view["p_tail_raw"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0483      triggered = view["risk_percentile"] >= 0.80
0484      path = _period_view(backtest, start, end)
0485      metrics = performance_summary(path["return"])
0486      return {
0487          "candidate": candidate,
0488          "period": period,
0489          **spec,
0490          **fit_stats,
0491          "prediction_observations": int(len(view)),
0492          "events": int(y.sum()),
0493          "event_rate": float(y.mean()),
0494          "roc_auc": _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, y, p),
0495          "average_precision": _metric_or_nan(average_precision_score, y, p),
0496          "brier_score": float(brier_score_loss(y, p)),
0497          "prevalence_brier": float(y.mean() * (1 - y.mean())),
0498          "log_loss": float(log_loss(y, p, labels=[0, 1])),
0499          "ece_5bin": _ece(y, p, bins=5),
0500          "policy_trigger_rate": float(triggered.mean()),
0501          "policy_recall": float(y[triggered].sum() / max(int(y.sum()), 1)),
0502          "policy_precision": float(y[triggered].mean()) if triggered.any() else np.nan,
0503          **metrics.to_dict(),
0504          "AvgTurnover": float(path["turnover"].mean()),
0505      }
0506  
0507  
0508  def summarize_logistic(
0509      long: pd.DataFrame,
0510      loss_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0511      return_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0512  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
0513      metadata = long.drop_duplicates("candidate").set_index("candidate")[[
0514          "penalty",
0515          "solver",
0516          "C",
0517          "class_weight",
0518          "l1_ratio",
0519          "fit_count",
0520          "convergence_warning_count",
0521          "median_coefficient_l1_norm",
0522          "median_coefficient_l2_norm",
0523          "median_nonzero_coefficients",
0524      ]]
0525      summary = metadata.copy()
0526      metric_columns = (
0527          "roc_auc",
0528          "average_precision",
0529          "brier_score",
0530          "log_loss",
0531          "ece_5bin",
0532          "policy_recall",
0533          "policy_precision",
0534          "CAGR",
0535          "Sharpe",
0536          "MDD",
0537          "Calmar",
0538          "AvgTurnover",
0539      )
0540      for period in ("calibration_2007_2017", "validation_2013_2017", "locked_2018_2026"):
0541          view = long.loc[long["period"] == period].set_index("candidate")
0542          prefix = {
0543              "calibration_2007_2017": "cal",
0544              "validation_2013_2017": "val",
0545              "locked_2018_2026": "locked",
0546          }[period]
0547          for column in metric_columns:
0548              summary[f"{prefix}_{column}"] = view[column]
0549  
0550      for prefix in ("cal", "locked"):
0551          summary[f"{prefix}_prediction_score"] = _rank_score(
0552              summary,
0553              [f"{prefix}_roc_auc", f"{prefix}_average_precision"],
0554              [f"{prefix}_brier_score", f"{prefix}_ece_5bin"],
0555          )
0556          summary[f"{prefix}_portfolio_score"] = _rank_score(
0557              summary,
0558              [f"{prefix}_CAGR", f"{prefix}_Sharpe", f"{prefix}_MDD"],
0559              [],
0560          )
0561          summary[f"{prefix}_prediction_rank"] = summary[
0562              f"{prefix}_prediction_score"
0563          ].rank(ascending=False, method="min")
0564          summary[f"{prefix}_portfolio_rank"] = summary[
0565              f"{prefix}_portfolio_score"
0566          ].rank(ascending=False, method="min")
0567  
0568      deployed_id = "l2_liblinear_c0.1_balanced"
0569      summary["is_deployed"] = summary.index == deployed_id
0570      summary = add_locked_bootstrap(summary, loss_matrix, return_matrix, deployed_id)
0571  
0572      cal_prediction_winner = str(summary["cal_prediction_score"].idxmax())
0573      cal_portfolio_winner = str(summary["cal_portfolio_score"].idxmax())
0574      deployed = summary.loc[deployed_id]
0575      cal = long.loc[long["period"] == "calibration_2007_2017"].set_index("candidate")
0576      val = long.loc[long["period"] == "validation_2013_2017"].set_index("candidate")
0577      deployed_cal = cal.loc[deployed_id]
0578      deployed_val = val.loc[deployed_id]
0579      strict = (
0580          (cal["roc_auc"] >= deployed_cal["roc_auc"])
0581          & (cal["brier_score"] <= deployed_cal["brier_score"])
0582          & (cal["Sharpe"] >= deployed_cal["Sharpe"])
0583          & (cal["MDD"] >= deployed_cal["MDD"])
0584          & (val["roc_auc"] >= deployed_val["roc_auc"])
0585          & (val["brier_score"] <= deployed_val["brier_score"])
0586          & (val["Sharpe"] >= deployed_val["Sharpe"])
0587          & (val["MDD"] >= deployed_val["MDD"])
0588      )
0589      strict_candidates = [str(candidate) for candidate in strict.index[strict]]
0590      warning_mask = summary["convergence_warning_count"] > 0
0591      warning_candidates = [
0592          {
0593              "candidate": str(candidate),
0594              "warning_count": int(summary.loc[candidate, "convergence_warning_count"]),
0595          }
0596          for candidate in summary.index[warning_mask]
0597      ]
0598      stable_candidates = [str(candidate) for candidate in summary.index[~warning_mask]]
0599      zero_coefficient_candidates = [
0600          str(candidate)
0601          for candidate in summary.index[summary["median_nonzero_coefficients"] == 0]
0602      ]
0603  
0604      report = {
0605          "candidate_count": int(len(summary)),
0606          "grid": {
0607              "C": [0.01, 0.03, 0.10, 0.30, 1.00, 3.00],
0608              "families": [
0609                  "balanced L2 liblinear",
0610                  "balanced L1 liblinear",
0611                  "balanced L2 lbfgs",
0612                  "unweighted L2 liblinear",
0613                  "balanced ElasticNet saga",
0614              ],
0615          },
0616          "deployed_candidate": deployed_id,
0617          "deployed_calibration_prediction_rank": int(deployed["cal_prediction_rank"]),
0618          "deployed_locked_prediction_rank": int(deployed["locked_prediction_rank"]),
0619          "deployed_calibration_portfolio_rank": int(deployed["cal_portfolio_rank"]),
0620          "deployed_locked_portfolio_rank": int(deployed["locked_portfolio_rank"]),
0621          "calibration_prediction_winner": cal_prediction_winner,
0622          "calibration_prediction_winner_locked_rank": int(
0623              summary.loc[cal_prediction_winner, "locked_prediction_rank"]
0624          ),
0625          "calibration_portfolio_winner": cal_portfolio_winner,
0626          "calibration_portfolio_winner_locked_rank": int(
0627              summary.loc[cal_portfolio_winner, "locked_portfolio_rank"]
0628          ),
0629          "prediction_rank_correlation_calibration_vs_locked": _rank_correlation(
0630              summary["cal_prediction_score"], summary["locked_prediction_score"]
0631          ),
0632          "portfolio_rank_correlation_calibration_vs_locked": _rank_correlation(
0633              summary["cal_portfolio_score"], summary["locked_portfolio_score"]
0634          ),
0635          "strict_both_prelock_windows_count": int(strict.sum()),
0636          "strict_both_prelock_windows_candidates": strict_candidates,
0637          "candidates_with_convergence_warnings": int(warning_mask.sum()),
0638          "total_convergence_warnings": int(
0639              summary["convergence_warning_count"].sum()
0640          ),
0641          "convergence_warning_candidates": warning_candidates,
0642          "zero_coefficient_candidates": zero_coefficient_candidates,
0643          "locked_cscv_note": "CSCV/PBO is calculated only on pre-2018 data; locked data is reserved for rank validation.",
0644          "prelock_prediction_brier_pbo": cscv_pbo(
0645              -loss_matrix.loc[loss_matrix.index <= CAL_END], kind="mean"
0646          ),
0647          "prelock_portfolio_sharpe_pbo": cscv_pbo(
0648              return_matrix.loc[return_matrix.index <= CAL_END], kind="sharpe"
0649          ),
0650          "prelock_prediction_brier_pbo_excluding_warning_candidates": cscv_pbo(
0651              -loss_matrix.loc[loss_matrix.index <= CAL_END, stable_candidates],
0652              kind="mean",
0653          ),
0654          "prelock_portfolio_sharpe_pbo_excluding_warning_candidates": cscv_pbo(
0655              return_matrix.loc[return_matrix.index <= CAL_END, stable_candidates],
0656              kind="sharpe",
0657          ),
0658      }
0659      return summary.sort_values("cal_prediction_rank"), report
0660  
0661  
0662  def run_logistic_robustness(
0663      returns: pd.DataFrame,
0664      signals: pd.DataFrame,
0665      defensive: pd.DataFrame,
0666  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
0667      data = tail_data()
0668      stored_factor = pd.read_csv(
0669          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_factor.csv", index_col=0
0670      )
0671      stored_factor.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_factor.index, freq="M")
0672      stored_backtest = pd.read_csv(
0673          RESULTS / "openassetpricing_medium_horizon_backtest.csv", index_col=0
0674      )
0675      stored_backtest.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_backtest.index, freq="M")
0676  
0677      long_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0678      losses: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
0679      portfolio_returns: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
0680      reproduction: dict[str, float] = {}
0681      specs = logistic_specs()
0682      for number, spec in enumerate(specs, start=1):
0683          candidate = str(spec["candidate"])
0684          print(f"logistic {number:02d}/{len(specs)} {candidate}", flush=True)
0685          probability, fit_stats = fit_logistic_candidate(data, spec)
0686          factor = make_tail_factor(probability, data["tail_event"])
0687          backtest = run_factor_vol_target(
0688              returns,
0689              signals,
0690              defensive,
0691              factor,
0692              max_shift=0.20,
0693              target_vol=0.15,
0694          )
0695          valid = factor[["p_tail_raw", "tail_event"]].dropna()
0696          losses[candidate] = (
0697              valid["p_tail_raw"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6) - valid["tail_event"]
0698          ) ** 2
0699          portfolio_returns[candidate] = backtest["return"]
0700          for period, start, end in PERIODS:
0701              long_rows.append(
0702                  logistic_period_record(
0703                      candidate,
0704                      spec,
0705                      fit_stats,
0706                      factor,
0707                      backtest,
0708                      period,
0709                      start,
0710                      end,
0711                  )
0712              )
0713          if candidate == "l2_liblinear_c0.1_balanced":
0714              probability_comparison = factor[["p_tail_raw"]].join(
0715                  stored_factor[["p_tail_raw"]],
0716                  how="inner",
0717                  lsuffix="_refit",
0718                  rsuffix="_stored",
0719              ).dropna()
0720              common = backtest.index.intersection(stored_backtest.index)
0721              reproduction = {
0722                  "probability_observations": int(len(probability_comparison)),
0723                  "max_absolute_probability_difference": float(
0724                      (
0725                          probability_comparison["p_tail_raw_refit"]
0726                          - probability_comparison["p_tail_raw_stored"]
0727                      ).abs().max()
0728                  ),
0729                  "portfolio_observations": int(len(common)),
0730                  "max_absolute_portfolio_return_difference": float(
0731                      (
0732                          backtest.loc[common, "return"]
0733                          - stored_backtest.loc[common, "return"]
0734                      ).abs().max()
0735                  ),
0736              }
0737  
0738      long = pd.DataFrame(long_rows)
0739      loss_matrix = pd.concat(losses, axis=1).sort_index()
0740      return_matrix = pd.concat(portfolio_returns, axis=1).sort_index()
0741      summary, report = summarize_logistic(long, loss_matrix, return_matrix)
0742      report["deployed_reproduction"] = reproduction
0743      assert reproduction["probability_observations"] > 0
0744      assert reproduction["portfolio_observations"] > 0
0745      assert reproduction["max_absolute_probability_difference"] < 1e-12
0746      assert reproduction["max_absolute_portfolio_return_difference"] < 1e-12
0747      return long, summary, report
0748  
0749  
0750  def sjm_internal_grid() -> tuple[tuple[float, ...], tuple[int, ...]]:
0751      return (0.0, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0), (2, 4, 6)
0752  
0753  
0754  def build_or_load_sjm_paths(
0755      components: pd.DataFrame,
0756      macro: pd.DataFrame,
0757  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0758      penalties, keeps = sjm_internal_grid()
0759      expected = {(float(penalty), int(keep)) for penalty in penalties for keep in keeps}
0760      if SJM_CACHE_PATH.exists():
0761          cached = pd.read_csv(SJM_CACHE_PATH)
0762          cached["target_month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(cached["target_month"], freq="M")
0763          cached["signal_month"] = pd.PeriodIndex(cached["signal_month"], freq="M")
0764          found = set(
0765              zip(cached["jump_penalty"].astype(float), cached["keep_features"].astype(int))
0766          )
0767          counts = cached.groupby(["jump_penalty", "keep_features"]).size()
0768          if found == expected and counts.eq(len(components)).all():
0769              print("SJM internal path cache reused", cached.shape, flush=True)
0770              return cached
0771  
0772      rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
0773      combinations = list(itertools.product(penalties, keeps))
0774      for number, (penalty, keep) in enumerate(combinations, start=1):
0775          print(
0776              f"SJM internal {number:02d}/{len(combinations)} jump={penalty:g} keep={keep}",
0777              flush=True,
0778          )
0779          model = SparseJump2(jump_penalty=penalty, keep_features=keep)
0780          for target_month, component in components.iterrows():
0781              signal_month = component["signal_month"]
0782              history = macro.loc[: signal_month.to_timestamp("M")]
0783              pg, growth_detail = model.fit_predict_high(history["growth"])
0784              pi, inflation_detail = model.fit_predict_high(history["inflation"])
0785              rows.append(
0786                  {
0787                      "target_month": target_month,
0788                      "signal_month": signal_month,
0789                      "jump_penalty": penalty,
0790                      "keep_features": keep,
0791                      "growth_sjm": pg,
0792                      "inflation_sjm": pi,
0793                      "growth_switches": growth_detail["switches"],
0794                      "inflation_switches": inflation_detail["switches"],
0795                  }
0796              )
0797      output = pd.DataFrame(rows)
0798      output.to_csv(SJM_CACHE_PATH, index=False)
0799      return output
0800  
0801  
0802  def build_macro_signals(
0803      probabilities: pd.DataFrame,
0804      components: pd.DataFrame,
0805  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0806      output = pd.DataFrame(index=probabilities.index)
0807      output["signal_month"] = components["signal_month"]
0808      output["p_growth_high"] = probabilities["growth"]
0809      output["p_inflation_high"] = probabilities["inflation"]
0810      output["p_Goldilocks"] = probabilities["growth"] * (1 - probabilities["inflation"])
0811      output["p_Overheating"] = probabilities["growth"] * probabilities["inflation"]
0812      output["p_Slowdown"] = (1 - probabilities["growth"]) * (1 - probabilities["inflation"])
0813      output["p_Stagflation"] = (1 - probabilities["growth"]) * probabilities["inflation"]
0814      regime_columns = [
0815          "p_Goldilocks",
0816          "p_Overheating",
0817          "p_Slowdown",
0818          "p_Stagflation",
0819      ]
0820      output["regime"] = output[regime_columns].idxmax(axis=1).str.removeprefix("p_")
0821      return output
0822  
0823  
0824  def aligned_macro_targets(
0825      components: pd.DataFrame,
0826      targets: pd.DataFrame,
0827  ) -> pd.DataFrame:
0828      output = pd.DataFrame(index=components.index, dtype=float)
0829      for name in ("growth", "inflation"):
0830          output[name] = [
0831              targets.loc[signal_month.to_timestamp("M"), name]
0832              if signal_month.to_timestamp("M") in targets.index
0833              else np.nan
0834              for signal_month in components["signal_month"]
0835          ]
0836      return output
0837  
0838  
0839  def macro_period_record(
0840      candidate: str,
0841      penalty: float,
0842      keep: int,
0843      weight: float,
0844      probabilities: pd.DataFrame,
0845      target: pd.DataFrame,
0846      soft: pd.DataFrame,
0847      proposed: pd.DataFrame | None,
0848      switches: tuple[float, float],
0849      period: str,
0850      start: pd.Period | None,
0851      end: pd.Period | None,
0852  ) -> dict[str, object]:
0853      pred = _period_view(probabilities.join(target, rsuffix="_target"), start, end).dropna()
0854      growth_y = pred["growth_target"].astype(int)
0855      inflation_y = pred["inflation_target"].astype(int)
0856      growth_p = pred["growth"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0857      inflation_p = pred["inflation"].clip(1e-6, 1 - 1e-6)
0858      quadrant = (
0859          ((growth_p >= 0.5) == growth_y.astype(bool))
0860          & ((inflation_p >= 0.5) == inflation_y.astype(bool))
0861      )
0862      soft_view = _period_view(soft, start, end)
0863      soft_metrics = performance_summary(soft_view["return"])
0864      record: dict[str, object] = {
0865          "candidate": candidate,
0866          "period": period,
0867          "jump_penalty": penalty,
0868          "keep_features": keep,
0869          "sjm_weight": weight,
0870          "is_deployed": bool(
0871              math.isclose(penalty, 3.0)
0872              and keep == 4
0873              and math.isclose(weight, 0.10)
0874          ),
0875          "prediction_observations": int(len(pred)),
0876          "growth_auc": _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, growth_y, growth_p),
0877          "inflation_auc": _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, inflation_y, inflation_p),
0878          "mean_auc": float(
0879              np.nanmean(
0880                  [
0881                      _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, growth_y, growth_p),
0882                      _metric_or_nan(roc_auc_score, inflation_y, inflation_p),
0883                  ]
0884              )
0885          ),
0886          "growth_brier": float(brier_score_loss(growth_y, growth_p)),
0887          "inflation_brier": float(brier_score_loss(inflation_y, inflation_p)),
0888          "mean_brier": float(
0889              (
0890                  brier_score_loss(growth_y, growth_p)
0891                  + brier_score_loss(inflation_y, inflation_p)
0892              )
0893              / 2
0894          ),
0895          "quadrant_accuracy": float(quadrant.mean()),
0896          "growth_switches": switches[0],
0897          "inflation_switches": switches[1],
0898          **{f"Soft_{key}": value for key, value in soft_metrics.to_dict().items()},
0899          "Soft_AvgTurnover": float(soft_view["turnover"].mean()),
0900      }
0901      if proposed is not None:
0902          proposed_view = _period_view(proposed, start, end)
0903          proposed_metrics = performance_summary(proposed_view["return"])
0904          record.update(
0905              {f"Proposed_{key}": value for key, value in proposed_metrics.to_dict().items()}
0906          )
0907          record["Proposed_AvgTurnover"] = float(proposed_view["turnover"].mean())
0908      return record
0909  
0910  
0911  def summarize_sjm(
0912      long: pd.DataFrame,
0913      loss_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0914      return_matrix: pd.DataFrame,
0915  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
0916      metadata = long.drop_duplicates("candidate").set_index("candidate")[[
0917          "jump_penalty",
0918          "keep_features",
0919          "sjm_weight",
0920          "is_deployed",
0921          "growth_switches",
0922          "inflation_switches",
0923      ]]
0924      summary = metadata.copy()
0925      metric_columns = (
0926          "mean_auc",
0927          "mean_brier",
0928          "quadrant_accuracy",
0929          "Soft_CAGR",
0930          "Soft_Sharpe",
0931          "Soft_MDD",
0932          "Soft_Calmar",
0933          "Soft_AvgTurnover",
0934          "Proposed_CAGR",
0935          "Proposed_Sharpe",
0936          "Proposed_MDD",
0937      )
0938      for period in ("calibration_2007_2017", "validation_2013_2017", "locked_2018_2026"):
0939          view = long.loc[long["period"] == period].set_index("candidate")
0940          prefix = {
0941              "calibration_2007_2017": "cal",
0942              "validation_2013_2017": "val",
0943              "locked_2018_2026": "locked",
0944          }[period]
0945          for column in metric_columns:
0946              if column in view:
0947                  summary[f"{prefix}_{column}"] = view[column]
0948  
0949      for prefix in ("cal", "locked"):
0950          summary[f"{prefix}_prediction_score"] = _rank_score(
0951              summary,
0952              [f"{prefix}_mean_auc", f"{prefix}_quadrant_accuracy"],
0953              [f"{prefix}_mean_brier"],
0954          )
0955          summary[f"{prefix}_soft_score"] = _rank_score(
0956              summary,
0957              [f"{prefix}_Soft_CAGR", f"{prefix}_Soft_Sharpe", f"{prefix}_Soft_MDD"],
0958              [],
0959          )
0960          summary[f"{prefix}_prediction_rank"] = summary[
0961              f"{prefix}_prediction_score"
0962          ].rank(ascending=False, method="min")
0963          summary[f"{prefix}_soft_rank"] = summary[f"{prefix}_soft_score"].rank(
0964              ascending=False, method="min"
0965          )
0966  
0967      deployed_id = "sjm_j3_k4_w0.1"
0968      summary = add_locked_bootstrap(summary, loss_matrix, return_matrix, deployed_id)
0969      cal_prediction_winner = str(summary["cal_prediction_score"].idxmax())
0970      cal_soft_winner = str(summary["cal_soft_score"].idxmax())
0971      deployed = summary.loc[deployed_id]
0972  
0973      cal = long.loc[long["period"] == "calibration_2007_2017"].set_index("candidate")
0974      val = long.loc[long["period"] == "validation_2013_2017"].set_index("candidate")
0975      deployed_cal = cal.loc[deployed_id]
0976      deployed_val = val.loc[deployed_id]
0977      strict = (
0978          (cal["mean_brier"] <= deployed_cal["mean_brier"])
0979          & (cal["quadrant_accuracy"] >= deployed_cal["quadrant_accuracy"])
0980          & (cal["Soft_Sharpe"] >= deployed_cal["Soft_Sharpe"])
0981          & (cal["Soft_MDD"] >= deployed_cal["Soft_MDD"])
0982          & (val["mean_brier"] <= deployed_val["mean_brier"])
0983          & (val["quadrant_accuracy"] >= deployed_val["quadrant_accuracy"])
0984          & (val["Soft_Sharpe"] >= deployed_val["Soft_Sharpe"])
0985          & (val["Soft_MDD"] >= deployed_val["Soft_MDD"])
0986      )
0987      strict_candidates = [str(candidate) for candidate in strict.index[strict]]
0988  
0989      report = {
0990          "candidate_count": int(len(summary)),
0991          "grid": {
0992              "jump_penalty": [0.0, 1.5, 3.0, 6.0],
0993              "keep_features": [2, 4, 6],
0994              "sjm_weight": [0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30],
0995              "no_sjm_candidate": True,
0996          },
0997          "deployed_candidate": deployed_id,
0998          "deployed_calibration_prediction_rank": int(deployed["cal_prediction_rank"]),
0999          "deployed_locked_prediction_rank": int(deployed["locked_prediction_rank"]),
1000          "deployed_calibration_soft_rank": int(deployed["cal_soft_rank"]),
1001          "deployed_locked_soft_rank": int(deployed["locked_soft_rank"]),
1002          "calibration_prediction_winner": cal_prediction_winner,
1003          "calibration_prediction_winner_locked_rank": int(
1004              summary.loc[cal_prediction_winner, "locked_prediction_rank"]
1005          ),
1006          "calibration_soft_winner": cal_soft_winner,
1007          "calibration_soft_winner_locked_rank": int(
1008              summary.loc[cal_soft_winner, "locked_soft_rank"]
1009          ),
1010          "prediction_rank_correlation_calibration_vs_locked": _rank_correlation(
1011              summary["cal_prediction_score"], summary["locked_prediction_score"]
1012          ),
1013          "soft_rank_correlation_calibration_vs_locked": _rank_correlation(
1014              summary["cal_soft_score"], summary["locked_soft_score"]
1015          ),
1016          "strict_both_prelock_windows_count": int(strict.sum()),
1017          "strict_both_prelock_windows_candidates": strict_candidates,
1018          "prelock_macro_brier_pbo": cscv_pbo(
1019              -loss_matrix.loc[loss_matrix.index <= CAL_END], kind="mean"
1020          ),
1021          "prelock_soft_sharpe_pbo": cscv_pbo(
1022              return_matrix.loc[return_matrix.index <= CAL_END], kind="sharpe"
1023          ),
1024          "proposed_one_axis_candidate_count": int(
1025              summary["cal_Proposed_Sharpe"].notna().sum()
1026          ),
1027      }
1028      return summary.sort_values("cal_prediction_rank"), report
1029  
1030  
1031  def run_sjm_robustness(
1032      returns: pd.DataFrame,
1033  ) -> tuple[pd.DataFrame, pd.DataFrame, dict[str, object]]:
1034      components, macro_targets = _macro_components()
1035      macro, _ = load_macro_data()
1036      aligned_target = aligned_macro_targets(components, macro_targets)
1037      internal = build_or_load_sjm_paths(components, macro)
1038  
1039      candidates: list[tuple[str, float, int, float]] = [("no_sjm", 3.0, 4, 0.0)]
1040      penalties, keeps = sjm_internal_grid()
1041      for penalty, keep, weight in itertools.product(
1042          penalties, keeps, (0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.30)
1043      ):
1044          candidates.append(
1045              (f"sjm_j{penalty:g}_k{keep}_w{weight:g}", penalty, keep, weight)
1046          )
1047  
1048      long_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
1049      valid_long_rows: list[dict[str, object]] = []
1050      invalid_candidates: list[dict[str, object]] = []
1051      losses: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
1052      soft_returns: dict[str, pd.Series] = {}
1053      deployed_reproduction: dict[str, float] = {}
1054      stored_signals = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "regime_signals.csv", index_col=0)
1055      stored_signals.index = pd.PeriodIndex(stored_signals.index, freq="M")
1056  
1057      for number, (candidate, penalty, keep, weight) in enumerate(candidates, start=1):
1058          print(f"SJM candidate {number:02d}/{len(candidates)} {candidate}", flush=True)
1059          selected = internal.loc[
1060              np.isclose(internal["jump_penalty"], penalty)
1061              & (internal["keep_features"] == keep)
1062          ].set_index("target_month")
1063          selected.index = pd.PeriodIndex(selected.index, freq="M")
1064          variant_components = components.copy()
1065          variant_components["growth_sjm"] = selected["growth_sjm"]
1066          variant_components["inflation_sjm"] = selected["inflation_sjm"]
1067          probabilities = _macro_probabilities(
1068              variant_components,
1069              d3_weight=0.20,
1070              sigmoid_scale=0.55,
1071              sjm_weight=weight,
1072              current_weight=0.85,
1073          )
1074          nonfinite = ~np.isfinite(probabilities.to_numpy(dtype=float))
1075          if nonfinite.any():
1076              first_bad_position = int(np.argwhere(nonfinite)[0, 0])
1077              first_bad_month = str(probabilities.index[first_bad_position])
1078              reason = (
1079                  "SparseJump2 produced a non-finite state probability; recursive "
1080                  f"macro smoothing became non-finite from {first_bad_month}."
1081              )
1082              invalid_candidates.append(
1083                  {
1084                      "candidate": candidate,
1085                      "jump_penalty": penalty,
1086                      "keep_features": keep,
1087                      "sjm_weight": weight,
1088                      "first_bad_month": first_bad_month,
1089                      "reason": reason,
1090                  }
1091              )
1092              for period, _, _ in PERIODS:
1093                  long_rows.append(
1094                      {
1095                          "candidate": candidate,
1096                          "period": period,
1097                          "jump_penalty": penalty,
1098                          "keep_features": keep,
1099                          "sjm_weight": weight,
1100                          "is_deployed": False,
1101                          "invalid_reason": reason,
1102                      }
1103                  )
1104              print(f"  invalid: {reason}", flush=True)
1105              continue
1106          signals = build_macro_signals(probabilities, components)
1107          soft = run_backtest(returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="soft")
1108          one_axis = (
1109              (math.isclose(penalty, 3.0) and keep == 4)
1110              or (
1111                  math.isclose(weight, 0.10)
1112                  and (math.isclose(penalty, 3.0) or keep == 4)
1113              )
1114          )
1115          proposed = (
1116              run_backtest(returns, signals, StrategyConfig(), mode="proposed")
1117              if one_axis
1118              else None
1119          )
1120          joined = probabilities.join(aligned_target, rsuffix="_target").dropna()
1121          losses[candidate] = (
1122              (joined["growth"] - joined["growth_target"]) ** 2
1123              + (joined["inflation"] - joined["inflation_target"]) ** 2
1124          ) / 2
1125          soft_returns[candidate] = soft["return"]
1126          switches = (
1127              float(selected["growth_switches"].iloc[-1]),
1128              float(selected["inflation_switches"].iloc[-1]),
1129          )
1130          for period, start, end in PERIODS:
1131              record = macro_period_record(
1132                      candidate,
1133                      penalty,
1134                      keep,
1135                      weight,
1136                      probabilities,
1137                      aligned_target,
1138                      soft,
1139                      proposed,
1140                      switches,
1141                      period,
1142                      start,
1143                      end,
1144                  )
1145              record["invalid_reason"] = ""
1146              long_rows.append(record)
1147              valid_long_rows.append(record)
1148          if candidate == "sjm_j3_k4_w0.1":
1149              common = probabilities.index.intersection(stored_signals.index)
1150              deployed_reproduction = {
1151                  "observations": int(len(common)),
1152                  "max_absolute_growth_probability_difference": float(
1153                      (
1154                          probabilities.loc[common, "growth"]
1155                          - stored_signals.loc[common, "p_growth_high"]
1156                      ).abs().max()
1157                  ),
1158                  "max_absolute_inflation_probability_difference": float(
1159                      (
1160                          probabilities.loc[common, "inflation"]
1161                          - stored_signals.loc[common, "p_inflation_high"]
1162                      ).abs().max()
1163                  ),
1164              }
1165  
1166      long = pd.DataFrame(long_rows)
1167      valid_long = pd.DataFrame(valid_long_rows)
1168      loss_matrix = pd.concat(losses, axis=1).sort_index()
1169      return_matrix = pd.concat(soft_returns, axis=1).sort_index()
1170      summary, report = summarize_sjm(valid_long, loss_matrix, return_matrix)
1171      if invalid_candidates:
1172          invalid_summary = pd.DataFrame(invalid_candidates).set_index("candidate")
1173          invalid_summary["is_deployed"] = False
1174          invalid_summary["invalid_reason"] = invalid_summary["reason"]
1175          summary["invalid_reason"] = ""
1176          summary = pd.concat([summary, invalid_summary], axis=0, sort=False)
1177      else:
1178          summary["invalid_reason"] = ""
1179      report["attempted_candidate_count"] = int(len(candidates))
1180      report["valid_candidate_count"] = int(len(candidates) - len(invalid_candidates))
1181      report["invalid_candidate_count"] = int(len(invalid_candidates))
1182      report["invalid_candidates"] = invalid_candidates
1183      report["deployed_reproduction"] = deployed_reproduction
1184      deployed = summary.loc["sjm_j3_k4_w0.1"]
1185      no_sjm = summary.loc["no_sjm"]
1186      report["no_sjm_vs_deployed"] = {
1187          "cal_mean_brier_delta": float(no_sjm["cal_mean_brier"] - deployed["cal_mean_brier"]),
1188          "locked_mean_brier_delta": float(
1189              no_sjm["locked_mean_brier"] - deployed["locked_mean_brier"]
1190          ),
1191          "locked_soft_sharpe_delta": float(
1192              no_sjm["locked_Soft_Sharpe"] - deployed["locked_Soft_Sharpe"]
1193          ),
1194          "locked_proposed_sharpe_delta": float(
1195              no_sjm["locked_Proposed_Sharpe"] - deployed["locked_Proposed_Sharpe"]
1196          ),
1197          "bootstrap_probability_no_sjm_brier_better": float(
1198              no_sjm["locked_boot_probability_brier_better_than_deployed"]
1199          ),
1200          "bootstrap_probability_no_sjm_soft_sharpe_better": float(
1201              no_sjm["locked_boot_probability_sharpe_better_than_deployed"]
1202          ),
1203      }
1204      assert deployed_reproduction["observations"] > 0
1205      assert deployed_reproduction["max_absolute_growth_probability_difference"] < 1e-12
1206      assert deployed_reproduction["max_absolute_inflation_probability_difference"] < 1e-12
1207      return long, summary, report
1208  
1209  
1210  def main() -> None:
1211      returns, _ = load_monthly_asset_returns(False)
1212      signals = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "regime_signals.csv", index_col=0)
1213      signals.index = pd.PeriodIndex(signals.index, freq="M")
1214      defensive = pd.read_csv(RESULTS / "proposed_backtest.csv", index_col=0)
1215      defensive.index = pd.PeriodIndex(defensive.index, freq="M")
1216  
1217      cached_logistic_report = (
1218          json.loads(LOGISTIC_REPORT_PATH.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
1219          if LOGISTIC_REPORT_PATH.exists()
1220          else {}
1221      )
1222      if (
1223          LOGISTIC_LONG_PATH.exists()
1224          and LOGISTIC_SUMMARY_PATH.exists()
1225          and cached_logistic_report.get("schema_version") == REPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION
1226      ):
1227          logistic_long = pd.read_csv(LOGISTIC_LONG_PATH)
1228          logistic_summary = pd.read_csv(LOGISTIC_SUMMARY_PATH).set_index("candidate")
1229          logistic_report = cached_logistic_report
1230          print("logistic robustness cache reused", logistic_long.shape, flush=True)
1231      else:
1232          logistic_long, logistic_summary, logistic_report = run_logistic_robustness(
1233              returns, signals, defensive
1234          )
1235          logistic_long.to_csv(LOGISTIC_LONG_PATH, index=False)
1236          logistic_summary.reset_index().to_csv(LOGISTIC_SUMMARY_PATH, index=False)
1237          logistic_report["schema_version"] = REPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION
1238          LOGISTIC_REPORT_PATH.write_text(
1239              json.dumps(logistic_report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
1240          )
1241  
1242      sjm_long, sjm_summary, sjm_report = run_sjm_robustness(returns)
1243      sjm_long.to_csv(SJM_LONG_PATH, index=False)
1244      sjm_summary.reset_index().to_csv(SJM_SUMMARY_PATH, index=False)
1245      SJM_REPORT_PATH.write_text(
1246          json.dumps(sjm_report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
1247      )
1248  
1249      report = {
1250          "schema_version": REPORT_SCHEMA_VERSION,
1251          "method": {
1252              "selection_boundary": "all candidate ranking and CSCV/PBO use data through 2017-12 only",
1253              "locked_boundary": "2018-01 onward is used only for rank stability and failure audits",
1254              "bootstrap": "paired six-month moving-block bootstrap, 2,000 simulations",
1255              "cscv": "8 contiguous blocks and all 4-vs-4 combinations, 70 splits",
1256              "scope": "logistic medium-horizon allocation and SJM macro/regime layers; the final daily VKOSPI overlay is held outside this hyperparameter audit",
1257          },
1258          "logistic": logistic_report,
1259          "sjm": sjm_report,
1260          "conclusion": "Hyperparameter sensitivity, rank reversal, and CSCV/PBO quantify selection risk; they cannot prove the absence of overfitting. Locked data was not used to retune the deployed strategy.",
1261      }
1262      REPORT_PATH.write_text(
1263          json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), encoding="utf-8"
1264      )
1265      print(json.dumps(report, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2), flush=True)
1266      print("saved", LOGISTIC_LONG_PATH.name, logistic_long.shape, flush=True)
1267      print("saved", LOGISTIC_SUMMARY_PATH.name, logistic_summary.shape, flush=True)
1268      print("saved", SJM_LONG_PATH.name, sjm_long.shape, flush=True)
1269      print("saved", SJM_SUMMARY_PATH.name, sjm_summary.shape, flush=True)
1270      print("saved", REPORT_PATH.name, flush=True)
1271  
1272  
1273  if __name__ == "__main__":
1274      main()

0009  import strategies.stage06_vkospi.vkospi_dynamic_risk_experiment as base
0010  import strategies.stage06_vkospi.vkospi_robust_dynamic_attribution as attribution
0011  import strategies.stage06_vkospi.vkospi_robust_dynamic_experiment as robust
0012  class VKOSPIRobustDynamicExperimentTest(unittest.TestCase):
0013      @classmethod
0014      def setUpClass(cls) -> None:
0015          cls.reference = base.load_reference_weights()
0016          cls.arrays = base.prepare_arrays(
0017              base.load_daily_open_levels(),
0018              cls.reference,

0022              robust.build_robust_daily_features(), cls.arrays

0030      def test_signals_precede_returns_and_stress_is_bounded(self) -> None:
0031          signal_dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(self.arrays["signal_dates"])
0032          return_dates = pd.DatetimeIndex(self.arrays["dates"])
0033          valid = signal_dates.notna()
0034          self.assertTrue((signal_dates[valid] < return_dates[valid]).all())
0035          stress = robust.stress_from_features(
0036              self.features, "acceleration", level_threshold=0.9, shock_threshold=1.0
0037          )
0038          self.assertTrue(np.isfinite(stress).all())
0039          self.assertGreaterEqual(float(stress.min()), 0.0)
0040          self.assertLessEqual(float(stress.max()), 1.0)
0041  
0042      def test_stress_override_rejects_wrong_shape(self) -> None:
0043          with self.assertRaisesRegex(ValueError, "stress_override must have shape"):
0044              base.simulate(self.arrays, None, stress_override=np.zeros(3))
0045  
0046      def test_selected_configuration_is_reproducible_and_prelocked(self) -> None:
0047          self.assertEqual(self.report["calibration_end"], "2017-12")

0049          self.assertEqual(self.report["candidate_count"], 810)

0052              self.report["winner"],
0053              {
0054                  "mode": "acceleration",
0055                  "level_threshold": 0.9,
0056                  "shock_threshold": 1.0,
0057                  "max_risk_transfer": 0.35,
0058                  "bond_share": 0.0,

0061              },

0074      def test_reconciliation_applies_overlay_relative_factor(self) -> None:
0075          baseline = pd.read_csv(
0076              robust.RESULTS / "vkospi_robust_dynamic_monthly.csv", index_col=0
0077          )
0078          baseline.index = pd.PeriodIndex(baseline.index, freq="M")
0079          _, neutral = base.simulate(self.arrays, None, keep_daily=False)
0080          reconciled = base.reconcile_to_monthly_reference(
0081              self.reference, neutral, baseline
0082          )
0083          common = reconciled.index
0084          expected = (
0085              (1 + self.reference.loc[common, "return"])
0086              * (1 + baseline.loc[common, "return"])
0087              / (1 + neutral.loc[common, "return"])
0088              - 1
0089          )
0090          np.testing.assert_allclose(reconciled["return"], expected, atol=1e-14, rtol=0)
0091

코드로 확인한 것과 아직 확인하지 못한 것

코드로 다시 확인할 수 있는 내용

데이터 시점 정렬, 후보 수 810개, 엄격 통과 2개, 선택 규칙, 비용 산식, 103개월 성과, 5,000회 부트스트랩은 코드와 결과 파일을 다시 계산해 확인할 수 있습니다.

아직 최적성까지 확인하지 못한 설정

로지스틱 C=0.1, OAP 합성 동일가중, 거시 10/90 혼합, SLSQP 목적함수 계수는 현재 결과만으로 최적성을 입증할 수 없습니다. 이 값들은 재현 가능한 고정 설계이지 통계적으로 유일한 답이 아닙니다.

설정값 근거는 14절, 팩터 선택은 15절 앞부분, 수식 설계는 15절 상수표, 실행 코드와 원본 주석은 16절에서 확인할 수 있습니다.
18 · CHALLENGE & RESPONSE

예상 반박 질문과 코드에 근거한 답변

아래 답변은 전략을 방어하기 위한 홍보문이 아니라, 어디까지 말할 수 있고 어디서 멈춰야 하는지를 정리한 질의응답입니다. 반박이 타당한 경우에는 그대로 인정합니다.

Q1. 결국 2018년 이후를 보고 무SJM으로 바꾼 것 아닌가?

맞습니다. 그래서 무SJM 경로를 신규 홀드아웃 승자라고 부르지 않습니다. 무SJM은 잠금 결과를 확인한 뒤 수행한 post-lock ablation이고, 2007–2017 및 2013–2017 동시 관문도 실패했습니다. 현재 저장소의 비교 기준일 뿐, 사전선정 근거가 가장 강한 경로는 SJM 10% 기준입니다.

Q2. 그렇다면 CAGR 15.64%, Sharpe 1.133, MDD -12.96%를 믿으면 안 되는가?

계산값 자체는 회귀 테스트와 재현 오차 검증을 통과했습니다. 다만 측정값의 재현성과 미래 일반화는 별개입니다. 이 숫자는 현재 자료에서의 사후 비교치이며, 2026-08 이후 새 데이터나 외부시장 사전등록 검증이 있어야 증거 수준이 올라갑니다.

Q3. 학습을 하지 않는다면서 로지스틱 회귀를 매달 쓰는 것은 모순 아닌가?

‘고정모형을 한 번도 적합하지 않는다’는 뜻이 아닙니다. 하이퍼파라미터와 입력정의는 고정하고, 매월 그 시점까지 확정된 과거만으로 로지스틱을 다시 적합하는 확장형 walk-forward입니다. 목표월 기준 두 달 embargo가 있어 아직 완성되지 않은 미래 라벨은 학습에 들어가지 않습니다.

Q4. 거시 국면확률은 실제 전이확률인가?

아닙니다. HMM의 상태전이행렬에서 나온 확률이 아닙니다. 현재 경로는 거시 z-score 합성을 sigmoid로 0~1에 매핑하고 전월 확률 15%를 섞은 상태 점수입니다. 네 국면확률은 성장·물가 두 확률의 곱으로 만든 결합확률입니다.

Q5. hard 국면이 바뀌면 포트폴리오도 한 번에 바뀌지 않는가?

hard 경로만 보면 그렇지만 최종 기준 비중에서 hard는 40%입니다. 나머지 60%는 네 국면확률 전체를 쓰는 SLSQP 경로이고, 이후 꼬리위험 이동·변동성 목표·거래비용·VKOSPI 20% 무거래 밴드가 적용됩니다. 실제 232개월 동안 hard 이름은 44번 바뀌었지만 전체 비중은 여러 완충층을 거칩니다.

Q6. 로지스틱의 raw 확률이 Brier 기준으로 나쁜데 왜 사용하는가?

타당한 지적입니다. class-balanced 로짓은 희귀사건 탐지에 초점을 두므로 절대확률 보정이 좋지 않을 수 있고, 사전 AUC도 낮았습니다. 그래서 raw 확률을 실제 발생확률로 쓰지 않고 과거 60개월 내 순위로 변환합니다. 그래도 사전 사건이 5개뿐이라는 약점은 사라지지 않으며, 확률보정 모델은 앞으로 별도 사전검증해야 합니다.

Q7. 잠금 AUC가 0.836이면 예측력이 증명된 것 아닌가?

아닙니다. 잠금 102개 관측과 8개 사건에서 나온 결과이고, 이미 여러 후속 연구를 거친 저장소입니다. 좋은 잠금 AUC는 긍정적 증거지만 다중시도와 작은 사건 수를 지우지 못합니다. 또한 예측 AUC가 좋아도 거래비용과 비중매핑 후 포트폴리오 성과가 자동으로 좋아지는 것은 아닙니다.

Q8. C=.1과 balanced L2는 왜 최적인가?

‘최적’이라고 말할 수 없습니다. 28개 후보 강건성 감사에서 이 후보만 두 사전 창의 포트폴리오 관문을 통과했고 사전·잠금 포트폴리오 순위가 모두 1위였지만, 사전 Sharpe 선택 CSCV/PBO는 95.7%로 높았습니다. 재현 가능한 고정값이지 유일한 통계적 해답은 아닙니다.

Q9. 복잡한 LightGBM이 로지스틱보다 낫지 않은가?

이 월간 표본은 실질 적합확률이 194개이고 사건 수도 적습니다. Stage 04에서 LightGBM을 비교했지만 선택된 위험이동은 max_shift=0이어서 승격되지 않았습니다. 복잡한 모델이 표현력은 높아도 이 표본에서는 분산과 선택편향이 더 큰 문제가 됐습니다.

Q10. 810개 VKOSPI 후보는 전형적인 데이터 마이닝 아닌가?

그 위험이 큽니다. 후보선정은 2017년 이전으로 제한했고 두 사전 창에서 CAGR·Sharpe·MDD 동시 개선을 요구했지만, 2013–2017은 2007–2017에 포함돼 독립 표본이 아닙니다. 엄격 통과가 2개뿐이어도 우연 가능성은 남습니다. 잠금 5,000회 블록 부트스트랩에서 세 지표 동시 개선 확률도 40.4%에 그쳤습니다.

Q11. Robust 오버레이의 개선 폭이 너무 작은 것 아닌가?

그렇습니다. 사전선정 SJM 10% 경로에서 Robust 오버레이 자체의 잠금 개선은 CAGR 약 +0.14%p, Sharpe +0.034, MDD 약 +0.16%p입니다. 이는 독립 알파 전략이 아니라 기존 포트폴리오의 월중 방어 실행을 조금 다듬은 결과입니다. 과장해서는 안 됩니다.

Q12. 그런데 현재 무SJM 경로의 잠금 개선은 왜 훨씬 큰가?

그 차이는 Robust VKOSPI 오버레이만의 효과가 아닙니다. SJM 제거로 거시 확률·hard 기준 포트폴리오·로지스틱 입력과 라벨이 함께 바뀝니다. 따라서 무SJM 대비 SJM 10% 차이를 ‘VKOSPI 알파’로 귀속하면 잘못입니다.

Q13. 왜 방어 이전분을 채권이 아니라 GLD에 전부 보내는가?

bond_share=0은 810개 사전 탐색의 승자 설정입니다. 위험자산 KODEX200·USO에서 줄인 비중을 GLD로 보냈을 때 두 사전 창의 관문을 통과했습니다. 다만 미래에도 금이 항상 주식·원유 스트레스의 헤지라는 보장은 없고, 금리·달러 국면에서는 실패할 수 있습니다.

Q14. 왜 VKOSPI가 높은 것만 보지 않고 충격과 가속을 섞는가?

높은 수준만 쓰면 위기가 이미 오래 진행된 때 계속 방어해 반등을 놓칠 수 있습니다. 5일 shock은 당시 변동성 대비 이례성을, acceleration은 최근 5일 공포상승이 직전 5일보다 빨라졌는지를 봅니다. 가속은 독립 알파보다는 방어 진입 확인항입니다.

Q15. 20% 무거래 밴드가 왜 중요한가?

같은 robust 신호를 기존 15% 정책에 넣었을 때 잠금 CAGR과 Sharpe가 오히려 낮아졌습니다. 최대 이전을 35%로 올리는 것만으로도 부족했고, 20% 밴드까지 적용했을 때 세 지표가 함께 개선됐습니다. 신호보다 거래로 번역하는 빈도가 성과를 갈랐다는 뜻입니다.

Q16. 비용 가정이 너무 낙관적인 것 아닌가?

매매 15bp, 달러자산 순비중 변화 환전 5bp, 차입 연 4%를 반영했습니다. 비용 2배에서도 Robust 오버레이의 상대 우위는 남았지만 세금, 시장충격, 상품 롤비용, 실시간 슬리피지, 대규모 운용 제약은 포함하지 않았습니다. 실거래 기대수익은 백테스트보다 낮게 보는 것이 맞습니다.

Q17. 레버리지 1.5배는 MDD를 과소평가하지 않는가?

레버리지는 24개월 가중 변동성으로 15% 목표를 맞추며 0.5–1.5로 제한되고 연 4% 금융비용을 냅니다. 그래도 월말 MDD는 일중 마진콜과 갭 위험을 잡지 못합니다. 따라서 −12.96%를 실거래 최대손실의 상한으로 해석하면 안 됩니다.

Q18. 매크로 데이터 개정과 발표시차 누수는 없는가?

코드는 GDP·수출·BSI를 한 달, 물가자료를 두 월말 밀고 목표월 전 신호만 사용합니다. 그러나 현재 파일은 당시 공개본을 보존한 point-in-time vintage 데이터베이스가 아닙니다. 발표시차는 모사했지만 사후 개정값 위험까지 제거했다고 말할 수 없습니다.

Q19. VKOSPI 신호에 당일 종가를 쓰고 당일 수익을 먹는 누수는 없는가?

배열 단언과 테스트에서 signal_date < return_date를 확인합니다. t일 종가로 만든 신호는 t+1일 오픈부터 적용됩니다. 월별 가격 특징도 목표월 시작 이틀 전에서 끊어 미국장 종가 시차를 피합니다.

Q20. 2005년부터 결과를 왜 보여주지 않는가?

네 자산의 공통 월수익은 USO와 채권자료 때문에 2006-04부터 가능하고 거시 국면은 24개월 워밍업이 필요합니다. 같은 전략의 첫 거래월은 2007-04입니다. 2005년을 현금이나 다른 지수로 채우면 별도 프록시 전략이므로 이어 붙이지 않았습니다.

Q21. VIX6 decomposition과 옵션은 왜 최종 비중에 없는가?

VIX6와 KOSPI200 옵션을 신호·자산으로 비교했지만 사전 강건성 및 슬리피지 관문을 통과한 옵션 후보가 없었습니다. 그래서 현재 옵션 비중은 0%입니다. ‘아이디어가 틀렸다’가 아니라 현재 데이터·비용 가정에서 승격 증거가 부족했다는 결론입니다.

Q22. 이 전략을 실전 배포해도 되는가?

연구 프로토타입으로는 재현 가능하지만 즉시 실전 배포를 정당화하지는 않습니다. 필요한 다음 단계는 point-in-time 거시자료, 2026-08 이후 사전등록 검증, 주문·세금·롤비용을 포함한 페이퍼 트레이딩, 포지션·손실 한도, 데이터 결측 시 fallback, 모델 드리프트 경보입니다.

답변 원칙: 계산된 성과는 숨기지 않되, 사전선정과 사후분석을 구분하고, raw 확률을 보정확률로 과장하지 않으며, 작은 표본·다중탐색·데이터 개정·실거래 비용을 한계로 먼저 인정합니다. 이 원칙을 지켜야 보고서 수치가 반박 질문을 견딜 수 있습니다.
